The teaching's hearth (thab) is the empty,

<a id="u02247"></a>

emptiness itself, and the terminator. [N-099](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-099)

<a id="u02248"></a>

The remaining times of individual persons

<a id="u02249"></a>

are the same as what was taught above.

<a id="u02250"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-fifth reply.]**

<a id="u02251"></a>

Ema! Lord of gods, sovereign,

<a id="u02252"></a>

faithful one of great fortune, listen.

<a id="u02253"></a>

Hidden in other continua,

<a id="u02254"></a>

known by no one,

<a id="u02255"></a>

the rite of conferring empowerment will be explained.

<a id="u02256"></a>

Outer, inner, secret,

<a id="u02257"></a>

and the fully complete secret:

<a id="u02258"></a>

through these four combinations,

<a id="u02259"></a>

the four—beings' bodies, speech, mental faculty, and ordinary mind—

<a id="u02260"></a>

in order to purify [them],

<a id="u02261"></a>

through four empowerments, too,

<a id="u02262"></a>

bring the fortunate to maturity. [N-100](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100)

<a id="u02263"></a>

With elaborations, without elaborations,

<a id="u02264"></a>

utterly without elaborations,

<a id="u02265"></a>

and, likewise, completely without elaborations:

<a id="u02266"></a>

through these four distinctions,

<a id="u02267"></a>

the faithful mature their own continua.

<a id="u02268"></a>

For the purpose of [empowerment] with elaborations, too,

<a id="u02269"></a>

approach, accomplishment, and close approach

<a id="u02270"></a>

are to be undertaken by a guru whose characteristics are complete.

<a id="u02271"></a>

Furthermore, with the earth rite,

<a id="u02272"></a>

preparations, lines, and spreading colors,

<a id="u02273"></a>

construct the maṇḍala that occurs in the continuum.

<a id="u02274"></a>

Then, for the rite of conferring empowerment, too,

<a id="u02275"></a>

beginning with the hearer vehicle,

<a id="u02276"></a>

complete the eight stages,

<a id="u02277"></a>

then confer the Great Perfection empowerment of awareness's expressiveness. [N-100](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100)

<a id="u02278"></a>

Explain their respective enlightened intents,

<a id="u02279"></a>

entry, and distinctive capacities.

<a id="u02280"></a>

To mature the faithful who enter without elaborations,

<a id="u02281"></a>

for the supreme empowerment without elaborations,

<a id="u02282"></a>

relying on a maṇḍala and vase,

<a id="u02283"></a>

give empowerment in verses.

<a id="u02284"></a>

For [empowerment] utterly without elaborations,

<a id="u02285"></a>

distinguish samsara and nirvana through the secret preliminary, as below. [N-100](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100)

<a id="u02286"></a>

Then, through the characteristics of space,

<a id="u02287"></a>

abandon the obscurations of body, speech, and mental faculty.

<a id="u02288"></a>

Definitely examine [this] through its characteristics.

<a id="u02289"></a>

When activity is impure,

<a id="u02290"></a>

purify [it] through virtue appropriate to each individual.

<a id="u02291"></a>

For empowerment, relying on signs,

<a id="u02292"></a>

introduce the own identity of clear self-awareness. [N-T46](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t46)

<a id="u02293"></a>

The skilled person should give pith instructions that explain the signs.

<a id="u02294"></a>

For [empowerment] completely without elaborations,

<a id="u02295"></a>

open the gate of the maṇḍala of ordinary mind,

<a id="u02296"></a>

and definitely undertake the bodily posture.

<a id="u02297"></a>

Becoming familiar with it, apply the gaze.

<a id="u02298"></a>

Directly perceived awareness is the sign of authoritative transmission. [N-100](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100)

<a id="u02299"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-sixth reply.]**

<a id="u02300"></a>

Hero Free from Conceptualization, son of the gods,

<a id="u02301"></a>

to satisfy the sovereign Delight-Maker,

<a id="u02302"></a>

the pledges depending on empowerment will be explained.

<a id="u02303"></a>

Though words do not suffice for their particulars,

<a id="u02304"></a>

in brief, relate embodiment, speech, and awakened mind

<a id="u02305"></a>

to the yogin's body, speech, and mental faculty.

<a id="u02306"></a>

The objects of focus—the guru and vajra siblings—

<a id="u02307"></a>

are distinguished as the objects to be guarded.

<a id="u02308"></a>

Vows are included in body and speech. [N-101](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101)

<a id="a2000-c01-s05"></a>

*Scan-restored main verses:*[^G-A2000-C01-S05]

The site of the vajra secret is to be held.

<a id="a2000-c01-s06"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-seventh reply.]**[^G-A2000-C01-S06]

<a id="a2000-c01-s07"></a>

*Scan-restored main verses:*[^G-A2000-C01-S07]

Again, Lord of Gods, Joy-Maker, listen.

<a id="u02309"></a>

Through verbal expressions and the categories of tenet systems,

<a id="u02310"></a>

the nine stages involve the activity of the mental faculty.

<a id="u02311"></a>

Separated from the crucial point of direct perception,

<a id="u02312"></a>

future sentient beings to be trained

<a id="u02313"></a>

will place their trust in words.

<a id="u02314"></a>

Sentient beings with coarse conceptual thought,

<a id="u02315"></a>

because they engage in words, are not mature.

<a id="u02316"></a>

At the final time of this teaching,

<a id="u02317"></a>

this is called the ground of aspirational activity. [N-101](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101)

<a id="u02318"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-eighth reply.]**

<a id="u02319"></a>

Beyond words, elaborations are greatly complete. [N-102](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-102)

<a id="u02320"></a>

This continuum of definite secrecy,

<a id="u02321"></a>

relying on the path of direct perception,

<a id="u02322"></a>

gathers one's own knowing into awareness.

<a id="u02323"></a>

Gathering all buddhas,

<a id="u02324"></a>

the definite nature of phenomena of self-awareness

<a id="u02325"></a>

is enacted through evenness without mixing. [N-102](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-102)

<a id="u02326"></a>

This is the stage beyond words.

<a id="u02327"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-ninth reply.]**

<a id="u02328"></a>

The crucial point of direct perception: body, speech, and ordinary mind.

<a id="u02329"></a>

Definitely bring their respective crucial points into accord.

<a id="u02330"></a>

Furthermore, the body [abides] in the three embodiments;

<a id="u02331"></a>

one should never depart from them. [N-103](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)

<a id="u02332"></a>

For speech, training and abiding, [N-103](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)[^G-U02332]

<a id="u02333"></a>

and likewise passing beyond, are to be taught.

<a id="u02334"></a>

Ordinary mind is accomplished by directing it toward basic space.

<a id="u02335"></a>

Do not separate from the pith instructions on the three crucial points.

<a id="u02336"></a>

Furthermore, rely on the crucial points of the door, object,

<a id="u02337"></a>

wind, and awareness.

<a id="u02338"></a>

For the door, view the three embodiments with the eyes.

<a id="u02339"></a>

For the object, be free of what obscures.

<a id="u02340"></a>

Through wind and awareness being slow, primordial knowing is accomplished.

<a id="u02341"></a>

In this there are four visions.

<a id="u02342"></a>

Through the vision of the direct perception of the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u02343"></a>

one transcends words held through mental examination. [N-103](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)

<a id="u02344"></a>

Through the vision of increasing experience,

<a id="u02345"></a>

delusive appearances are made to subside.

<a id="u02346"></a>

Through the vision of awareness reaching its limit,

<a id="u02347"></a>

one transcends the path appearances of realizing the three embodiments.

<a id="u02348"></a>

Through the vision of the exhaustion of nature of phenomena,

<a id="u02349"></a>

one cuts the flow of samsara of the three realms.

<a id="u02350"></a>

Rely on this to the end and have conviction. [N-103](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)

<a id="u02351"></a>

With a Ground in the three nonmovements,

<a id="u02352"></a>

one arrives at the crucial point of wind-mind.

<a id="u02353"></a>

Taking the measure through three ways of remaining,

<a id="u02354"></a>

dreams are reversed; for body, speech, and mental faculty,

<a id="u02355"></a>

signs and measures are definitely grasped.

<a id="u02356"></a>

Fixing [this] with the three attainments,

<a id="u02357"></a>

contaminated aggregates do not appear. [N-103](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)

<a id="u02358"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixtieth reply.]**

<a id="u02359"></a>

Ema! How greatly wondrous!

<a id="u02360"></a>

Unless samsara and nirvana are distinguished through the secret preliminary,

<a id="u02361"></a>

the connection of the three realms with body, speech,

<a id="u02362"></a>

and mental faculty will not be cut.

<a id="u02363"></a>

Therefore, I explain distinguishing samsara and nirvana through the secret preliminary.

<a id="u02364"></a>

This has three aspects. [N-104](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

<a id="u02365"></a>

For the body: walking and remaining,

<a id="u02366"></a>

compressing [the body] and manifold bodily mechanisms,

<a id="u02367"></a>

extending and gathering the limbs,

<a id="u02368"></a>

and relying on manifold actions.

<a id="u02369"></a>

Rousing [these] with a mindful ordinary mind,

<a id="u02370"></a>

the activities of the forms of the six destinies—

<a id="u02371"></a>

take [them] up in ordinary mind and perform [them] with the body.

<a id="u02372"></a>

Whoever can engage in this way—

<a id="u02373"></a>

whatever is undertaken, its flow is later cut. [N-104](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

<a id="u02374"></a>

This has two purposes,

<a id="u02375"></a>

examined as common and supreme.

<a id="u02376"></a>

When signs and measures are reached in this way,

<a id="u02377"></a>

the yogin begins verbal activity.

<a id="u02378"></a>

Because expression has the intrinsic nature of conceptual thought,

<a id="u02379"></a>

[use] manifold sounds and voices.

<a id="u02380"></a>

Because whatever is spoken has no reversal, [N-104](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

<a id="u02381"></a>

[use] the voices of gods, nāgas, and yakṣas,

<a id="u02382"></a>

gandharvas, kumbhāṇḍas, and the All-Pervader;

<a id="u02383"></a>

utter their favorable and unfavorable varieties.

<a id="u02384"></a>

In brief, the voices of the six destinies:

<a id="u02385"></a>

utter ordinary mind's mindfulness and reflection through speech. [N-104](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

<a id="u02386"></a>

Here, too, there are two purposes:

<a id="u02387"></a>

the distinction between supreme and common culminations.

<a id="u02388"></a>

Having reached the measure in this way, too,

<a id="u02389"></a>

the yogin should train ordinary mind.

<a id="u02390"></a>

For past, future, and present,

<a id="u02391"></a>

rely on the wheel of mindfulness and reflection, proliferating and gathering.

<a id="u02392"></a>

Ordinary minds of liking and disliking, favorable and unfavorable—

<a id="u02393"></a>

all manifold [notions] of existence and nonexistence—

<a id="u02394"></a>

examine outwardly and reflect inwardly.

<a id="u02395"></a>

The mindfulness of whatever is reflected on—

<a id="u02396"></a>

afterward, its flow of arising will definitely be cut. [N-104](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

<a id="u02397"></a>

Its purposes are distinguished by what was said above.

<a id="u02398"></a>

When activity has been completed in this way,

<a id="u02399"></a>

continually apply [it] to the crucial point of direct perception.

<a id="u02400"></a>

When that is complete, one does not enter the three realms.

<a id="u02401"></a>

It should be taught in utmost secrecy.

<a id="u02402"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-first reply.]**

<a id="u02403"></a>

Relying on bodily activity,

<a id="u02404"></a>

one wishing to retain the inner wind:

<a id="u02405"></a>

the twenty-one activities of retaining wind— [N-105](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105)

<a id="u02406"></a>

when the yogin performs and engages in these,

<a id="u02407"></a>

one becomes familiar with wind and proficient in the body.

<a id="u02408"></a>

For one endowed with ornaments,

<a id="u02409"></a>

make a wheel of dependent connections at the place.

<a id="u02410"></a>

Direct wind and knowing toward the ornaments.

<a id="u02411"></a>

While moving, apply the manner of gazing with the eyes.

<a id="u02412"></a>

While remaining, project ordinary mind. [N-105](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105)

<a id="u02413"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-second reply.]**

<a id="u02414"></a>

Through twenty-one trainings in retaining ordinary mind,

<a id="u02415"></a>

first, bring it to abide;

<a id="u02416"></a>

second, separate body and ordinary mind;

<a id="u02417"></a>

third, see the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u02418"></a>

At this time, the crucial points of body, speech, and ordinary mind

<a id="u02419"></a>

are to be allowed to settle by one endowed with yoga.

<a id="u02420"></a>

Also with the signs of the wind that causes movement,

<a id="u02421"></a>

mixing, projecting, and gathering—

<a id="u02422"></a>

when the skilled person relies on these crucial points,

<a id="u02423"></a>

the flow of ordinary mind's conceptual thought is cut. [N-105](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105)

<a id="u02424"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-third reply.]**

<a id="u02425"></a>

There are twenty-one signs of death, too.

<a id="u02426"></a>

Having gone to a secluded charnel-ground place,

<a id="u02427"></a>

offer to the guru and make a torma.

<a id="u02428"></a>

Furthermore, with food and drink,

<a id="u02429"></a>

satisfy other beings as well.

<a id="u02430"></a>

Then examine the outer, inner, and secret

<a id="u02431"></a>

signs of death, too.

<a id="u02432"></a>

When they are unfavorable, undertake reversal;

<a id="u02433"></a>

when favorable, make supplication to the guru. [N-105](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105)

<a id="u02434"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-fourth reply.]**

<a id="u02435"></a>

Lord of the gods, for the sake of sentient beings,

<a id="u02436"></a>

the answer to that compassionate question you asked—

<a id="u02437"></a>

I teach it; grasp it without delusion.

<a id="u02438"></a>

In the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature,

<a id="u02439"></a>

although the conceptual mind of a view with a directed object of focus is exhausted,

<a id="u02440"></a>

for now, establish certainty through words. [N-106](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106)

<a id="u02441"></a>

Through the crucial point of directly perceiving the nature of phenomena, too,

<a id="u02442"></a>

one realizes that activities have neither favorable nor unfavorable [status].

<a id="u02443"></a>

Through definitely gathering the heart-sphere,

<a id="u02444"></a>

faculties are neither sharp nor dull.

<a id="u02445"></a>

Because the limit of the words of mental examination is exhausted,

<a id="u02446"></a>

one sees through the faculty of the eye of discerning knowing.

<a id="u02447"></a>

Because words do not reveal the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u02448"></a>

the common vehicles do not accomplish the meaning.

<a id="u02449"></a>

Because the three embodiments are path appearances,

<a id="u02450"></a>

seeing this, one does not return to the three realms. [N-106](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106)

<a id="u02451"></a>

Because all phenomena are equally of one taste,

<a id="u02452"></a>

there is no dependence on Ground and result.

<a id="u02453"></a>

Because self-awareness is free from all conceptual thought,

<a id="u02454"></a>

there are four ways of liberation.

<a id="u02455"></a>

Through confidence in liberation at the four crucial points,

<a id="u02456"></a>

samsara and passing beyond sorrow do not abide.

<a id="u02457"></a>

Phenomena that have not arisen and are not made

<a id="u02458"></a>

have neither Ground nor root.

<a id="u02459"></a>

The crucial points of liberation through hearing and seeing

<a id="u02460"></a>

are realized through two, five, and eight. [N-106](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106)

<a id="u02461"></a>

Through four great confidences, one joins basic space.

<a id="u02462"></a>

Through three instances of pointing out, one should gain conviction.

<a id="u02463"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-fifth reply.]**

<a id="u02464"></a>

Next, the life-tree of cultivation is explained.

<a id="u02465"></a>

Definitely relate [this] through the authoritative transmission of ordinary mind.

<a id="u02466"></a>

Furthermore, concerning the crucial points of cultivation,

<a id="u02467"></a>

preliminary practice, main practice,

<a id="u02468"></a>

and subsequent practice are held to be three.

<a id="u02469"></a>

Training the body, taming the nature of ordinary mind,

<a id="u02470"></a>

and completing the activities of speech,

<a id="u02471"></a>

the nature of ordinary mind enters the path.

<a id="u02472"></a>

Relying on wind, body and ordinary mind separate.

<a id="u02473"></a>

In order to cut the opening from which the three realms proliferate,

<a id="u02474"></a>

reverse attachment to body and speech from its depths.

<a id="u02475"></a>

Because the precipices of the six destinies are equal,

<a id="u02476"></a>

press the crucial points of body, speech, and mental faculty. [N-107](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107)

<a id="u02477"></a>

In order to mix the nature of ordinary mind with phenomena,

<a id="u02478"></a>

draw wind and knowing upward.

<a id="u02479"></a>

In order to exhaust delusive appearances,

<a id="u02480"></a>

continually become familiar with wind. [N-107](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107)

<a id="u02481"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-sixth reply.]**

<a id="u02482"></a>

For the application of activity, in basic space and awareness,

<a id="u02483"></a>

endowed with three fixings, without separation,

<a id="u02484"></a>

continual familiarity makes activity easeful.[^G-U02484]

<a id="u02485"></a>

When all phenomena are free from doing,

<a id="u02486"></a>

and the connection of samsara and nirvana is cut,

<a id="u02487"></a>

delusion with conceptual thought is stopped.

<a id="u02488"></a>

By cutting the flow of samsara's precipice,

<a id="u02489"></a>

of the great yogin's activity—[N-108](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108)[^G-U02489]

<a id="scan-ch1-layer-02489"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-seventh reply.]**[^G-SCAN-CH1-LAYER-02489]

<a id="u02490"></a>

although the result cannot be expressed,

<a id="u02491"></a>

one reaches the exhaustion of phenomena,

<a id="u02492"></a>

and the limits of one's own tenet system, too, collapse.

<a id="u02493"></a>

At this time, the guru's pith instructions subside.

<a id="u02494"></a>

Because the limits of viewing, cultivation, and activity are empty,

<a id="u02495"></a>

there is no appearance as phenomena.

<a id="u02496"></a>

Because the flow of embodiments and primordial knowing is cut,

<a id="u02497"></a>

there are neither buddhas nor sentient beings.

<a id="u02498"></a>

In brief, nothing whatsoever abides.

<a id="u02499"></a>

Because there is no going, there is no coming. [N-108](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108)

<a id="u02500"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-eighth reply.]**

<a id="u02501"></a>

One wishing to cut the flow of samsara,

<a id="u02502"></a>

having reversed attachment to body and speech,

<a id="u02503"></a>

should press the crucial point of direct perception.

<a id="u02504"></a>

Through the application of calculating delusion,

<a id="u02505"></a>

grasp the measures of the times of samsara and nirvana,

<a id="u02506"></a>

and become skilled in the crucial point of reversal.

<a id="u02507"></a>

By reaching the measure of the six capabilities,

<a id="u02508"></a>

one is thoroughly liberated from the six faults.

<a id="u02509"></a>

This cuts the flow of samsara. [N-109](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-109)

<a id="u02510"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-ninth reply.]**

<a id="u02511"></a>

Taking nourishment from vital essences is like this:

<a id="u02512"></a>

not taught in any other continuum,

<a id="u02513"></a>

Ema! It is explained as greatly wondrous.

<a id="u02514"></a>

Lord of gods, hero, listen!

<a id="u02515"></a>

By relying on precious substances,

<a id="u02516"></a>

the lifespan becomes equal to the sun and moon.

<a id="u02517"></a>

Through preparations of various stones,

<a id="u02518"></a>

weapons do not penetrate the body.

<a id="u02519"></a>

Through preparations relying on herbs,

<a id="u02520"></a>

white hair and wrinkles disappear. [N-110](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110)

<a id="u02521"></a>

Through taking nourishment from vital essences of medicinal preparations,

<a id="u02522"></a>

one becomes endowed with splendor and youthful.[^G-U02522]

<a id="u02523"></a>

Through preparations relying on meat,

<a id="u02524"></a>

the bodily constituents are sustained, and strength develops.

<a id="u02525"></a>

Furthermore, relying on wind,

<a id="u02526"></a>

greatly wondrous taking of nourishment from vital essences is explained.

<a id="u02527"></a>

What is called cutting the flow of the elements

<a id="u02528"></a>

is known through the upper and lower winds.

<a id="u02529"></a>

For the skilled person, wind is easily made to abide.

<a id="u02530"></a>

Next, through calculating wind's movement,

<a id="u02531"></a>

there is the yoga of food.

<a id="u02532"></a>

Relying on the elements,

<a id="u02533"></a>

whatever appears becomes food.

<a id="u02534"></a>

Through taking nourishment from vital essences in this way,

<a id="u02535"></a>

the torments of beings are cut off. [N-110](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110)

<a id="u02536"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventieth reply.]**

<a id="u02537"></a>

The yoga of clothing is like this:

<a id="u02538"></a>

perform it through wind and profound dependent connections.

<a id="u02539"></a>

From projecting and gathering wind,

<a id="u02540"></a>

through the crucial point of going, coming, and holding, too,

<a id="u02541"></a>

distinguish the respective elemental objects of focus

<a id="u02542"></a>

in accord with the yogin's body.

<a id="u02543"></a>

Through the application of dependent connections,

<a id="u02544"></a>

with two, six, seven, and three,

<a id="u02545"></a>

properly combine pills and powders,

<a id="u02546"></a>

and carry out the culmination of ingesting and applying [them]. [N-111](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-111)

<a id="u02547"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventy-first reply.]**

<a id="u02548"></a>

Next, for cutting the flow of the body's elements,

<a id="u02549"></a>

greatly wondrous taking of nourishment from vital essences is explained.

<a id="u02550"></a>

By continually circulating and reversing ‘great odor’ (dri chen), [N-112](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112)

<a id="u02551"></a>

finally, the body's discharges are exhausted.

<a id="u02552"></a>

Alternatively, extracted vital essence and liquid butter—

<a id="u02553"></a>

by relying on [these] in the proper measure,

<a id="u02554"></a>

the flows of the elements are cut.

<a id="u02555"></a>

Because the flow of the body's elements is cut,

<a id="u02556"></a>

living creatures do not abide in the body;

<a id="u02557"></a>

there are no feces, urine, and so forth;

<a id="u02558"></a>

outer and inner wind, too, cease to be. [N-112](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112)

<a id="u02559"></a>

At this time, the great yogin

<a id="u02560"></a>

becomes pure with respect to both food and clothing.

<a id="u02561"></a>

Through this, concerning lifespan and enjoyments,

<a id="u02562"></a>

whatever is desired is accomplished. [N-112](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112)

<a id="u02563"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventy-second reply.]**

<a id="u02564"></a>

For the four elements,

<a id="u02565"></a>

a yogin who makes their tastes equal

<a id="u02566"></a>

brings the crucial points of earth, water, fire, and wind into accord.

<a id="u02567"></a>

Mixing [them] with the body, raise [them] through wind.

<a id="u02568"></a>

Transform one's own body through the respective elements,

<a id="u02569"></a>

and, in engaging in this itself,

<a id="u02570"></a>

cultivate the maṇḍala gathering the crucial points. [N-113](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113)

<a id="u02571"></a>

Through reaching the measure of familiarity with this,

<a id="u02572"></a>

the powers of the four become equal in their own identity.

<a id="u02573"></a>

One engages easefully and is free from destruction.

<a id="u02574"></a>

Through this, too, both food and clothing are accomplished,

<a id="u02575"></a>

and lifespan and enjoyments are complete.

<a id="u02576"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventy-third reply.]**

<a id="u02577"></a>

The qualities of the treasury are like this:

<a id="u02578"></a>

with common and supreme accomplishments,

<a id="u02579"></a>

one plays as one pleases, accomplishing [them] easefully.

<a id="u02580"></a>

The branches of dependent connections are complete.

<a id="u02581"></a>

Miracles and higher knowing, too,

<a id="u02582"></a>

are definitely accomplished through engaging with the treasury.

<a id="u02583"></a>

Illnesses and measures of time become known. [N-113](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113)

<a id="u02584"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventy-fourth reply.]**

<a id="u02585"></a>

One wishing to accomplish noble miracles,

<a id="u02586"></a>

with a support that is an entity or with what is not an entity,

<a id="u02587"></a>

definitely accomplishes [them] through training in wind.

<a id="u02588"></a>

Word, craft, and dependent connections;

<a id="u02589"></a>

signs, phrases, and conventions;

<a id="u02590"></a>

examples, actions, and methods of training—

<a id="u02591"></a>

relying on these and engaging in karmic wind,

<a id="u02592"></a>

one accomplishes whatever miracles are desired.

<a id="u02593"></a>

Through cultivating primordial knowing free from conceptualization,

<a id="u02594"></a>

reflecting on emptiness, and the crucial point, too,

<a id="u02595"></a>

without endeavor or effort, miracles

<a id="u02596"></a>

will definitely be accomplished. [N-114](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114)

<a id="u02597"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventy-fifth reply.]**[^G-U02597]

<a id="u02598"></a>

One wishing to enjoy inexhaustible treasure

<a id="u02599"></a>

relies on the mechanism of the market (khrom).

<a id="u02600"></a>

Become skilled in calculating its application. [N-114](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114)

<a id="u02601"></a>

Endowed with times of three, four, and two,

<a id="u02602"></a>

having concealed [it] for ten months and thoroughly dried [it],

<a id="u02603"></a>

begin directing [it] toward whichever direction is desired.

<a id="u02604"></a>

By joining [it] with rays and wind,

<a id="u02605"></a>

whatever activities one wishes to undertake are accomplished. [N-114](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114)

<a id="u02606"></a>

Alternatively, through profound practice of accomplishment,

<a id="u02607"></a>

distinguishing approach, accomplishment, and the culmination of activities,

<a id="u02608"></a>

rely on the lord of wealth.

<a id="u02609"></a>

Through familiarity with mantra, enjoy [the treasure].

<a id="u02610"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventy-sixth reply.]**

<a id="u02611"></a>

By bringing four and five under one's power,

<a id="u02612"></a>

the master of ordinary mind holds the body.

<a id="u02613"></a>

Within the wheels of the three places, too,

<a id="u02614"></a>

with four and five syllables, and the assignment of forms, [N-115](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

<a id="u02615"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U02615.*[^G-U02615]

<a id="u02616"></a>

perform proliferating, gathering, absorbing, and coiling.

<a id="u02617"></a>

Through completing this, there is flight,

<a id="u02618"></a>

entry without separation, and no burning;

<a id="u02619"></a>

one cannot sink and can run. [N-115](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

<a id="u02620"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U02620.*[^G-U02620]

<a id="u02621"></a>

The teacher, the buddha, the Blessed One,

<a id="u02622"></a>

all his qualities complete in themselves,

<a id="u02623"></a>

without utterance or spoken discourse,

<a id="u02624"></a>

from the play of naturally arisen primordial knowing,

<a id="u02625"></a>

as a miraculous display of words, phrases, and names,

<a id="u02626"></a>

appears to those of good fortune.

<a id="u02627"></a>

Thus, the secret phrases of the Great Perfection—

<a id="u02628"></a>

the lord of the gods, having definitely heard them,

<a id="u02629"></a>

with respectful body and palms properly joined,

<a id="u02630"></a>

rejoiced in the teacher himself. [N-115](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

<a id="u02631"></a>

From the Great Perfection of everything,

<a id="u02632"></a>

intrinsic nature became free from elaborations,

<a id="u02633"></a>

inseparable from the basic space of the nature of phenomena. [N-115](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

<a id="u02634"></a>

Thus, from The Great All-Penetrating Word, the Root of All Phenomena,

<a id="u02635"></a>

this is the first chapter: ‘Array of Diversity: The Definite Arising of the Root of Naturally Arisen Pith Instructions.’ [N-115](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)[^G-U02635]

<a id="a2000-c01-s09"></a>

*[Unresolved source inscription at the Chapter 1/2 boundary; no transcription supplied.]*[^G-A2000-C01-S09]

<a id="part-2"></a>
## Chapter 2

<a id="u02636"></a>

Then the lord of the gods, free from conceptual thought,

<a id="u02637"></a>

though inseparable from the basic space of phenomena,

<a id="u02638"></a>

for the sentient beings of future times,

<a id="u02639"></a>

through realization, definitely from samsara—

<a id="u02640"></a>

with the conceptual mind [intent on] liberating them,

<a id="u02641"></a>

first arousing ordinary mind,

<a id="u02642"></a>

addressed the teacher himself in these words: [N-117](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117)

<a id="u02643"></a>

‘Kyé, kyé! Miraculously born embodiment!

<a id="u02644"></a>

For sentient beings, from the samsara of the three realms,

<a id="u02645"></a>

what is the crucial point that brings liberation?

<a id="u02646"></a>

Blessed One, I make this request.

<a id="u02647"></a>

What is the beginning of sentient beings' samsara?

<a id="u02648"></a>

By what is the final extent of passing beyond [sorrow] grasped? [N-117](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117) [N-122](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-122)

<a id="u02649"></a>

What is this crucial point of buddhas' not being deluded?

<a id="u02650"></a>

From what does sentient beings' delusion arise?

<a id="u02651"></a>

Where does the primordial knowing of self-awareness abide?

<a id="u02652"></a>

From what does its path arise?

<a id="u02653"></a>

What is the door through which primordial knowing arises?

<a id="u02654"></a>

What is the crucial point of the lamps like?

<a id="u02655"></a>

How do their characteristics appear?

<a id="u02656"></a>

Where does the crucial point of the object arise?

<a id="u02657"></a>

Relying on that, by what are the distinctive [qualities] accomplished?

<a id="u02658"></a>

What is the crucial point of the stages of conceptual mind like?

<a id="u02659"></a>

How is [this] to be taken up in experience?

<a id="u02660"></a>

What appearances of signs and measures does it have? [N-117](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117)

<a id="u02661"></a>

What are the appearances of the intermediate state?

<a id="u02662"></a>

By what is the liberation of one's own ordinary mind effected?

<a id="u02663"></a>

After liberation, how does compassionate responsiveness arise?

<a id="u02664"></a>

What is the crucial point of that primordial knowing itself?

<a id="u02665"></a>

What are its manner of abiding and natural state like?

<a id="u02666"></a>

Is enlightened intent one or different?

<a id="u02667"></a>

What is the crucial point concerning the all-basis and dharma embodiment, the two?

<a id="u02668"></a>

What is the crucial point concerning ordinary mind and primordial knowing?

<a id="u02669"></a>

By what are the clear and turbid aspects of the elements distinguished?

<a id="u02670"></a>

How are the lamps packed? [N-117](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117)

<a id="u02671"></a>

What is the appearance of packing like?

<a id="u02672"></a>

How many crucial points of the channels are there?

<a id="u02673"></a>

How many aspects of training ordinary mind are there?

<a id="u02674"></a>

What causes afflictions to arise?

<a id="u02675"></a>

Because I do not know these,

<a id="u02676"></a>

Teacher of great compassionate responsiveness,

<a id="u02677"></a>

although you have neither verbal expression nor conceptual examination,

<a id="u02678"></a>

from the space of uncontrived intrinsic nature,

<a id="u02679"></a>

speak to me through inexpressible speech.’ [N-117](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-117)

<a id="u02680"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U02680.*[^G-U02680]

<a id="u02681"></a>

Then the teacher, the vajra-holder,

<a id="u02682"></a>

from the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature free from conceptual elaborations,

<a id="u02683"></a>

though not abiding as words, phrases, or names,

<a id="u02684"></a>

spoke to the lord of the gods in these words.

<a id="u02685"></a>

From the space in which conceptual thought is exhausted,

<a id="u02686"></a>

though not proclaimed as words, phrases, or names,

<a id="u02687"></a>

great naturally arisen primordial knowing itself,

<a id="u02688"></a>

from intrinsic nature, unmade from the outset,

<a id="u02689"></a>

arose thus as unarranged phrases: [N-118](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118)

<a id="u02690"></a>

‘The beginning of samsara:

<a id="u02691"></a>

from the undifferentiated, unmade, self and other,

<a id="u02692"></a>

an object held as a focus arose.

<a id="u02693"></a>

From conceptualizing and holding, distinct appearances [arose].

<a id="u02694"></a>

In the twelve connections of focusing and holding,

<a id="u02695"></a>

there is the very beginning of samsara. [N-118](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118)

<a id="u02696"></a>

This is the manner of delusion through three ignorances.

<a id="u02697"></a>

The single cause, undifferentiated, is the root:

<a id="u02698"></a>

not knowing [this] to be one's own identity.

<a id="u02699"></a>

This, too, is the beginning of samsara.

<a id="u02700"></a>

From this: one, three, and four hundred—[Numerical grouping unresolved.] [N-118](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118)

<a id="u02701"></a>

definitely grasped through eight surrounding conditions—

<a id="u02702"></a>

the application of calculating delusion abiding in the Ground. [N-118](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118)

<a id="u02703"></a>

From this, there is the co-emergent [ignorance]. [N-T58](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t58)

<a id="u02704"></a>

From the ultimate and the relative, apparent and imputed—

<a id="u02705"></a>

the objects belonging to the two truths—

<a id="u02706"></a>

engagement in all activities becomes twenty. [N-118](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118)

<a id="u02707"></a>

From the particulars of the apparent aspects of those objects,

<a id="u02708"></a>

[imputing ignorance has] four definite conditions: [N-T58](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t58)

<a id="u02709"></a>

definitely grasped through cause and dominance,

<a id="u02710"></a>

the object of focus itself, and simultaneity. [N-118](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-118)

<a id="u02711"></a>

The portions of delusion, too, are eight and two.

<a id="u02712"></a>

Because the times of the objects of focus coincide,

<a id="u02713"></a>

as for the delusion imputed as formations:

<a id="u02714"></a>

for a hundred gatherings, one agent. [N-119](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-119)[^G-U02714]

<a id="u02715"></a>

Through the completion of all activities, limitless [forms] arise.

<a id="u02716"></a>

Calculate their times and faculties.

<a id="u02717"></a>

According to the manner of the delusion of consciousness,

<a id="u02718"></a>

the objects appearing are eight and six.

<a id="u02719"></a>

Examination through knowing becomes twenty.

<a id="u02720"></a>

Through this, calculate the body and limbs. [N-119](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-119)

<a id="u02721"></a>

Furthermore, for the portion of delusion of name-and-form:

<a id="u02722"></a>

from naming, four designations, sixty-two—[Numerical grouping unresolved.] [N-119](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-119)

<a id="u02723"></a>

samsara and nirvana, four, together with letters.

<a id="u02724"></a>

For form, distinguish fine and coarse characteristics;

<a id="u02725"></a>

establishing and holding: one hundred and five.

<a id="u02726"></a>

Calculate four grounds that transform all activities. [N-119](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-119)

<a id="u02727"></a>

For the six wheels of the sense bases, too,

<a id="u02728"></a>

the crucial points of objects, knowing, and faculties:

<a id="u02729"></a>

their respective portions of entry are eight, two, and six.

<a id="u02730"></a>

That is the extent of calculating the objects of activities. [N-120](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-120)

<a id="u02731"></a>

For the portion of contact, the manner of ordinary mind's delusion:

<a id="u02732"></a>

causing, appearing, and accompanied by experience—six.

<a id="u02733"></a>

Activities, agents, and contact-knowing—five.

<a id="u02734"></a>

Calculate seven transformations of the final conceptual thought. [N-120](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-120)

<a id="u02735"></a>

The manners of feeling are seven and two.

<a id="u02736"></a>

From conceptual thought concerning the apparent aspect endowed with mental faculty,

<a id="u02737"></a>

transformations of mental faculty and ordinary mind become eighty.

<a id="u02738"></a>

From this, calculate seventy accompanied by conceptual thought.

<a id="u02739"></a>

From the three and five delusions of craving,

<a id="u02740"></a>

when enacted, the number is sixty. [N-120](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-120)[^G-U02740]

<a id="u02741"></a>

The number of ways of reversing at the limits of the branches

<a id="u02742"></a>

is two hundred; reverse at the culmination of activities.

<a id="u02743"></a>

The Grounds of delusion of grasping: thirteen, six. [Numerical grouping unresolved.] [N-120](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-120)

<a id="u02744"></a>

A hundred can contact forms at the culmination of activities.

<a id="u02745"></a>

For an object held in delusion, [these] become aspects of appearance.

<a id="u02746"></a>

Calculate their transformation into agents. [N-120](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-120)

<a id="u02747"></a>

For the twelve delusions of becoming,

<a id="u02748"></a>

the undifferentiated portion has sixteen.

<a id="u02749"></a>

Through this, objects and ordinary mind, with conceptual thought, are bound.

<a id="u02750"></a>

Examine the four activities individually. [N-120](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-120)

<a id="u02751"></a>

From the portion of birth: three, two, and five.

<a id="u02752"></a>

Entering and coming are performed by wind.

<a id="u02753"></a>

For the times of rising and abiding, there are eight.

<a id="u02754"></a>

The results of the manner of circling are twelve.

<a id="u02755"></a>

From aging and death, there are nine conditions of delusion.

<a id="u02756"></a>

For the conceptual thought of effort and cessation, there are eighteen.

<a id="u02757"></a>

Entry into an abode: eight, and a portion;

<a id="u02758"></a>

[there are] dormant tendencies and circling everywhere. [N-121](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-121)

<a id="u02759"></a>

Thus, from the twelve roots of delusion,

<a id="u02760"></a>

bodies, faculties, years,

<a id="u02761"></a>

and the respective elements are distinguished by time.

<a id="u02762"></a>

Self and other become four.

<a id="u02763"></a>

These arose at the beginning of samsara.

<a id="u02764"></a>

For sentient beings' realms, with their many [aspects],

<a id="u02765"></a>

in order to enter, engagement in all activities to their culmination,

<a id="u02766"></a>

endowed [with these], is complete and closely taught. [N-121](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-121)

<a id="u02767"></a>

**[Source heading: Second reply.]**

<a id="u02768"></a>

Lord of the gods, listen to this great wonder.

<a id="u02769"></a>

The final extent of passing beyond sorrow:

<a id="u02770"></a>

when all conceptual thought of delusion is exhausted,

<a id="u02771"></a>

and the appearance of the agency of samsara is absent,

<a id="u02772"></a>

delusion is self-appearance with conceptual thought exhausted.

<a id="u02773"></a>

To posit transcendence, the realm of sentient beings,

<a id="u02774"></a>

in its intrinsic nature, too, has this very object:

<a id="u02775"></a>

not dependent on another, completely established;

<a id="u02776"></a>

empty of imputed activity and expression;

<a id="u02777"></a>

not distinguished as possessing an extreme. [N-122](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-122)

<a id="u02778"></a>

Entering in pairs and pervading everywhere—

<a id="u02779"></a>

activities, faculties, conceptual minds, and objects—

<a id="u02780"></a>

the activity of entering is immediate. [N-122](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-122)

<a id="u02781"></a>

**[Source heading: Third reply.]**

<a id="u02782"></a>

Thus, concerning the beginning and end of samsara and nirvana,

<a id="u02783"></a>

because the buddhas have not become deluded,

<a id="u02784"></a>

through a noble faculty from the Ground,

<a id="u02785"></a>

they know self-appearance to be without intrinsic nature.

<a id="u02786"></a>

Conceptual examination and mental faculty have not strayed outward;

<a id="u02787"></a>

movement is cut off of itself. [N-123](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123)

<a id="u02788"></a>

Furthermore, in the great natural state,

<a id="u02789"></a>

what is called delusion is pure from the outset;

<a id="u02790"></a>

it cannot be contrived into nondelusion.

<a id="u02791"></a>

Phenomena that appear without existing, yet are not seen—

<a id="u02792"></a>

the conceptual thought that arises through doing ceases of itself.

<a id="u02793"></a>

There is no appearance as an object of focus through delusion.

<a id="u02794"></a>

Because the primordial radiance of intrinsic nature has not stirred,

<a id="u02795"></a>

all phenomena are equally of one taste.

<a id="u02796"></a>

Free from holding and conceptualizing, there is primordial buddhahood.

<a id="u02797"></a>

It cannot be contrived or transformed.

<a id="u02798"></a>

Its crucial point is the object:

<a id="u02799"></a>

the object, the empty, and awareness's expressiveness are equal.

<a id="u02800"></a>

This is not known through differentiating conceptual thought. [N-123](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123)

<a id="u02801"></a>

Furthermore, the crucial point of not being deluded:

<a id="u02802"></a>

through knowing one, all are liberated.

<a id="u02803"></a>

Through knowing that itself, there is reversal.

<a id="u02804"></a>

With no appearance and no exhaustion,

<a id="u02805"></a>

conceptual thought of arising, awareness, and abiding is exhausted. [N-123](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123)

<a id="u02806"></a>

Not differentiated through doing, it cannot be contrived.

<a id="u02807"></a>

Because there is no conceptual mind of action and agent,

<a id="u02808"></a>

with objects and conditions cleared away, there is buddhahood.

<a id="u02809"></a>

Free from extremes and without cessation,

<a id="u02810"></a>

one abides in fully complete deep absorption.

<a id="u02811"></a>

Because it is great, there is nothing above it,

<a id="u02812"></a>

for intrinsic nature has not stirred. [N-123](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-123)

<a id="u02813"></a>

**[Source heading: Fourth reply.]**

<a id="u02814"></a>

Sentient beings' delusion, by wandering in

<a id="u02815"></a>

the root dependent connections taught above,

<a id="u02816"></a>

arises as the foundation of calculating delusion's mechanism.

<a id="u02817"></a>

From the appearance of the nature of phenomena's own radiance,

<a id="u02818"></a>

the appearance of conceptualizing and holding captivates the mental faculty.

<a id="u02819"></a>

The object of delusion, through subtle activity,

<a id="u02820"></a>

is obscured, and self-appearance becomes impure. [N-124](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124)

<a id="u02821"></a>

From this, dormant tendencies themselves,

<a id="u02822"></a>

subtle, and the portions of particles,

<a id="u02823"></a>

come to be mere movement and holding.

<a id="u02824"></a>

From knowing that becomes subtle through subtlety,

<a id="u02825"></a>

conceptual examination and the apprehended portion become two.

<a id="u02826"></a>

The appearance of subtle-particle primordial knowing ceases.

<a id="u02827"></a>

Mere immediate object-knowing arises. [N-124](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124)

<a id="u02828"></a>

The twelve phenomena, ignorance and so forth,

<a id="u02829"></a>

become the twelve elemental classes of beings.

<a id="u02830"></a>

Their inner knowing, bodies of elements,

<a id="u02831"></a>

aggregates, elements, and also

<a id="u02832"></a>

sense bases—the gatherings of activities assemble.

<a id="u02833"></a>

From immeasurable gatherings of afflictions,

<a id="u02834"></a>

the assemblages of conceptual thought in beings' bodies are gathered.

<a id="u02835"></a>

From the many gatherings of dependent connections,

<a id="u02836"></a>

there are particular discordant forms and so forth. [N-124](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124)

<a id="u02837"></a>

The causes generated by the four elements

<a id="u02838"></a>

are thoroughly grouped by class and number.

<a id="u02839"></a>

From different, distinct colors,

<a id="u02840"></a>

they become manifest from the preceding portions.

<a id="u02841"></a>

Through the gathering of conceptual thoughts from the Ground,

<a id="u02842"></a>

also through twelve activities of agency,

<a id="u02843"></a>

the elemental aggregates are supported,

<a id="u02844"></a>

made to abide, form, and be destroyed.

<a id="u02845"></a>

From the twelve manners of delusion,

<a id="u02846"></a>

through maturing into agents,

<a id="u02847"></a>

beings' karma and merit,

<a id="u02848"></a>

and pure and impure forms,

<a id="u02849"></a>

are made to appear in their respective distinctions. [N-124](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124)

<a id="u02850"></a>

Furthermore, of the environment and its inhabitants,

<a id="u02851"></a>

the activities of the elements, too, are performed by wind.

<a id="u02852"></a>

Wind projects and gathers,

<a id="u02853"></a>

and makes [them] go, come, and enter.

<a id="u02854"></a>

Therefore, wind performs all activities.

<a id="u02855"></a>

Reversal itself also depends on wind;

<a id="u02856"></a>

distinguish [it] by the culmination of activities of entering and increasing. [N-124](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-124)

<a id="u02857"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifth reply.]**

<a id="u02858"></a>

The primordial knowing of self-awareness abides in the body,

<a id="u02859"></a>

like oil from a sesame seed.

<a id="u02860"></a>

The body's luster and splendor

<a id="u02861"></a>

are pervaded by the moisture of primordial knowing.

<a id="u02862"></a>

The embodiments that accord with the body,

<a id="u02863"></a>

and the signs of primordial knowing's light and colors—

<a id="u02864"></a>

through activities, entry, and embodiments,

<a id="u02865"></a>

[primordial knowing] pervades together with splendor. [N-125](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125)

<a id="u02866"></a>

Through discerning knowing itself and the activities of the lamps,

<a id="u02867"></a>

the characteristics of ultimate and relative are distinguished.

<a id="u02868"></a>

The respective crucial points of the spheres that bring maturity,

<a id="u02869"></a>

the conceptual thought that causes movement and holds coarsely,

<a id="u02870"></a>

and the four and two portions of primordial knowing's appearance—

<a id="u02871"></a>

through distinctions of arising and entering activity,

<a id="u02872"></a>

in the precious palace of the heart (citta), [N-125](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125)

<a id="u02873"></a>

a gathering of precious [qualities], with eight corners and doors,

<a id="u02874"></a>

there are five primordial knowings and five embodiments;

<a id="u02875"></a>

five winds, and five expressivenesses of knowing.

<a id="u02876"></a>

All have the intrinsic nature of fivefold groups. [N-125](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125)

<a id="u02877"></a>

The crucial point of embodiments is through radiance;

<a id="u02878"></a>

that of primordial knowing is through its manner of abiding.

<a id="u02879"></a>

Spheres are accomplished through retaining and stirring.

<a id="u02880"></a>

Light is spread and gathered. [N-125](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125)

<a id="u02881"></a>

The crucial point of wind is binding what is connected.

<a id="u02882"></a>

The activity of entering is distinguished by its culmination.

<a id="u02883"></a>

The karmic wind that causes its movement is exhausted,

<a id="u02884"></a>

and [this] is joined to the arrangement of delusion. [N-125](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-125)

<a id="u02885"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U02885.*[^G-U02885]

<a id="u02886"></a>

The crucial point of awareness is arising and

<a id="u02887"></a>

retaining and directing [it] toward space. [N-126](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-126)[^G-U02887]

<a id="u02888"></a>

*Joined source fragment; no additional root text at U02888.*[^G-U02888][^G-U02887]

<a id="u02889"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U02889.*[^G-U02889][^G-U02887]

<a id="u02890"></a>

The crucial point of basic space is gathering,

<a id="u02891"></a>

and joining summoning with the elements' dispersal and spreading.

<a id="u02892"></a>

The crucial point of appearances is increase and exhaustion;

<a id="u02893"></a>

the manifold are liberated in their own places. [N-126](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-126)

<a id="u02894"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixth reply.]**

<a id="u02895"></a>

The path of the primordial knowing of awareness:

<a id="u02896"></a>

from the wheels of one's own channels,

<a id="u02897"></a>

where the three embodiments abide complete in themselves,

<a id="u02898"></a>

Roma, Kyangma, and Kundarma—

<a id="u02899"></a>

through these three ‘mothers’ [ma], the body is held.

<a id="u02900"></a>

The central [channel] supports the four wheels. [N-127](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127)

<a id="u02901"></a>

Because these abide on the right, left, and center,

<a id="u02902"></a>

through the distinction of relative and ultimate,

<a id="u02903"></a>

they generate the body and limbs

<a id="u02904"></a>

and also bring the lamps to maturity.

<a id="u02905"></a>

Through Roma, tastes enter the wheels:

<a id="u02906"></a>

wind stirs [them] and projects the spheres.

<a id="u02907"></a>

Ro [‘taste’], through reliance on the channels,

<a id="u02908"></a>

expands body and ordinary mind through bliss.

<a id="u02909"></a>

Ma [‘mother’] gathers the refined parts

<a id="u02910"></a>

and makes a foundation for the upper and lower abodes. [N-127](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127)

<a id="u02911"></a>

Here, retaining and packing are the crucial point.

<a id="u02912"></a>

On its six branches,

<a id="u02913"></a>

bloodletting and cauterization obstruct primordial knowing.

<a id="u02914"></a>

To attain warmth, massage is the crucial point.

<a id="u02915"></a>

Wind and spheres are to be summoned. [N-127](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127)

<a id="u02916"></a>

Through Kyangma, in the generative wheel,

<a id="u02917"></a>

wind projects and gathers, and spheres enter.

<a id="u02918"></a>

Rkyang [‘single’] is so called because it has no counterpart;

<a id="u02919"></a>

it reveals the enlightened intent of the unique.

<a id="u02920"></a>

Ma [‘mother’] clears away the turbid parts

<a id="u02921"></a>

and generates the splendor of essence.

<a id="u02922"></a>

Here, loosening and massage are the crucial point.

<a id="u02923"></a>

Because its branches do not appear,

<a id="u02924"></a>

apply spheres to the four junctures of maturation.

<a id="u02925"></a>

One wishing to gain mastery over food

<a id="u02926"></a>

packs wind and does not discard saliva.

<a id="u02927"></a>

[This] is to be thoroughly examined through the pledge. [N-127](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127)[^G-U02927]

<a id="u02928"></a>

Through Kundarma, mindfulness

<a id="u02929"></a>

and the wheel of the summit mature through gathering.

<a id="u02930"></a>

It reverses karmic wind and retains primordial knowing.

<a id="u02931"></a>

Kun [‘all’] grants the qualities of the emanation embodiment.

<a id="u02932"></a>

'Dar [‘quivering’] [grants] the qualities of the complete enjoyment embodiment.

<a id="u02933"></a>

Ma [‘mother’] directly reveals the dharma embodiment.

<a id="u02934"></a>

For its twelve branches,

<a id="u02935"></a>

loosening and rolling are the crucial point.

<a id="u02936"></a>

To increase the lifespan, application [of an ointment] is taught.

<a id="u02937"></a>

Spheres are to be [worked with] through body and ordinary mind. [N-127](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127)

<a id="u02938"></a>

From these paths of primordial knowing,

<a id="u02939"></a>

spheres come to be accompanied by embodiments.

<a id="u02940"></a>

Wind performs all the activities of going and coming.

<a id="u02941"></a>

The master of ordinary mind is held by the mental faculty;

<a id="u02942"></a>

through the path, [it] is held and summoned. [N-127](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-127)

<a id="u02943"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventh reply.]**

<a id="u02944"></a>

The door through which primordial knowing arises:

<a id="u02945"></a>

the gathering of all refined bodily vital essence

<a id="u02946"></a>

emerges through the door called cakṣu (‘eye’). [N-128](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128)

<a id="u02947"></a>

Holding the two portions, it reveals appearances. [N-128](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128)

<a id="u02948"></a>

From the generative channel, arising in connection,

<a id="u02949"></a>

becoming one at the tip,

<a id="u02950"></a>

making the faculty arise toward its object—

<a id="u02951"></a>

one has five branches,

<a id="u02952"></a>

appearing at the respective sense gates.

<a id="u02953"></a>

In particular, through its faults and actions,

<a id="u02954"></a>

from a coil like a buffalo's horn (ba-min), [N-128](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128)

<a id="u02955"></a>

what is called a-bras is half white and half black. [N-128](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128)

<a id="u02956"></a>

It is aware and holds forms,

<a id="u02957"></a>

and completely reveals knowing's own expressiveness.

<a id="u02958"></a>

Here primordial knowing actually arises.

<a id="u02959"></a>

Through direct perception and its own crucial point,

<a id="u02960"></a>

in the self-appearing object of the pure nature of phenomena,

<a id="u02961"></a>

what is accompanied by conceptual thought abides in cessation. [N-128](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-128)

<a id="u02962"></a>

What brings this about?

<a id="u02963"></a>

From the arising of the ultimate nature of phenomena,

<a id="u02964"></a>

there are what are called the four lamps.

<a id="u02965"></a>

Through maturing, entering, and pervading emptiness,

<a id="u02966"></a>

they gather the nature of phenomena's appearance as object. [N-129](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129)

<a id="u02967"></a>

The water lamp of the far-reaching lasso gathers forms. [N-T64](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t64)

<a id="u02968"></a>

Wind moves and projects [them].

<a id="u02969"></a>

Through increase and decrease in coming and going,

<a id="u02970"></a>

it gathers primordial knowing.

<a id="u02971"></a>

The lamp of the empty sphere performs all activities. [N-T64](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t64)

<a id="u02972"></a>

Taking the lead on the path of transcendence,

<a id="u02973"></a>

through wind it generates luster and splendor;

<a id="u02974"></a>

transforming [them], it reveals appearances of primordial knowing.

<a id="u02975"></a>

The lamp of naturally arising discerning knowing

<a id="u02976"></a>

gathers all phenomena into equal, single taste,

<a id="u02977"></a>

and holds self-appearance.

<a id="u02978"></a>

This cuts the connected flow of habitual tendencies.

<a id="u02979"></a>

Gathering the refined part of basic space,

<a id="u02980"></a>

and retaining the embodiments of awareness,

<a id="u02981"></a>

through maturing the three crucial points into vital essence,

<a id="u02982"></a>

it causes samsara itself to be abandoned. [N-129](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129)

<a id="u02983"></a>

Thus, because the lamps arise through the eyes,

<a id="u02984"></a>

with the crucial points of the buddhas' enlightened intent gathered,

<a id="u02985"></a>

the objects of self-appearance are held. [N-129](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-129)

<a id="u02986"></a>

**[Source heading: Eighth reply.]**

<a id="u02987"></a>

I will explain the crucial point of the lamps.

<a id="u02988"></a>

Fortunate son of the gods, listen!

<a id="u02989"></a>

Through the object, awareness, and wind,

<a id="u02990"></a>

because the nature of phenomena is taken as the path,

<a id="u02991"></a>

the nature of phenomena in which primordial knowing is complete—

<a id="u02992"></a>

this, too, is the crucial point of the lamps.

<a id="u02993"></a>

The crucial point of the water lamp of the far-reaching lasso is nonmovement. [N-T64](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t64)

<a id="u02994"></a>

At this time, join [it] with the three gazes.

<a id="u02995"></a>

The crucial point of the discerning-knowing lamp is training.

<a id="u02996"></a>

Through three rites, increase [it],

<a id="u02997"></a>

and through the crucial point of accomplishment, expand its extent.

<a id="u02998"></a>

The crucial point of the sphere lamp is packing.

<a id="u02999"></a>

By not separating the channels, thumb, and finger,

<a id="u03000"></a>

primordial knowing increases. [N-130](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-130)

<a id="u03001"></a>

The crucial point of basic space is guiding.

<a id="u03002"></a>

The yogin, in the methods of training with the sun,

<a id="u03003"></a>

should train thoroughly and guide [this]. [N-131](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131)

<a id="u03004"></a>

One should also train with the moon.

<a id="u03005"></a>

Whoever continually does this

<a id="u03006"></a>

cuts the flow of delusive appearances.

<a id="u03007"></a>

The pure light of the five primordial knowings—

<a id="u03008"></a>

earth, stones, mountains, and crags emptied of their traces—

<a id="u03009"></a>

in isolated patches and mere portions of a region,

<a id="u03010"></a>

appears to one skilled in guiding. [N-131](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131)

<a id="u03011"></a>

At this time, adjustment through methods is the crucial point.

<a id="u03012"></a>

The complete nature of phenomena is attained.

<a id="u03013"></a>

Through familiarity in this way, the power of the nature of phenomena

<a id="u03014"></a>

[brings] the body's elements to completion in their own places.

<a id="u03015"></a>

Thus, because the flow of the body's elements is cut,

<a id="u03016"></a>

without the appearance of contaminated aggregates,

<a id="u03017"></a>

one attains manifest buddhahood in this. [N-131](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-131)

<a id="u03018"></a>

**[Source heading: Ninth reply.]**

<a id="u03019"></a>

The characteristics of the lamps are like this:

<a id="u03020"></a>

in general, they reveal appearances,

<a id="u03021"></a>

join primordial knowing as mother and child,

<a id="u03022"></a>

and connect the boundary of samsara and nirvana.

<a id="u03023"></a>

Through their own radiance being clear, within a body of light,

<a id="u03024"></a>

they gather primordial knowing free from conceptualization.

<a id="u03025"></a>

Within the appearances of objects accompanied by conceptual thought,

<a id="u03026"></a>

they hold the foundation of non-conceptual primordial knowing. [N-132](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132)

<a id="u03027"></a>

The characteristic of the far-reaching-lasso lamp:

<a id="u03028"></a>

through pervading it enters; through entering it pervades.

<a id="u03029"></a>

It abides as the master-agent of the four elements.

<a id="u03030"></a>

It holds, enters, spreads, and acts. [N-132](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132)

<a id="u03031"></a>

The characteristics of discerning knowing:

<a id="u03032"></a>

entering clear words, it gathers the foundation;

<a id="u03033"></a>

it burns afflictions, karma, and habitual tendencies;

<a id="u03034"></a>

it matures and cuts the life-force of samsara and nirvana. [N-132](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132)

<a id="u03035"></a>

The characteristics of the empty sphere:

<a id="u03036"></a>

round, with intrinsic nature entirely clear,

<a id="u03037"></a>

red and colored, with clear light,

<a id="u03038"></a>

it spreads with elaboration. [N-132](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-132)

<a id="u03039"></a>

The characteristics of pure basic space itself:

<a id="u03040"></a>

blue, complete in itself, with two portions.

<a id="u03041"></a>

Through methods and discerning knowing being complete,

<a id="u03042"></a>

from the two paired portions, it holds two embodiments

<a id="u03043"></a>

and makes appearances spread abundantly.

<a id="u03044"></a>

These, too, complete one's own knowing.

<a id="u03045"></a>

They reveal a reflection of the all-pervading lord, the hero.

<a id="u03046"></a>

They draw vajra chains from the balance (srang). [N-133](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133)

<a id="u03047"></a>

Like the form of a complete maṇḍala,

<a id="u03048"></a>

they reveal the splendor of the primordial knowings,

<a id="u03049"></a>

and also reveal the deep splendor and luster of light.

<a id="u03050"></a>

There is aspiration for embodiments and spheres. [N-133](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133)

<a id="u03051"></a>

Thus, the embodiment in which primordial knowing is complete—

<a id="u03052"></a>

whoever sees this attains buddhahood.

<a id="u03053"></a>

Where the three embodiments are exhausted, intrinsic nature is liberated.

<a id="u03054"></a>

Crossing the precipice of apprehended object and apprehending subject,

<a id="u03055"></a>

the tether of samsara and nirvana is cut. [N-133](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-133)

<a id="u03056"></a>

**[Source heading: Tenth reply.]**

<a id="u03057"></a>

Its crucial point of the object is like this:

<a id="u03058"></a>

in the empty outer object, free from clouds,

<a id="u03059"></a>

yoga abides in the birds' path through the sky.

<a id="u03060"></a>

Not separate from this is the nature of phenomena. [N-134](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134)

<a id="u03061"></a>

In general, through their individual characteristics,

<a id="u03062"></a>

objects are distinct; completion in one is granted. [N-134](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134)

<a id="u03063"></a>

Through primordial knowing gathering, ordinary mind's flow stops.

<a id="u03064"></a>

Through the elements gathering, the body is complete.

<a id="u03065"></a>

From appearances of great distinction,

<a id="u03066"></a>

[this] abides as the object of maturing, entering, and moving.

<a id="u03067"></a>

Because the limit of conceptual thought is exhausted, one is liberated from samsara.

<a id="u03068"></a>

The far-reaching lasso's objects are forms and so forth.

<a id="u03069"></a>

It gathers, holds, and projects [them].

<a id="u03070"></a>

Through the crucial point, bind and place [them],

<a id="u03071"></a>

transform entities and cut the root;

<a id="u03072"></a>

draw [them] forth, and train with form. [N-134](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134)

<a id="u03073"></a>

Discerning knowing's objects are words and meaning.

<a id="u03074"></a>

Because words and meaning are intended to be connected,

<a id="u03075"></a>

there is knowing through indication, and through expression.

<a id="u03076"></a>

Indication: examples and symbolic indications;

<a id="u03077"></a>

indication through words is expressed last.

<a id="u03078"></a>

Knowing through expression: conventions,

<a id="u03079"></a>

pointing out, guiding,

<a id="u03080"></a>

decisive resolution, and distinguishing. [N-134](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134)

<a id="u03081"></a>

The sphere's object is color:

<a id="u03082"></a>

five, six, three, two, and six.

<a id="u03083"></a>

Clear, warm, cool,

<a id="u03084"></a>

light, and moving—it holds these identities.

<a id="u03085"></a>

These are the elements of the ultimate nature of phenomena. [N-134](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134)

<a id="u03086"></a>

Basic space's object is empty,

<a id="u03087"></a>

clear, free from obscuration, and emitting splendor;

<a id="u03088"></a>

pervading and vast, gathered into one.

<a id="u03089"></a>

In space whose fine particles have cleared,

<a id="u03090"></a>

the pure nature of phenomena is complete in evenness.

<a id="u03091"></a>

Clear, it is the radiance of the five primordial knowings,

<a id="u03092"></a>

the very body of pure clear light. [N-134](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-134)

<a id="u03093"></a>

**[Source heading: Eleventh reply.]**

<a id="u03094"></a>

The supports of the lamps of pure primordial knowing:

<a id="u03095"></a>

in general, through support and what relies upon it,

<a id="u03096"></a>

the seeds of samsara and nirvana increase.

<a id="u03097"></a>

The far-reaching lasso's support is appearance.

<a id="u03098"></a>

Through the distinction of pure and impure,

<a id="u03099"></a>

it relies on karma and accumulations,

<a id="u03100"></a>

and on embodiments and primordial knowing. [N-135](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135)

<a id="u03101"></a>

Naturally arising discerning knowing relies on

<a id="u03102"></a>

words, conceptual thought about is and is not,

<a id="u03103"></a>

tenet systems, viewing and cultivation,

<a id="u03104"></a>

and the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u03105"></a>

The empty sphere relies on

<a id="u03106"></a>

consciousness itself, the intervening space,

<a id="u03107"></a>

channels, wind, and spheres,

<a id="u03108"></a>

and primordial knowing.

<a id="u03109"></a>

Perfectly pure basic space relies on

<a id="u03110"></a>

the space between the eyebrows, space,

<a id="u03111"></a>

light and spheres,

<a id="u03112"></a>

and manifold embodiments. [N-135](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135)

<a id="u03113"></a>

Furthermore, the distinctive supports and objects:

<a id="u03114"></a>

through three, they rely on the distinction of gathered agency. [N-135](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135)

<a id="u03115"></a>

By relying on the far-reaching lasso itself,

<a id="u03116"></a>

free from faults, having abandoned action and agency,

<a id="u03117"></a>

one engages in the mother nature of phenomena.

<a id="u03118"></a>

Through purifying karma and habitual tendencies,

<a id="u03119"></a>

relying on the vision of the direct perception of the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u03120"></a>

one empties samsara from its depths,

<a id="u03121"></a>

and can touch the boundary of passing beyond sorrow.

<a id="u03122"></a>

Through the nature of ordinary mind in which conceptual thought and examination are exhausted,

<a id="u03123"></a>

samsara is cast far away.

<a id="u03124"></a>

Having seen the embodiment of pure primordial knowing,

<a id="u03125"></a>

the point of aim of samsara and nirvana, the two, is destroyed. [N-135](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135)

<a id="u03126"></a>

Whoever sees a beautiful flower—

<a id="u03127"></a>

through this, the nature of phenomena is led into an irrigation channel.

<a id="u03128"></a>

Having placed awareness on the balance (srang),

<a id="u03129"></a>

one is brought to where phenomena are exhausted. [N-135](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135)

<a id="u03130"></a>

A person who becomes familiar with basic space,

<a id="u03131"></a>

having emptied delusive appearances,

<a id="u03132"></a>

through seeing the object of the pure nature of phenomena,

<a id="u03133"></a>

emerges from the very source of objects of attachment. [N-135](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-135)

<a id="u03134"></a>

**[Source heading: Twelfth reply.]**

<a id="u03135"></a>

The crucial point of the stages of conceptual mind is like this:

<a id="u03136"></a>

through distinctions in the vessel of the elements,

<a id="u03137"></a>

through distinctions in the elements' junctures of maturation,

<a id="u03138"></a>

and through differences in beings' conceptual minds,

<a id="u03139"></a>

families and faculties differ.

<a id="u03140"></a>

Furthermore, through the magical displays of the elements:

<a id="u03141"></a>

water's earth, its activity purified,

<a id="u03142"></a>

at first can spread;

<a id="u03143"></a>

finally it does not forget the teaching it has grasped.

<a id="u03144"></a>

Water's fire grasps readily and forgets.

<a id="u03145"></a>

Water's wind transforms conceptual mind,

<a id="u03146"></a>

entering everything, without holding a ground. [N-136](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136)

<a id="u03147"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U03147.*[^G-U03147]

<a id="u03148"></a>

Water's wind [brings] spaciousness,

<a id="u03149"></a>

and brings object and knowing into contact.

<a id="u03150"></a>

Water's water has a strong inclination,

<a id="u03151"></a>

with little perception, and grasps. [N-136](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136)

<a id="u03152"></a>

From the pure elements of earth:

<a id="u03153"></a>

earth's water increases words

<a id="u03154"></a>

and multiplies manifold conceptual thoughts.

<a id="u03155"></a>

Earth's fire [makes] the faculties supremely pure,

<a id="u03156"></a>

revealing them as clear and endowed with gentleness.

<a id="u03157"></a>

Earth's wind [brings] stupefaction

<a id="u03158"></a>

and interruptions, alternating at intervals.

<a id="u03159"></a>

Earth's earth extensively holds joints and limbs. [N-136](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136)

<a id="u03160"></a>

Fire's earth expands the faculties.

<a id="u03161"></a>

[Fire's] water reveals entering and pervading.

<a id="u03162"></a>

[Fire's] wind makes the faculties exceedingly sharp.

<a id="u03163"></a>

Fire's fire grasps readily and forgets.

<a id="u03164"></a>

Wind's earth has great engagement with meaning.

<a id="u03165"></a>

[Wind's] water makes the object of deep absorption clear.

<a id="u03166"></a>

[Wind's] fire enables enjoyment of words.

<a id="u03167"></a>

Wind's wind holds conceptual thought and examination. [N-136](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-136)

<a id="u03168"></a>

When these elements are impure,

<a id="u03169"></a>

they reveal engagement in all mistaken activities.

<a id="u03170"></a>

Appearances distract, and knowing changes.

<a id="u03171"></a>

Conceptual thought is coarse and arouses nonvirtue.

<a id="u03172"></a>

Even the ordinary mind [oriented] toward the teaching is abandoned. [N-137](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137)

<a id="u03173"></a>

Thus, through the vessel of the elements being pure,

<a id="u03174"></a>

the many paths of liberation are revealed,

<a id="u03175"></a>

and the three embodiments are authentically established.

<a id="u03176"></a>

Then, having entered the mother's womb,

<a id="u03177"></a>

through the calculation of years, months, and days,

<a id="u03178"></a>

and the respective elements' own times,

<a id="u03179"></a>

having definitely grasped the measures of time,

<a id="u03180"></a>

through restoring each respective element,

<a id="u03181"></a>

the mantra practitioner should diligently rely on dependent connections.

<a id="u03182"></a>

Through the rite of generating the elements,

<a id="u03183"></a>

faculties are sharp, and primordial knowing is clear.

<a id="u03184"></a>

Form and color are complete.

<a id="u03185"></a>

One is loving toward beings and endowed with enjoyments,

<a id="u03186"></a>

maturing into one who abandons misdeeds and acts virtuously. [N-137](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137)

<a id="u03187"></a>

Thus, the elements protect the vital essence.

<a id="u03188"></a>

Through differences in the vessel's color,

<a id="u03189"></a>

just as the color of glass (man-shel), too, changes,

<a id="u03190"></a>

the stainless nature of ordinary mind, too,

<a id="u03191"></a>

is like that through the vessel of the elements. [N-137](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-137)

<a id="u03192"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirteenth reply.]**

<a id="u03193"></a>

This is the crucial point of taking [the teaching] up in experience:

<a id="u03194"></a>

the support of focus and the abiding of reflection.

<a id="u03195"></a>

The objects of focus are form, sound, smell, taste, and touch.

<a id="u03196"></a>

Train in the domains of the five desires.

<a id="u03197"></a>

For form: colors and shapes,

<a id="u03198"></a>

different crafts and illusory expressiveness,

<a id="u03199"></a>

and manifold optical illusions. [N-138](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138)

<a id="u03200"></a>

For sound: lute and booming pot,

<a id="u03201"></a>

many-stringed instrument, earthenware drum, and flute,

<a id="u03202"></a>

and sounds such as ‘phek’ and ‘har.’ [N-138](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138)

<a id="u03203"></a>

For smell: combine fragrant substances,

<a id="u03204"></a>

and act through profound, agreeable dependent connections.

<a id="u03205"></a>

For taste: jaggery endowed with six [unspecified aspects],

<a id="u03206"></a>

and various mixtures of grape wine are transformed.

<a id="u03207"></a>

Join the branches of dependent connections. [N-138](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138)

<a id="u03208"></a>

For touch, the skilled person sits on a seat.

<a id="u03209"></a>

Through crossed bows, a combination of elements,

<a id="u03210"></a>

and certain leaves of trees,

<a id="u03211"></a>

through mere touch, in the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u03212"></a>

ordinary mind abides without separation. [N-138](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138)

<a id="u03213"></a>

For ordinary mind: holding and abiding,

<a id="u03214"></a>

cutting the root and pursuing through entities,

<a id="u03215"></a>

searching between object and knowing,

<a id="u03216"></a>

and casting into distraction. [N-138](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-138)

<a id="u03217"></a>

**[Source heading: Fourteenth reply.]**

<a id="u03218"></a>

From taking [this] up in experience in this way,

<a id="u03219"></a>

the signs and measures are as follows.

<a id="u03220"></a>

The body appears light, with unimpeded penetration.

<a id="u03221"></a>

It does not touch the earth and is unobstructed.

<a id="u03222"></a>

Speech's capacity is complete; its store of words is exhausted.

<a id="u03223"></a>

Ordinary mind is capable of transference.

<a id="u03224"></a>

Eye, ear, nose, tongue,

<a id="u03225"></a>

and all bodily faculties are exhausted.

<a id="u03226"></a>

Because there is no intrinsic nature, their capacities are exhausted. [N-139](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-139)

<a id="u03227"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifteenth reply.]**

<a id="u03228"></a>

The appearances of the intermediate state:

<a id="u03229"></a>

the aggregates of abiding in intrinsic nature,

<a id="u03230"></a>

the elements, and afflictions appear as objects. [N-140](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140)

<a id="u03231"></a>

At death there are mistiness and wavering,

<a id="u03232"></a>

and knowing does not remain in one [place].

<a id="u03233"></a>

In the intermediate state of the nature of phenomena: embodiments,

<a id="u03234"></a>

primordial knowing, light, and colors.

<a id="u03235"></a>

Spheres spread, and elemental appearances are pure.

<a id="u03236"></a>

Whatever appears is the immeasurable palace of the nature of phenomena. [N-140](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140)

<a id="u03237"></a>

The appearances of becoming are fitful,

<a id="u03238"></a>

fleeting, like dream appearances.

<a id="u03239"></a>

Appearing true, though false, is the body of mental faculty.

<a id="u03240"></a>

White, gleg [unresolved], and so forth—the lights of the six classes—[^G-U03240]

<a id="u03241"></a>

are appearances of their respective objects of focus. [N-140](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-140)

<a id="u03242"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixteenth reply.]**

<a id="u03243"></a>

What is called the liberation of one's own ordinary mind

<a id="u03244"></a>

is not a going from one [place] to another.

<a id="u03245"></a>

Ordinary mind is not released by anyone.

<a id="u03246"></a>

Complete in the Ground, there is no going or coming.

<a id="u03247"></a>

It is not found by examination; there is no reason.

<a id="u03248"></a>

Without Ground or root, it abides empty.

<a id="u03249"></a>

That ordinary mind, abiding in its own clarity,

<a id="u03250"></a>

liberated through the crucial point, is not partial to any side. [N-141](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-141)

<a id="u03251"></a>

Liberated through time, there is no basis of dependence.

<a id="u03252"></a>

Liberated without effort, there is no effortful accomplishment.

<a id="u03253"></a>

Liberated through confidence, there is no endeavor or effort.

<a id="u03254"></a>

This is explained as liberation through confidence.

<a id="u03255"></a>

Liberated from the outset, there is nothing to repeat.

<a id="u03256"></a>

Liberated by itself, there is no antidote.

<a id="u03257"></a>

Liberated nakedly, it vanishes where it is seen.

<a id="u03258"></a>

Completely liberated, it is effortless. [N-142](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-142)

<a id="u03259"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventeenth reply.]**

<a id="u03260"></a>

In the nature of ordinary mind liberated in this way,

<a id="u03261"></a>

it is not that compassionate responsiveness is absent.

<a id="u03262"></a>

Through primordial knowing that abides in the Ground,

<a id="u03263"></a>

intrinsic nature, by its own momentum, serves as a support for holding. [N-143](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143)

<a id="u03264"></a>

Through primordial knowing that holds characteristics,

<a id="u03265"></a>

purity is brought to maturity through conditions.

<a id="u03266"></a>

Through primordial knowing of knowing and what is to be known,

<a id="u03267"></a>

accomplishments are granted to those with aspiration and devotion.

<a id="u03268"></a>

Furthermore, when the nature of ordinary mind matures,

<a id="u03269"></a>

the Ground abides in the dharma embodiment.

<a id="u03270"></a>

The stains of what is pure from the outset are exhausted. [N-143](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143)

<a id="u03271"></a>

Essence is undeluded from the outset.

<a id="u03272"></a>

Making appearances arise without cessation is spontaneous presence.

<a id="u03273"></a>

Intrinsic nature is not determined as ‘like this.’

<a id="u03274"></a>

Because it pervades all, it is compassionate responsiveness.

<a id="u03275"></a>

Samsara and passing beyond are not cut off.

<a id="u03276"></a>

The primordial knowing of the complete enjoyment embodiment holds characteristics.

<a id="u03277"></a>

Through objects and entities being pure, there is the mirror-like [primordial knowing],

<a id="u03278"></a>

the clear reflection of samsara and nirvana.

<a id="u03279"></a>

There is evenness without partiality.

<a id="u03280"></a>

Relying on the objects of the faculties, there is discriminating [primordial knowing]. [N-143](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143) [N-T80](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80)

<a id="u03281"></a>

Not through effort, activity is accomplished.

<a id="u03282"></a>

Because there is no difference, there is the basic space of phenomena. [N-143](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143) [N-T80](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t80)

<a id="u03283"></a>

In the emanation embodiment, there is what is to be known.

<a id="u03284"></a>

Through knowing how [things are], an entity's

<a id="u03285"></a>

natural state is known for one's own purpose.

<a id="u03286"></a>

Through knowing the multiplicity, concerning those to be trained,

<a id="u03287"></a>

one knows how their intentions abide.

<a id="u03288"></a>

Thus, from the ways compassionate responsiveness arises,

<a id="u03289"></a>

for the different beings to be trained,

<a id="u03290"></a>

there are that many embodiments that train them. [N-143](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-143)

<a id="u03291"></a>

**[Source heading: Eighteenth reply.]**

<a id="u03292"></a>

In teaching that very crucial point of primordial knowing:

<a id="u03293"></a>

first, its manner of pervading the Ground;

<a id="u03294"></a>

in between, its completion on the path;

<a id="u03295"></a>

finally, it pervades the bodies of beings.

<a id="u03296"></a>

At the time of the body, it is like this:

<a id="u03297"></a>

in the aggregates of gathered elements,

<a id="u03298"></a>

within the precious palace of the heart (citta),

<a id="u03299"></a>
