the portion of essence, pure from the outset,

<a id="u03300"></a>

is the radiance of embodiment, empty and clear united.

<a id="u03301"></a>

Faces and hands complete: the manner of the vase embodiment.

<a id="u03302"></a>

It abides as an orb of spontaneously present light.

<a id="u03303"></a>

Compassionate responsiveness is clear in distinct colors.

<a id="u03304"></a>

Roma, Kyangma, and Kundarma;

<a id="u03305"></a>

the Kati crystal tube channel—

<a id="u03306"></a>

the mirror-like, evenness, and discriminating,

<a id="u03307"></a>

activity-accomplishing and basic-space-of-phenomena primordial knowings,

<a id="u03308"></a>

with moving and nonmoving spheres,

<a id="u03309"></a>

abide as supports of completion in their own channels. [N-144](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144)

<a id="u03310"></a>

From the two eyes, where the refined parts gather,

<a id="u03311"></a>

through the distinction of methods and discerning knowing,

<a id="u03312"></a>

knowing how [things are] matures as the sphere [lamp] and

<a id="u03313"></a>

the lamp of pure basic space.

<a id="u03314"></a>

Knowing the multiplicity matures as the far-reaching-lasso [lamp] and

<a id="u03315"></a>

the discerning-knowing lamp.

<a id="u03316"></a>

Also concerning the karma of beings,

<a id="u03317"></a>

whatever the distinctions of their faculties,

<a id="u03318"></a>

through training in this, they become known. [N-144](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-144)

<a id="u03319"></a>

**[Source heading: Nineteenth reply.]**

<a id="u03320"></a>

The manner of abiding and the natural state have two aspects:

<a id="u03321"></a>

abiding in the initial Ground,

<a id="u03322"></a>

and abiding in the object of delusion.

<a id="u03323"></a>

The initial [abiding] is as essence, intrinsic nature,

<a id="u03324"></a>

and compassionate responsiveness—the three.

<a id="u03325"></a>

Furthermore, because essence abides as embodiment,

<a id="u03326"></a>

into portions of dharma, complete enjoyment, and emanation,

<a id="u03327"></a>

it is not divided: the manner of no division.

<a id="u03328"></a>

Even in what is accomplished from its own condition,

<a id="u03329"></a>

the embodiment's color and so forth are not objects of conceptual mind. [N-145](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145)

<a id="u03330"></a>

Intrinsic nature makes appearances arise in the manner of light:

<a id="u03331"></a>

white, red, yellow, green, and blue.

<a id="u03332"></a>

Not as characteristics with delineations,

<a id="u03333"></a>

without delineation, it is accomplished from the condition of what is to be known. [N-145](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145)

<a id="u03334"></a>

From compassionate responsiveness arising in manifold ways,

<a id="u03335"></a>

because it is not determined as one ‘like this,’

<a id="u03336"></a>

and manifold [appearances] arise, it is called the Ground.

<a id="u03337"></a>

In the object of delusion, leave [it] of itself,

<a id="u03338"></a>

and seal leaving just as it is with self-liberation. [N-145](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-145)

<a id="u03339"></a>

**[Source heading: Twentieth reply.]**

<a id="u03340"></a>

Enlightened intent has not moved from one.

<a id="u03341"></a>

Because appearance is clear right upon appearance,

<a id="u03342"></a>

as with a face and a mirror,

<a id="u03343"></a>

liberation—that and that itself are liberated. [N-146](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146)

<a id="u03344"></a>

Because moving knowing's object is empty,

<a id="u03345"></a>

seeing, knowing, and awareness are not different.

<a id="u03346"></a>

That itself is liberated right where it is.

<a id="u03347"></a>

Knowing one liberates all.

<a id="u03348"></a>

The numerical limits of two and three are exhausted.

<a id="u03349"></a>

No object of conceptualizing and holding appears.

<a id="u03350"></a>

Movement and holding are empty from the outset.

<a id="u03351"></a>

Because empty enlightened intent is clear and complete,

<a id="u03352"></a>

the numerical limits of the many are exhausted. [N-146](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-146)

<a id="u03353"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-first reply.]**

<a id="u03354"></a>

The crucial point of the all-basis and the dharma embodiment:

<a id="u03355"></a>

first, the all-basis will be explained.

<a id="u03356"></a>

It is the basis of all phenomena and nonphenomena.

<a id="u03357"></a>

Its essence, word explanation, and subdivisions are explained.

<a id="u03358"></a>

Its essence is the vessel of habitual tendencies.

<a id="u03359"></a>

Its subdivisions are object, application,

<a id="u03360"></a>

the manifold, and the primordial Ground. [N-147](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-147)

<a id="u03361"></a>

In the word explanation, ‘all’ means gathering;

<a id="u03362"></a>

‘basis’ means assembling and accumulating.

<a id="u03363"></a>

The dharma embodiment is free from conceptual elaborations.

<a id="u03364"></a>

There are no characteristics of arbitrary holding.

<a id="u03365"></a>

Its essence is empty and clear, indivisible.

<a id="u03366"></a>

Its subdivisions: dharma, complete enjoyment, and emanation embodiments.

<a id="u03367"></a>

Through this threefold division, distinctions apply.

<a id="u03368"></a>

In the word explanation, ‘dharma’ is the authentic path;

<a id="u03369"></a>

‘embodiment’ is what is accomplished from it.

<a id="u03370"></a>

Thus, through the crucial point of distinguishing,

<a id="u03371"></a>

time, method, and example,

<a id="u03372"></a>

and the measure of distinction, there is the result. [N-147](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-147)

<a id="u03373"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-second reply.]**

<a id="u03374"></a>

The crucial point of ordinary mind and primordial knowing:

<a id="u03375"></a>

what is called ordinary mind concerns delusive conceptual thought.

<a id="u03376"></a>

Its essence enters and pervades;

<a id="u03377"></a>

it is the Ground of mindfulness and reflection.

<a id="u03378"></a>

Its subdivisions are pure and impure.

<a id="u03379"></a>

Its word explanation: thought arises as what is recalled.

<a id="u03380"></a>

Furthermore, what one thinks about, and that by which one thinks,

<a id="u03381"></a>

what thinks, and why one thinks. [N-148](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-148)

<a id="u03382"></a>

Primordial knowing is not conceptual thought.

<a id="u03383"></a>

Its essence is self-clear: the core meaning.

<a id="u03384"></a>

Its subdivisions are held to be three.

<a id="u03385"></a>

Its word explanation means abiding from the outset.

<a id="u03386"></a>

Through knowing in this way, it is primordial knowing.

<a id="u03387"></a>

The meaning of distinguishing these is taught:

<a id="u03388"></a>

time, method, example, and measure;

<a id="u03389"></a>

the results of their respective enlightened intents;

<a id="u03390"></a>

and the characteristics of realizing primordial knowing.

<a id="u03391"></a>

Through body, speech, and ordinary mind, too,

<a id="u03392"></a>

signs, measures, and entry;

<a id="u03393"></a>

knowing the changes in characteristics;

<a id="u03394"></a>

and the distinction of complete evenness. [N-148](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-148)

<a id="u03395"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-third reply.]**

<a id="u03396"></a>

One wishing to distinguish the refined and turbid parts of the elements:

<a id="u03397"></a>

through the distinction of ultimate and relative,

<a id="u03398"></a>

the elements, too, are included in two.

<a id="u03399"></a>

Their maturation and activities are likewise twofold.

<a id="u03400"></a>

Earth's turbid part is coarse.

<a id="u03401"></a>

Its maturation is exceedingly fine particles.

<a id="u03402"></a>

Its activity is flesh and its illnesses.

<a id="u03403"></a>

The refined part completes the bodily constituents,

<a id="u03404"></a>

increases ultimate awakening ordinary mind, [N-149](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-149) [N-T82](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t82)

<a id="u03405"></a>

and matures as the far-reaching-lasso lamp.

<a id="u03406"></a>

Through familiarity with the crucial point, too, it is accomplished.

<a id="u03407"></a>

Water's turbid part moistens and gathers.

<a id="u03408"></a>

Its activity is blood and its illnesses.

<a id="u03409"></a>

Through maturation it gathers collections of water particles.

<a id="u03410"></a>

The refined part overcomes aging and death;

<a id="u03411"></a>

its activity stabilizes the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u03412"></a>

and it matures as the discerning-knowing lamp.

<a id="u03413"></a>

Through the crucial point, the yoga of food is accomplished.

<a id="u03414"></a>

Fire's turbid part burns and spreads.

<a id="u03415"></a>

Through maturation, there are sheep and ox particles. [N-149](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-149)

<a id="u03416"></a>

Its activity is warmth and its illnesses.

<a id="u03417"></a>

The refined part completes speech and body,

<a id="u03418"></a>

and matures as the sphere lamp.

<a id="u03419"></a>

Through cultivating the meditative stabilization of sleep,

<a id="u03420"></a>

the crucial point causes dreams to cease.

<a id="u03421"></a>

Wind's turbid part separates the refined parts and moves [them].

<a id="u03422"></a>

Through maturation, sun particles become complete. [N-149](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-149)

<a id="u03423"></a>

Its activity is breath and its illnesses.

<a id="u03424"></a>

The refined part gives ordinary mind ease and capacity.

<a id="u03425"></a>

Its activity makes the nature of phenomena clear

<a id="u03426"></a>

and distinguishes varieties of differentiating conceptualization.

<a id="u03427"></a>

Through maturation, from the lamp of basic space,

<a id="u03428"></a>

the crucial point uproots habitual tendencies.

<a id="u03429"></a>

Through time, method, crucial point, and word,

<a id="u03430"></a>

entering its intrinsic nature,

<a id="u03431"></a>

the pith instructions on the elements' crucial points are complete. [N-149](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-149)

<a id="u03432"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-fourth reply.]**

<a id="u03433"></a>

Packing the lamps is like this:

<a id="u03434"></a>

one with faith, free of a mental faculty [turned toward] samsara,

<a id="u03435"></a>

should offer to the guru and make a torma.

<a id="u03436"></a>

Having abandoned all connections with samsara,

<a id="u03437"></a>

in a secluded place or a charnel ground,

<a id="u03438"></a>

abandon companions and remain alone.

<a id="u03439"></a>

Not relying on the three [unspecified supports],

<a id="u03440"></a>

with one's own thumb and forefinger—[Editorial note: Do not infer or perform eye pressure from this draft.] [N-150](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-150)

<a id="u03441"></a>

one packs the lamp of the empty sphere.

<a id="u03442"></a>

Whatever arises from that packing

<a id="u03443"></a>

is the vision of the direct perception of the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u03444"></a>

From this, the experience of increased primordial knowing,

<a id="u03445"></a>

through colors and shapes,

<a id="u03446"></a>

clearly pervades both outer and inner.

<a id="u03447"></a>

It definitely increases further and further.

<a id="u03448"></a>

At this time, the signs of bliss in body and speech

<a id="u03449"></a>

are joined to the measures and stages of appearances.

<a id="u03450"></a>

From the appearance in which primordial knowing is complete,

<a id="u03451"></a>

and the appearance of the pure nature of phenomena,

<a id="u03452"></a>

when the three appearances are complete, there is exhaustion.

<a id="u03453"></a>

This is called the result. [N-150](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-150)

<a id="u03454"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-fifth reply.]**

<a id="u03455"></a>

The appearances of packing in this way:

<a id="u03456"></a>

first, pure red,

<a id="u03457"></a>

complete in power, round, and fully self-clear.

<a id="u03458"></a>

From two fingerbreadths, it becomes three. [N-151](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-151)

<a id="u03459"></a>

Then, through packing again and again,

<a id="u03460"></a>

the colors of the five primordial knowings—

<a id="u03461"></a>

white, red, green, yellow, and blue—

<a id="u03462"></a>

appear as the object; this involves effort.

<a id="u03463"></a>

Then, also on reaching freedom from effort,

<a id="u03464"></a>

through the portion of wind, they go and come.

<a id="u03465"></a>

From merely the form of a mirror,

<a id="u03466"></a>

they appear in portions of two and three. [N-151](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-151)

<a id="u03467"></a>

Through becoming continually familiar with this,

<a id="u03468"></a>

also through the five-colored light,

<a id="u03469"></a>

isolated patches appear, the size of a blanket;

<a id="u03470"></a>

the extent of seeing is the size of a region. [N-151](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-151)

<a id="u03471"></a>

Having found the measure of familiarity with this,

<a id="u03472"></a>

within the spheres, [appearances] include embodiments.

<a id="u03473"></a>

For a time they abide as half-embodiments.

<a id="u03474"></a>

Then, five and five are joined in pairs,

<a id="u03475"></a>

connected by exceedingly clear spheres.

<a id="u03476"></a>

There are appearances of father and mother embodiments.

<a id="u03477"></a>

Delusive appearances exhausted, there are fields.

<a id="u03478"></a>

At this time, the measures of body, speech, and mental faculty—

<a id="u03479"></a>

at the time of seeing the respective appearances,

<a id="u03480"></a>

through maturation in oneself, the signs are complete.

<a id="u03481"></a>

The measures of exhaustion appear manifestly.

<a id="u03482"></a>

Through greater or lesser diligence in this,

<a id="u03483"></a>

in days, months, and years,

<a id="u03484"></a>

accomplishment definitely occurs at each respective time.

<a id="u03485"></a>

Through times that do not change,

<a id="u03486"></a>

one reaches the respective measures. [N-151](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-151)

<a id="u03487"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-sixth reply.]**

<a id="u03488"></a>

The crucial points of the channels number two hundred.

<a id="u03489"></a>

Thoroughly condensing their crucial points:

<a id="u03490"></a>

one hundred and fifteen perform all activities.

<a id="u03491"></a>

The crucial point of the channels is thorough transformation.

<a id="u03492"></a>

Sixty-eight join with the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u03493"></a>

The channels of supremely great bliss,

<a id="u03494"></a>

through the Ground, reveal the appearances of beings. [N-152](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-152)[^G-U03494]

<a id="u03495"></a>

The place of the Ground and the path of going;

<a id="u03496"></a>

the door of maturation and the activities of agents;

<a id="u03497"></a>

time and its measures of appearance.

<a id="u03498"></a>

The pith instructions on their crucial points:

<a id="u03499"></a>

rely on abiding, coming, and the path of going.

<a id="u03500"></a>

Join the ground of maturation and the sphere. [N-152](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-152)

<a id="u03501"></a>

Whoever acts in this way—

<a id="u03502"></a>

differentiating conceptualization stops, and primordial knowing arises.

<a id="u03503"></a>

Grasp time through the measure of wind.

<a id="u03504"></a>

Through the number of movements, primordial knowing abides.

<a id="u03505"></a>

The skilled person should know and retain [this].

<a id="u03506"></a>

Through the numerical measure of wind's activities,

<a id="u03507"></a>

in freedom from error, [these] reveal the nature of phenomena

<a id="u03508"></a>

and the distinctions of impure activities.

<a id="u03509"></a>

The measures of appearance, too, are body, speech, and mental faculty,

<a id="u03510"></a>

because their respective times have arrived. [N-153](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-153)

<a id="u03511"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-seventh reply.]**

<a id="u03512"></a>

The training of ordinary mind is like this:

<a id="u03513"></a>

outer objects and the inner body;

<a id="u03514"></a>

between them, restraining body and ordinary mind;

<a id="u03515"></a>

acting through the activities of speech;

<a id="u03516"></a>

supremely, training ordinary mind with ordinary mind.

<a id="u03517"></a>

For objects, reflect on existence and nonexistence,

<a id="u03518"></a>

and the crucial point of going and coming.

<a id="u03519"></a>

For the body, through names and exceedingly fine particles,

<a id="u03520"></a>

analyze [it] into many distinctions.

<a id="u03521"></a>

Examine the interval in which body and ordinary mind are joined.

<a id="u03522"></a>

Through the particulars of verbal expression,

<a id="u03523"></a>

the respective winds, going and coming,

<a id="u03524"></a>

are accomplished through mixing the nonduality of times. [N-154](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-154)

<a id="u03525"></a>

Supremely, ordinary mind's going, coming, and abiding—

<a id="u03526"></a>

train through the absence of examination of their reasons. [N-154](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-154)

<a id="u03527"></a>

Also grasp the measure of proficiency in this way.

<a id="u03528"></a>

In this way, its characteristics are complete.

<a id="u03529"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-eighth reply.]**

<a id="u03530"></a>

Afflictions arise from their own conditions:

<a id="u03531"></a>

as when one fears one's own shadow,

<a id="u03532"></a>

one comes to see one's own intrinsic nature.

<a id="u03533"></a>

Also, from the numerical measures of wind,

<a id="u03534"></a>

one knows through the number one hundred and twenty.

<a id="u03535"></a>

Some rely on objects,

<a id="u03536"></a>

from which distinctions of true and false arise.

<a id="u03537"></a>

Some, from having no object of focus,

<a id="u03538"></a>

are to be known through mere neutrality.

<a id="u03539"></a>

Some desire samsara and nirvana as well;

<a id="u03540"></a>

from the two truths, two ordinary minds become deluded. [N-155](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-155)

<a id="u03541"></a>

From this, appearing conditions, afflictions, and so forth.

<a id="u03542"></a>

Furthermore, what is to be closely explained:

<a id="u03543"></a>

the intrinsic nature of delusive appearance

<a id="u03544"></a>

was not previously seen to be primordial knowing.

<a id="u03545"></a>

Because there is no Ground of delusion accompanied by conceptual thought,

<a id="u03546"></a>

the all-basis is realized as the great dharma embodiment.

<a id="u03547"></a>

The flow of delusion's own conceptual thought is cut.

<a id="u03548"></a>

Thus, undeluded appearance

<a id="u03549"></a>

arises from the crucial point of liberation from the outset. [N-155](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-155)

<a id="u03550"></a>

The aggregates of beings are the buddhas' embodiments:

<a id="u03551"></a>

who has not known them to be so from the outset?

<a id="u03552"></a>

As for the aggregates having the identity of the five buddhas,

<a id="u03553"></a>

[this] arises from the accomplishment of letters abiding in the channels.

<a id="u03554"></a>

From the bubble-like eyes, primordial knowing—

<a id="u03555"></a>

who has not seen it to be self-appearance?

<a id="u03556"></a>

From this, delusive appearance

<a id="u03557"></a>

arises from the crucial point of the lamps. [N-156](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-156)

<a id="u03558"></a>

Furthermore, because bodily conduct itself

<a id="u03559"></a>

is liberated as the three embodiments,

<a id="u03560"></a>

all actions and agents are the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u03561"></a>

Whatever is spoken through speech

<a id="u03562"></a>

is the secret recitation of Secret Mantra,

<a id="u03563"></a>

arising from the crucial point of the letters' magical displays.

<a id="u03564"></a>

All the conceptual thoughts of beings' ordinary minds—

<a id="u03565"></a>

why are they not known to be meditative stabilization?

<a id="u03566"></a>

Because one is liberated through realizing one's own ordinary mind,

<a id="u03567"></a>

this arises from the crucial point of the elements.

<a id="u03568"></a>

Thus, in the nature of phenomena of everything,

<a id="u03569"></a>

there is nothing whatsoever to be trained in,

<a id="u03570"></a>

and not the slightest thing to be placed.

<a id="u03571"></a>

It is the nature of phenomena with nothing above it.

<a id="u03572"></a>

From actions and agents, expression and reflection,

<a id="u03573"></a>

examine the crucial point of liberating samsara and nirvana.

<a id="u03574"></a>

There is not even one who is not a buddha.

<a id="u03575"></a>

The samsara of the three realms is emptied from its depths. [N-156](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-156)

<a id="u03576"></a>

Furthermore, all sentient beings of the three realms,

<a id="u03577"></a>

because they do not go beyond body, speech, and mental faculty,

<a id="u03578"></a>

need not seek the three embodiments elsewhere.

<a id="u03579"></a>

Even if sought, there is no place where they are found.

<a id="u03580"></a>

From what has not arisen and has no arising,

<a id="u03581"></a>

seeing through looking is greatly wondrous.

<a id="u03582"></a>

In the play of great wonder,

<a id="u03583"></a>

there is no division into buddhas and sentient beings.

<a id="u03584"></a>

Like clouds in space,

<a id="u03585"></a>

naturally arising, complete in themselves, they subside.

<a id="u03586"></a>

Through the distinction of directly perceiving the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u03587"></a>

because faculties are neither sharp nor dull,

<a id="u03588"></a>

all sentient beings do not abide

<a id="u03589"></a>

elsewhere than as buddhas.

<a id="u03590"></a>

Because those with bodies are pervaded by ordinary mind,

<a id="u03591"></a>

not a buddha—not a sentient being. [N-157](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-157)

<a id="u03592"></a>

Like a result from a seed,

<a id="u03593"></a>

the crucial point of the condition of one's own intrinsic nature,

<a id="u03594"></a>

not through words of expression,

<a id="u03595"></a>

but through clear appearance in the objects of the faculties,

<a id="u03596"></a>

is the identity of liberation through naked seeing. [N-157](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-157)

<a id="u03597"></a>

Furthermore, through conditions, among sentient beings,

<a id="u03598"></a>

there is not even one who is not a buddha.

<a id="u03599"></a>

Because self-appearance and primordial knowing accord as phenomena,

<a id="u03600"></a>

samsara has not existed from the outset.

<a id="u03601"></a>

Therefore, each is a buddha.

<a id="u03602"></a>

Birth itself is from conceptual thought.

<a id="u03603"></a>

Abiding in the womb is the basic space of phenomena.

<a id="u03604"></a>

Through body and ordinary mind gathering, basic space and awareness are connected.

<a id="u03605"></a>

Through abiding in a body, there are the three embodiments.

<a id="u03606"></a>

Through aging, phenomena are exhausted and delusive appearances stop.

<a id="u03607"></a>

Through illness, there is experiential acquaintance with phenomena.

<a id="u03608"></a>

Through death, there is emptiness without identification.

<a id="u03609"></a>

Therefore, sentient beings are buddhas. [N-157](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-157)

<a id="u03610"></a>

Furthermore, the crucial point of directly perceiving the nature of phenomena:

<a id="u03611"></a>

through seeing alone, there is knowing;

<a id="u03612"></a>

through knowing, there is realization;

<a id="u03613"></a>

through realization alone, one is liberated.

<a id="u03614"></a>

What is called liberated is not something unbound by an agent.

<a id="u03615"></a>

Just as space itself, into space,

<a id="u03616"></a>

dissolves, with intrinsic nature without an object of focus,

<a id="u03617"></a>

there is no partiality or portion.

<a id="u03618"></a>

For the fortunate who see directly,

<a id="u03619"></a>

because even the name ‘samsara of the three realms’ is absent,

<a id="u03620"></a>

the three existences are decisively resolved. [N-158](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-158)

<a id="u03621"></a>

Thus, from The Great All-Penetrating Word, the Root of All Phenomena,

<a id="u03622"></a>

this is the second chapter: ‘Array of Gathered Crucial Points: The Definite Arising of the Root of Awareness.’ [N-158](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-158)[^G-U03622][^G-C2-BOUNDARY-METADATA]

<a id="part-3"></a>
## Chapter 3

<a id="u03623"></a>

Then the lord of the gods, Delight-Maker,

<a id="u03624"></a>

concerning what is called ‘Array of Primordial Knowing,’

<a id="u03625"></a>

questioned the buddha, the Blessed One: [N-159](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-159)

<a id="u03626"></a>

‘How does the nature of phenomena abide?

<a id="u03627"></a>

How does delusion arise from it?

<a id="u03628"></a>

What are the causes and conditions of delusion?

<a id="u03629"></a>

Why are characteristics realized?

<a id="u03630"></a>

What causes sentient beings to differ?

<a id="u03631"></a>

What is the state in which intrinsic nature is complete?

<a id="u03632"></a>

What makes the characteristics of names?

<a id="u03633"></a>

How do karma and faculties accord?

<a id="u03634"></a>

For what purpose do words enter?

<a id="u03635"></a>

From what do conceptual mind and knowing arise?

<a id="u03636"></a>

Why are there an apprehended object and an apprehending subject?

<a id="u03637"></a>

Essence as primordial purity and primordial knowing,

<a id="u03638"></a>

and intrinsic nature as spontaneous presence—why are these so?

<a id="u03639"></a>

Through what does compassionate responsiveness pervade all?

<a id="u03640"></a>

Why is [primordial knowing] like a mirror?

<a id="u03641"></a>

What is intended by evenness?

<a id="u03642"></a>

What is the extent of discriminating [primordial knowing]?

<a id="u03643"></a>

How is activity accomplished?

<a id="u03644"></a>

How does one act within the basic space of phenomena?

<a id="u03645"></a>

What primordial knowing is gathered through knowing?

<a id="u03646"></a>

What pervades the multiplicity?

<a id="u03647"></a>

What is the enlightened intent of knowing how things are?

<a id="u03648"></a>

What single principle gathers the primordial knowings?

<a id="u03649"></a>

I did not previously know these.

<a id="u03650"></a>

Teacher of great compassionate responsiveness, speak.’ [N-159](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-159)

<a id="u03651"></a>

From the intervening space, without dividing space,

<a id="u03652"></a>

phrases made by no one,

<a id="u03653"></a>

intrinsically without any conceptual thought,

<a id="u03654"></a>

from words beyond expression, with reflection pure—[N-159](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-159)

<a id="u03655"></a>

these kings of phrases

<a id="u03656"></a>

arose as if spoken by the teacher.

<a id="u03657"></a>

**[Source annotation: The first reply is taught in ten subdivisions.]**

<a id="u03658"></a>

What is called the abiding of the nature of phenomena:

<a id="u03659"></a>

abiding in time, in intrinsic nature,

<a id="u03660"></a>

and through the definite measure of shifting;

<a id="u03661"></a>

abiding from the outset, through effort,

<a id="u03662"></a>

conditions, causes and conditions, and intervals. [N-160](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-160)

<a id="u03663"></a>

The nature of phenomena's abiding in time:

<a id="u03664"></a>

from the four parts—summer, winter, autumn, and spring—

<a id="u03665"></a>

at the beginning of autumn, aggregates,

<a id="u03666"></a>

elements, and primordial knowing increase.

<a id="u03667"></a>

At this time, profound dependent connections are taught.

<a id="u03668"></a>

It abides on the ninth and eighth of the waning moon,

<a id="u03669"></a>

and the fourteenth and [fifteenth] of the waxing moon. [N-160](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-160)

<a id="u03670"></a>

For days, half of twelve parts; [N-160](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-160)

<a id="u03671"></a>

also, among twelve times of the sun,

<a id="u03672"></a>

it abides in four aspects of a part. [N-160](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-160)

<a id="u03673"></a>

At this time, train in the nature of phenomena. [N-160](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-160)[^G-U03673]

<a id="u03674"></a>

As for abiding in intrinsic nature:

<a id="u03675"></a>

in outer objects and inner ordinary mind,

<a id="u03676"></a>

and in the breath between them, it abides.

<a id="u03677"></a>

This, too, the yogin should know.

<a id="u03678"></a>

Abiding in the definite measure of shifting:

<a id="u03679"></a>

through the winds of great movement,

<a id="u03680"></a>

in the measure of movement at each respective stage,

<a id="u03681"></a>

at each five hundred,

<a id="u03682"></a>

there is a shift in the abiding of the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u03683"></a>

Conceptual thought and holding are at the thousand-junctures. [N-160](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-160)

<a id="u03684"></a>

For the respective places where the six classes fall,

<a id="u03685"></a>

grasp the conjunctions between wind movements.

<a id="u03686"></a>

The method of making the nature of ordinary mind abide there:

<a id="u03687"></a>

reckoning from the time of five hundred,

<a id="u03688"></a>

strive at the point of applying the respective virtues.

<a id="u03689"></a>

This, too, changes into afflictions through conditions.

<a id="u03690"></a>

Grasping [this] from the respective intervals of wind,

<a id="u03691"></a>

the particulars of lifespan and karma, too,

<a id="u03692"></a>

are to be known through changes in wind. [N-160](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-160)

<a id="u03693"></a>

The nature of phenomena that abides from the outset:

<a id="u03694"></a>

at the initial time when there is no appearance,

<a id="u03695"></a>

intrinsic nature abides without contrivance.

<a id="u03696"></a>

Stainless ordinary mind, too, is like that.

<a id="u03697"></a>

Through effort, pressing the crucial point of direct perception,

<a id="u03698"></a>

one abides in the uncontrived Ground.

<a id="u03699"></a>

For abiding through conditions, through the elements'

<a id="u03700"></a>

bodily distinctions of purity,

<a id="u03701"></a>

and the distinction of impurity,

<a id="u03702"></a>

through the activity of primordial knowing's abiding,

<a id="u03703"></a>

the skilled person grasps the respective times.

<a id="u03704"></a>

Ordinary mind that abides through a cause,

<a id="u03705"></a>

because it pervades all, is in the condition of the nature of phenomena;

<a id="u03706"></a>

if uncontrived, it abides.

<a id="u03707"></a>

For abiding in an interval, draw through wind.

<a id="u03708"></a>

Know [this] within the interval of subtle movement.

<a id="u03709"></a>

This gathers the crucial points of primordial knowing. [N-161](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-161)

<a id="u03710"></a>

**[Source annotation: The second reply is taught in eight points.]**

<a id="u03711"></a>

From this, delusion: cause and conditions,

<a id="u03712"></a>

portions and branches, activity itself,

<a id="u03713"></a>

the immature, differentiating conceptualization, and samsara.

<a id="u03714"></a>

Ignorance has three aspects.

<a id="u03715"></a>

Through their single identity, it makes the root of delusion.

<a id="a2000-c03-s01"></a>

*Scan-restored main verses:*[^G-A2000-C03-S01]

Through co-emergent [ignorance], there are two conceptual thoughts;<br />
through thorough imputation, [it] becomes an object;<br />
as for conditions, through four aspects—

<a id="u03716"></a>

Through gathering and holding, it is called delusion.

<a id="u03717"></a>

As for portions, through formations and so forth,

<a id="u03718"></a>

subtract twelve from the portion of delusion's objects. [N-162](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-162)

<a id="u03719"></a>

Through maturation, there are years and activities.

<a id="u03720"></a>

The branches, from differing elaborations,

<a id="u03721"></a>

all, as distinctions of names,

<a id="u03722"></a>

become two aspects in the object of delusion.

<a id="u03723"></a>

Through activity, there are the desire and form realms,

<a id="u03724"></a>

and the formless realm: three are made.

<a id="u03725"></a>

The immature are the forms of the six classes;

<a id="u03726"></a>

names and particulars become distinct.

<a id="u03727"></a>

Differentiating conceptualization: dormant tendencies,

<a id="u03728"></a>

rising up everywhere, and the manner of pervasion.

<a id="u03729"></a>

What is called samsara is similarity,

<a id="u03730"></a>

and the activity of gathering—condensing and spreading,

<a id="u03731"></a>

completion and manifold distinctions.

<a id="u03732"></a>

Thus, apart from merely the name ‘delusion,’

<a id="u03733"></a>

through the signs of conceptualizing and holding as agents,

<a id="u03734"></a>

and the agency of assembling and condensing,

<a id="u03735"></a>

it is imputed through distinctions of names. [N-162](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-162)

<a id="u03736"></a>

**[Source annotation: The third reply is taught in nine points.]**

<a id="u03737"></a>

The cause of delusion is ignorance:

<a id="u03738"></a>

the Ground, knowing, contamination,

<a id="u03739"></a>

and the ways apprehended object and faculties circle. [N-163](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-163)

<a id="u03740"></a>

Conditions are from the object and the apprehended portion:

<a id="u03741"></a>

limits, the agents of limits,

<a id="u03742"></a>

not knowing one, and the support of focus.

<a id="u03743"></a>

The Ground does not differentiate anything,

<a id="u03744"></a>

and is merely without determination.

<a id="u03745"></a>

Knowing engages and vanishes,

<a id="u03746"></a>

and holds [what] spreads.

<a id="u03747"></a>

Contamination is holding as a stain:

<a id="u03748"></a>

one's own continuum is bound by what is to be known.

<a id="u03749"></a>

Even where there is no apprehended object,

<a id="u03750"></a>

[holding it as] true binds [one] tightly. [N-163](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-163)

<a id="u03751"></a>

The faculties, through portions of object and knowing,

<a id="u03752"></a>

appear in that object held as permanent.

<a id="u03753"></a>

Through knowing the manner of circling as one,

<a id="u03754"></a>

differentiating conceptualizations unite in pairs.

<a id="u03755"></a>

The agents of limits are activity and cause;

<a id="u03756"></a>

the elements become the faculties' activities.

<a id="u03757"></a>

Not knowing one: from primordial purity,

<a id="u03758"></a>

because the nature of phenomena's own identity is not known,

<a id="u03759"></a>

there is appropriation corresponding to the cause.

<a id="u03760"></a>

The support of focus, from colors,

<a id="u03761"></a>

through the moving support of two subtle portions,

<a id="u03762"></a>

becomes the object of focus of samsaric activity. [N-164](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-164)

<a id="u03763"></a>

**[Source annotation: The fourth reply is taught in eight points.]**

<a id="u03764"></a>

The particulars of realizing characteristics:

<a id="u03765"></a>

existence, nonexistence, appearance,

<a id="u03766"></a>

clarity, emptiness, the coarse, the apprehending subject,

<a id="u03767"></a>

consciousness, and what possesses form. [N-165](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-165)

<a id="u03768"></a>

What exists has the agency of an entity;

<a id="u03769"></a>

through entering appearance and characteristics, it pervades,

<a id="u03770"></a>

and closely reveals activities and particulars.

<a id="u03771"></a>

Through nonexistence, form and so forth are abandoned.

<a id="u03772"></a>

There is entry of mental faculty and knowing;

<a id="u03773"></a>

characteristics are never found.

<a id="u03774"></a>

Appearance is form and so forth.

<a id="u03775"></a>

It holds, spreads, and gathers,

<a id="u03776"></a>

with the intrinsic nature of paired entry and pervasion.

<a id="u03777"></a>

Clarity reveals its own identity

<a id="u03778"></a>

and is the reverse of nonappearance.

<a id="u03779"></a>

Emptiness is not birth;

<a id="u03780"></a>

it reveals radiance and absence of limits or center.

<a id="u03781"></a>

Because it has not arisen, it does not cease.

<a id="u03782"></a>

The coarse, from fine and coarse particles,

<a id="u03783"></a>

is complete as manifold forms;

<a id="u03784"></a>

it is tangible and appears as destructible.

<a id="u03785"></a>

The apprehending subject, toward the objects of the faculties,

<a id="u03786"></a>

binds through their respective apprehension,

<a id="u03787"></a>

and performs all activities of selfhood and afflictions.

<a id="u03788"></a>

Consciousness is a portion of conceptual thought:

<a id="u03789"></a>

it enters objects and engages with them,

<a id="u03790"></a>

and differentiating conceptualization concerns thought and activity.

<a id="u03791"></a>

What possesses form is aware [or] insentient,

<a id="u03792"></a>

gathering and arranging ordinary mind. [N-165](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-165)

<a id="u03793"></a>

**[Source annotation: The fifth reply is taught in eight points.]**

<a id="u03794"></a>

The causes of all sentient beings' differences:

<a id="u03795"></a>

elemental causes and activities of agency,

<a id="u03796"></a>

the faculties themselves, shapes and measures,

<a id="u03797"></a>

sounds and respective expressions,

<a id="u03798"></a>

and particulars of the vessels in which they abide.

<a id="u03799"></a>

The elemental causes, from four,

<a id="u03800"></a>

when examined, are sixty;

<a id="u03801"></a>

when differentiated, four hundred and four.

<a id="u03802"></a>

The activities of agency, from four,[^G-U03802]

<a id="u03803"></a>

when examined, become twelve. [N-166](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-166)[^G-U03803]

<a id="u03804"></a>

From these, the six classes differ. [N-166](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-166)

<a id="u03805"></a>

For faculties such as the eyes,

<a id="u03806"></a>

through differences in shapes and colors,

<a id="u03807"></a>

also through four hundred and four,

<a id="u03808"></a>

the bodies of beings differ.

<a id="u03809"></a>

Through bodily shape, body and limbs,

<a id="u03810"></a>

and their respective continents and faculties,

<a id="u03811"></a>

the forms of beings are differentiated.

<a id="u03812"></a>

Measures concern each of the six classes:

<a id="u03813"></a>

beginning, middle, and end,

<a id="u03814"></a>

distinguished by their respective merit and karma.

<a id="u03815"></a>

Sounds are long and short,

<a id="u03816"></a>

strong and weak, pressing and releasing,

<a id="u03817"></a>

subtle, seng [unresolved], and pointed. [N-166](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-166)

<a id="u03818"></a>

Entities, through earth, water, fire, and wind,

<a id="u03819"></a>

are examined through knowing itself, merit,

<a id="u03820"></a>

ascendancy, and bodily portions.

<a id="u03821"></a>

Expression: place, elements,

<a id="u03822"></a>

activity, and conceptual thought—their own crucial points.

<a id="u03823"></a>

The vessels of abiding are pure

<a id="u03824"></a>

or impure, distinguished by their inhabitants.

<a id="u03825"></a>

Therefore, through the elements' own crucial points,

<a id="u03826"></a>

the bodies of beings differ. [N-166](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-166)

<a id="u03827"></a>

**[Source annotation: The sixth reply is taught in eight points.]**

<a id="u03828"></a>

The state in which intrinsic nature is complete:

<a id="u03829"></a>

the nature of phenomena, basic space, and own identity;

<a id="u03830"></a>

movement empty and conceptual thought exhausted;

<a id="u03831"></a>

completion in the Ground, path, and result.

<a id="u03832"></a>

The nature of phenomena cannot be contrived by anyone.

<a id="u03833"></a>

Intrinsic nature is pure, free from conceptual elaborations,

<a id="u03834"></a>

beyond the object of distinct differentiating conceptualizations.

<a id="u03835"></a>

The flow of delusion arising through words is cut. [N-167](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-167)

<a id="u03836"></a>

Basic space has outer and inner aspects.

<a id="u03837"></a>

Outer: hold [the focus] in cloud-free space.

<a id="u03838"></a>

When fine dust has been cleared by rain,

<a id="u03839"></a>

the skilled person presses the crucial point of direct perception,

<a id="u03840"></a>

and takes the inner lamps as the path.

<a id="u03841"></a>

Own identity: awareness as vajra chains,

<a id="u03842"></a>

separating going and coming, in space. [N-167](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-167)

<a id="u03843"></a>

Movement: cut the activity of the two winds.

<a id="u03844"></a>

Distinguish through the ninth wind;

<a id="u03845"></a>

distinguish entry into abiding through methods.

<a id="u03846"></a>

The example: through the winds being empty,

<a id="u03847"></a>

all appearances become exhausted.

<a id="u03848"></a>

Likewise, the exhaustion of differentiating conceptualization

<a id="u03849"></a>

and unborn primordial knowing are simultaneous.

<a id="u03850"></a>

At this time, samsara and nirvana mix.

<a id="u03851"></a>

This, too, is the crucial point of completion in the Ground. [N-167](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-167)[^G-U03851]

<a id="u03852"></a>

Uncontrived intrinsic nature appears manifestly.

<a id="u03853"></a>

The path: through connecting with the lamps,

<a id="u03854"></a>

it appears in direct perception itself.

<a id="u03855"></a>

The result cannot be turned back anywhere.

<a id="u03856"></a>

It transcends objects of desire, hope, and fear.

<a id="u03857"></a>

Self-appearance is complete as pure from the outset;

<a id="u03858"></a>

knowing, holding, and conceptual thought are all exhausted. [N-167](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-167)

<a id="u03859"></a>

**[Source annotation: The seventh reply is taught in eight points.]**

<a id="u03860"></a>

What makes the characteristics of names:

<a id="u03861"></a>

cause, conditions, imputation,

<a id="u03862"></a>

limits, the first, transformation of the last,

<a id="u03863"></a>

not finding through examination, and the basis of convention. [N-168](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-168)

<a id="u03864"></a>

The cause of a name is a verbal symbol.

<a id="u03865"></a>

Because characteristics are not established anywhere,

<a id="u03866"></a>

what merely appears cannot be designated. [N-168](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-168)

<a id="u03867"></a>

The conditions are the objects directly before the faculties.

<a id="u03868"></a>

In their respective appearances accompanied by clinging,

<a id="u03869"></a>

the gathering of the all-basis arises as aspects of appearance.

<a id="u03870"></a>

Through imputation, from symbols and agents of transformation,

<a id="u03871"></a>

characteristics are closely shown.

<a id="u03872"></a>

In particular, the crucial point of what is transformed by a name:

<a id="u03873"></a>

joining [it] with each respective occasion,

<a id="u03874"></a>

even the Blessed One will be destroyed. [N-168](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-168)

<a id="u03875"></a>

Limits are distinguished through the elements' crucial points.

<a id="u03876"></a>

Killing, restoring, and destroying activities

<a id="u03877"></a>

are performed through names and dependent connections.

<a id="u03878"></a>

First, the letters of the imputed name:

<a id="u03879"></a>

through relying on their first [letters],

<a id="u03880"></a>

killing, restoring, and abiding itself—

<a id="u03881"></a>

know these through their respective measures.

<a id="u03882"></a>

For the end of the name transformed last,

<a id="u03883"></a>

reverse and place [it], joining elements and body.

<a id="u03884"></a>

In the authentic nature of phenomena, free from conceptual thought,

<a id="u03885"></a>

having thoroughly distinguished the characteristics of names,

<a id="u03886"></a>

through thorough examination of outer and inner,

<a id="u03887"></a>

because characteristics are not found through names,

<a id="u03888"></a>

one wishes to complete one's own rite. [N-168](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-168)

<a id="u03889"></a>

Conventions are the limits of words.

<a id="u03890"></a>

Distinguish them everywhere through the extremes of existence and nonexistence.

<a id="u03891"></a>

Through examining and analyzing body and ordinary mind, the two,

<a id="u03892"></a>

the basis of convention, too, is empty.

<a id="u03893"></a>

The Ground is neither one nor two.

<a id="u03894"></a>

Imputed emptiness: the extremes of existence and nonexistence stop.

<a id="u03895"></a>

No one finds what is made or what has arisen.

<a id="u03896"></a>

Likewise, there is no abiding, going, or coming.

<a id="u03897"></a>

Therefore, the nature of phenomena is empty. [N-168](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-168)

<a id="u03898"></a>

**[Source annotation: The eighth reply is taught in seven points.]**

<a id="u03899"></a>

Sentient beings' karma and faculties:

<a id="u03900"></a>

wind, knowing, the outer vessel,

<a id="u03901"></a>

the elements in general, and the manner of pervading;

<a id="u03902"></a>

three, nine, and twenty-one. [N-169](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-169)

<a id="u03903"></a>

Sentient beings' karma, too, is impelled

<a id="u03904"></a>

into the elemental projection of wind.

<a id="u03905"></a>

From this, through karmic particulars,

<a id="u03906"></a>

one enters and thoroughly distinguishes through methods.

<a id="u03907"></a>

The knowing that enacts this,

<a id="u03908"></a>

like the intrinsic nature of clouds in space

<a id="u03909"></a>

being scattered by wind,

<a id="u03910"></a>

reveals karmic appearances piece by piece.

<a id="u03911"></a>

From this, through maturing into body, speech, and mental faculty,

<a id="u03912"></a>

the forms of karma are revealed.

<a id="u03913"></a>

Through the elements—earth, water, fire, and wind—

<a id="u03914"></a>

completion as a body is wind's activity.

<a id="u03915"></a>

Pervading: all sentient beings,

<a id="u03916"></a>

because they do not go beyond a body of four elements,

<a id="u03917"></a>

are in their respective combinations of karma.

<a id="u03918"></a>

Manner: being established as form,

<a id="u03919"></a>

from the earlier and later aspects of its karma,

<a id="u03920"></a>

each takes up and experiences [them]. [N-169](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-169)

<a id="u03921"></a>

The three faculties are superior, middling, and inferior.

<a id="u03922"></a>

Through these, the three vehicles are free from perception. [N-169](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-169)

<a id="u03923"></a>

Thoroughly dividing each into three,

<a id="u03924"></a>

there are nine: the faculties' crucial point of the nine vehicles.

<a id="u03925"></a>

From these, thoroughly dividing into two,

<a id="u03926"></a>

there are two sets of nine: aspects of appearance.

<a id="u03927"></a>

Placing three in the Ground, through twenty-one,

<a id="u03928"></a>

the twenty-one appearances are complete. [N-169](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-169)

<a id="u03929"></a>

**[Source annotation: The ninth reply is taught in six inner points.]**

<a id="u03930"></a>

The entry of words is like this:

<a id="u03931"></a>

Brahmā, the All-Pervader, and the Hundred-Giver;

<a id="u03932"></a>

the Sky-Soarer, the Powerful Lord, and the Fierce One;

<a id="u03933"></a>

their respective sounds and the voices of gods.

<a id="u03934"></a>

By reckoning other places, one enters words. [N-170](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)

<a id="u03935"></a>

For Brahmā, the first verse,

<a id="u03936"></a>

half complete, becomes eight other places.

<a id="u03937"></a>

For the Powerful Lord, nine verses;

<a id="u03938"></a>

two halves of their places result.

<a id="u03939"></a>

For the All-Pervader, in two thousand,

<a id="u03940"></a>

through mastery of the places, one is complete. [N-170](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)

<a id="u03941"></a>

For the Hundred-Giver, five verses;

<a id="u03942"></a>

through reversing sound, three and two are complete.

<a id="u03943"></a>

For the Sky-Soarer, through six verses,

<a id="u03944"></a>

distinguishing signs: seven and two.

<a id="u03945"></a>

For the Fierce One, entry is in three and two. [N-170](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)[^G-U03945]

<a id="u03946"></a>

The activity of arranging signs: seven and four. [N-170](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)[^G-U03946]

<a id="u03947"></a>

Their respective sounds, from abiding,

<a id="u03948"></a>

entering, arising, and reversing,

<a id="u03949"></a>

relying on time, are complete with five.

<a id="u03950"></a>

The voices of gods number six thousand.

<a id="u03951"></a>

Adorning them with branches doubles that.

<a id="u03952"></a>

From the root foundation of other places— [N-170](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)[^G-U03952]

<a id="u03953"></a>

examining words: one hundred and two places. [N-170](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)[^G-U03953]

<a id="u03954"></a>

Extending from that: four hundred and four. [N-170](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)[^G-U03954]

<a id="u03955"></a>

The calculation of definite particulars becomes ten. [N-170](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)[^G-U03955]

<a id="u03956"></a>

By knowing how to combine these thoroughly,

<a id="u03957"></a>

one gains mastery through verses and becomes a king of speech.

<a id="u03958"></a>

Through this, the supreme combination is definitely accomplished. [N-170](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-170)

<a id="u03959"></a>

**[Source annotation: The tenth reply is taught in nine inner points.]**

<a id="u03960"></a>

What are called conceptual mind and knowing:

<a id="u03961"></a>

ordinary mind, mental faculty, movement,

<a id="u03962"></a>

proliferation, agitation, rigidity,

<a id="u03963"></a>

object, apprehending subject, and clinging.

<a id="u03964"></a>

Ordinary mind: the pure and impure

<a id="u03965"></a>

three realms, too, enter buddhahood. [N-171](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171)

<a id="u03966"></a>

Mental faculty is known through essence, word explanation,

<a id="u03967"></a>

and subdivisions.

<a id="u03968"></a>

Movement: portions of mindfulness and appearance,

<a id="u03969"></a>

through examination, become conceptual mind.

<a id="u03970"></a>

Proliferation, from the culmination of activities toward objects,

<a id="u03971"></a>

appears as the expressiveness of conceptual mind and knowing.

<a id="u03972"></a>

Agitation is the activity enacted by wind;

<a id="u03973"></a>

formations are a symbol of conceptual mind and knowing.

<a id="u03974"></a>

Rigidity, from three afflictions,

<a id="u03975"></a>

changes into what is called conceptual mind and is cut by knowing.

<a id="u03976"></a>

From the two gathering, three [kinds of] knowing arise. [N-171](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171)[^G-U03976]

<a id="u03977"></a>

Objects: from the apparent aspects of the five doors,

<a id="u03978"></a>

the entry portions of conceptual mind and knowing arise.

<a id="u03979"></a>

The apprehending subject: through eyes and so forth,

<a id="u03980"></a>

conceptual mind and knowing are actual in each respective [object].

<a id="u03981"></a>

Clinging: conceptual mind has four measures of realization;

<a id="u03982"></a>

through the measures of knowing, these become eight.

<a id="u03983"></a>

Arising from this, there are holding and attachment. [N-171](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-171)

<a id="u03984"></a>

**[Source heading: Eleventh reply.]**

<a id="u03985"></a>

Thus, from the agency of apparent aspects,

<a id="u03986"></a>

apprehended object and apprehending subject arise as two.

<a id="u03987"></a>

The apprehended object is the object; the apprehending subject is ordinary mind.

<a id="u03988"></a>

Object and ordinary mind gathered together are the three poisons.

<a id="u03989"></a>

The object's crucial point is in the faculties; the faculties' crucial point is ordinary mind.

<a id="u03990"></a>

Ordinary mind's crucial point is in the eyes; the eyes' crucial point is the channels.

<a id="u03991"></a>

Therefore, through bodily posture,

<a id="u03992"></a>

the connection of habitual tendencies with the body is cut,

<a id="u03993"></a>

and the buddhas' enlightened intent is complete.

<a id="u03994"></a>

The channels, through the wheel of dependent connections,

<a id="u03995"></a>

are the place of seeking, holding, and increasing—

<a id="u03996"></a>

the yogin's branches. [N-172](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-172)

<a id="u03997"></a>

**[Source heading: Twelfth reply.]**

<a id="u03998"></a>

From essence as primordial purity and primordial knowing,

<a id="u03999"></a>

there is no possible name ‘ignorance.’

<a id="u04000"></a>

There is no count of one and two. [N-173](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-173)

<a id="u04001"></a>

On examination, neither existence nor nonexistence is established.

<a id="u04002"></a>

In the nature of phenomena, undivided anywhere,

<a id="u04003"></a>

not even a mere primordial knowing is established.

<a id="u04004"></a>

Without phrases, nothing is established through expression. [N-174](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-174)

<a id="u04005"></a>

Pure self-awareness does not abide at an extreme;

<a id="u04006"></a>

the limits of the names ‘apprehended object and apprehending subject’ are exhausted.

<a id="u04007"></a>

There is no cause, nor any number of conditions;

<a id="u04008"></a>

there is no dual appearance, object, or possessor of an object.

<a id="u04009"></a>

No characteristic is differentiated anywhere;

<a id="u04010"></a>

coarse objects and conditions are self-exhausted.

<a id="u04011"></a>

Absent from the beginning, pure through purity,

<a id="u04012"></a>

with deluded conceptual thought ceased, it does not enact anything.

<a id="u04013"></a>

Since it has not arisen, cessation is empty. [N-174](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-174)

<a id="u04014"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirteenth reply.]**

<a id="u04015"></a>

What is called primordial knowing of intrinsic nature's spontaneous presence

<a id="u04016"></a>

does not arise, does not cease, and directs enlightened intent toward nothing,

<a id="u04017"></a>

because no object is fixed. [N-175](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175)

<a id="u04018"></a>

Since expressiveness and qualities do not cease,

<a id="u04019"></a>

it appears merely as play:

<a id="u04020"></a>

the foundation in which diversity is complete. [N-175](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175)

<a id="u04021"></a>

Because it does not exist, it appears; because it appears, it is empty.

<a id="u04022"></a>

It has the subsidiary aspects of appearance and emptiness entering together;

<a id="u04023"></a>

it is the pure domain of buddhas and sentient beings.

<a id="u04024"></a>

Thus it manifestly abides as what is called ‘the Ground.’

<a id="u04025"></a>

Abiding merely as intrinsic nature,

<a id="u04026"></a>

it is empty when examined from the aspect of essence.

<a id="u04027"></a>

Because its subsidiary aspects unfold, knowing is complete.

<a id="u04028"></a>

Since it abides of itself in spontaneous presence,

<a id="u04029"></a>

rejecting and accepting, the two, are absent here. [N-175](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-175)

<a id="u04030"></a>

**[Source heading: Fourteenth reply.]**

<a id="u04031"></a>

From primordial knowing of all-pervading compassionate responsiveness,

<a id="u04032"></a>

the inexhaustible gates of diverse arising

<a id="u04033"></a>

are complete in essence, though appearing to be exhausted. [N-176](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-176)

<a id="u04034"></a>

From the empty intrinsic nature of the dharma embodiment,

<a id="u04035"></a>

the aspect of primordial knowing whose knowing is complete

<a id="u04036"></a>

arises for sentient beings through awakened mind.

<a id="u04037"></a>

Without that, the central connection of samsara and nirvana would be severed;

<a id="u04038"></a>

through knowing, there is awareness and clarity. [N-176](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-176)

<a id="u04039"></a>

From the very identity of clear self-awareness,

<a id="u04040"></a>

through the force of intrinsic nature, compassionate responsiveness itself

<a id="u04041"></a>

has not ceased and does not cease.

<a id="u04042"></a>

From the pure aspect of the elements,

<a id="u04043"></a>

the one that is not an activity is complete. [N-176](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-176)

<a id="u04044"></a>

Like light from the sun,

<a id="u04045"></a>

it is inherent and unobscured.

<a id="u04046"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifteenth reply.]**

<a id="u04047"></a>

Then I shall explain the characteristic-holding

<a id="u04048"></a>

primordial knowing of the complete enjoyment embodiment.

<a id="u04049"></a>

Mirror-like primordial knowing makes forms clear;

<a id="u04050"></a>

the appearance aspects of shape and color are complete. [N-177](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

<a id="u04051"></a>

It is white because stains are purified.

<a id="u04052"></a>

Appearing inherently, it joins samsara and nirvana.

<a id="u04053"></a>

Because it sees the forms of all phenomena,

<a id="u04054"></a>

it is called ‘primordial knowing through itself.’ [N-177](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

<a id="u04055"></a>

Clear and empty, it is liberated into emptiness;

<a id="u04056"></a>

conceptual thought at the limits of liberation and grasping is exhausted.

<a id="u04057"></a>

With no other, the Ground of liberation is complete.

<a id="u04058"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixteenth reply.]**

<a id="u04059"></a>

What is called ‘evenness’

<a id="u04060"></a>

is through two causes of evenness, three conditions of evenness,

<a id="u04061"></a>

and the time and measure of evenness.

<a id="u04062"></a>

Here there is no duality, and it is free from awareness. [N-177](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

<a id="u04063"></a>

Neither divided nor dividing, it abides of itself.

<a id="u04064"></a>

What is called ‘even’ is, in the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u04065"></a>

freedom from the elaborations of pain.

<a id="u04066"></a>

‘-ness’ means there is no contrivance or accomplishment through effort.

<a id="u04067"></a>

Intrinsic nature abides; essence is empty.

<a id="u04068"></a>

In the nature of ordinary mind, all conceptual thought is exhausted.

<a id="u04069"></a>

What is called ‘primordial knowing’ abides;

<a id="u04070"></a>

through realizing its characteristics,

<a id="u04071"></a>

one does not abide in either samsara or nirvana. [N-177](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

<a id="u04072"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventeenth reply.]**

<a id="u04073"></a>

Discriminating [primordial knowing]: the types of faculty,

<a id="u04074"></a>

whatever appears to them, and the phenomena of that appearance

<a id="u04075"></a>

are each clear in sequence. [N-177](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

<a id="u04076"></a>

When the self-appearance of examination is purified,

<a id="u04077"></a>

awareness itself becomes the object.

<a id="u04078"></a>

What is called ‘individually’ arises from distinctions

<a id="u04079"></a>

in the manner of an antidote.

<a id="u04080"></a>

What is called ‘realization’ is seeing the characteristics,

<a id="u04081"></a>

together with the increase of self-appearance.

<a id="u04082"></a>

Primordial knowing arises at the beginning;

<a id="u04083"></a>

through knowing, the faults of afflictions are purified. [N-177](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

<a id="u04084"></a>

**[Source heading: Eighteenth reply.]**

<a id="u04085"></a>

What is called ‘doing accomplished’:

<a id="u04086"></a>

when striving and effort have self-ceased,

<a id="u04087"></a>

all phenomena rest of themselves and are self-liberated.

<a id="u04088"></a>

From ground-appearance in which self-liberation is complete,

<a id="u04089"></a>

the subtle aspect attains freedom from particles and stains.

<a id="u04090"></a>

Beyond the object of conceptual thought and examination,

<a id="u04091"></a>

it is the nature of phenomena, divided by no one.

<a id="u04092"></a>

Intrinsic nature being complete, the limits of appearance are exhausted.

<a id="u04093"></a>

Simultaneous realization is ‘doing.’

<a id="u04094"></a>

Through being ‘accomplished,’ desire is absent;

<a id="u04095"></a>

there is neither repetition nor reversal.

<a id="u04096"></a>

‘Primordial’ has the meaning of abiding;

<a id="u04097"></a>

through ‘knowing,’ it becomes manifest,

<a id="u04098"></a>

and one reaches the place where phenomena are exhausted. [N-177](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

<a id="u04099"></a>

**[Source heading: Nineteenth reply.]**

<a id="u04100"></a>

The basic space of phenomena is vast;

<a id="u04101"></a>

no edge or center is an object of focus.

<a id="u04102"></a>

In the pure nature of phenomena, the Ground of liberation,

<a id="u04103"></a>

‘phenomena’ means engaging in activity:

<a id="u04104"></a>

doing the doings of the transcendent and the world.

<a id="u04105"></a>

Intrinsic nature is clear and limpid.

<a id="u04106"></a>

‘Basic space’ opens room;

<a id="u04107"></a>

it is great naturally arisen abiding.

<a id="u04108"></a>

‘Primordial’ is the very identity of abiding,

<a id="u04109"></a>

uncontrived from the beginning.

<a id="u04110"></a>

Through ‘knowing,’ one is liberated from both samsara and nirvana.

<a id="u04111"></a>

This is enlightened intent in which self-appearance is complete. [N-177](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-177)

<a id="u04112"></a>

**[Source heading: Twentieth reply.]**

<a id="u04113"></a>

Primordial knowing encompassed by knowing

<a id="u04114"></a>

pervades all buddhas and sentient beings

<a id="u04115"></a>

indivisibly, without intrinsic nature.

<a id="u04116"></a>

In buddhas it is completely pure;

<a id="u04117"></a>

it abides unstained and free from particles.

<a id="u04118"></a>

In the appearance aspect of sentient beings of the six classes,

<a id="u04119"></a>

it abides in each one's continuum:

<a id="u04120"></a>

in gods, it is complete in its own clarity;

<a id="u04121"></a>

in asuras, subtle and flashing;

<a id="u04122"></a>

in humans, round and clear of itself;

<a id="u04123"></a>

in animals, clear within;

<a id="u04124"></a>

in hungry ghosts, subtle;

<a id="u04125"></a>

in hell, complete as self-appearance. [N-178](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178)

<a id="u04126"></a>

Through ‘knowing,’ there is awareness, clear in its own place.

<a id="u04127"></a>

Through ‘encompassed,’ essence is self-awareness.

<a id="u04128"></a>

‘Primordial’ is the clear aspect of knowing and awareness;

<a id="u04129"></a>

‘knowing’ is self-liberation, a single mandala. [N-178](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178)

<a id="u04130"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-first reply.]**

<a id="u04131"></a>

Primordial knowing encompassed by objects of knowledge

<a id="u04132"></a>

is to be known in two aspects.

<a id="u04133"></a>

Of these, I shall explain objects of knowledge in their multiplicity.

<a id="u04134"></a>

Through this, the intentions of those to be trained are known.

<a id="u04135"></a>

Through understanding others' benefit,

<a id="u04136"></a>

one knows the intrinsic nature of phenomena.

<a id="u04137"></a>

‘Whatever’ refers to the natural state;

<a id="u04138"></a>

‘multiplicity’ completes everything without remainder.

<a id="u04139"></a>

‘Primordial’ concerns the benefit of all sentient beings;

<a id="u04140"></a>

through ‘knowing,’ the three realms are drawn out from their depths. [N-178](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178)

<a id="u04141"></a>

The knowing aspect of the emanation embodiment

<a id="u04142"></a>

is complete in the identity of self-appearance.

<a id="u04143"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-second reply.]**

<a id="u04144"></a>

I shall explain the primordial knowing that knows how [things are].

<a id="u04145"></a>

The appearance aspect is free from a basis for expression;

<a id="u04146"></a>

the reflection of pure self-appearance is clear.

<a id="u04147"></a>

Through knowing the natural state of things,

<a id="u04148"></a>

and realizing one's own benefit, the flow of delusion is exhausted.

<a id="u04149"></a>

‘How’ refers to the natural state;

<a id="u04150"></a>

‘view’ engages without distraction. [N-178](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178)

<a id="u04151"></a>

‘Knowing’ is complete because it does not cease.

<a id="u04152"></a>

‘Primordial’ has become the path;

<a id="u04153"></a>

‘knowing’ has reached its measure.

<a id="u04154"></a>

There is no movement from the Ground

<a id="u04155"></a>

of great uncontrived self-liberation. [N-178](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-178)

<a id="u04156"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-third reply.]**

<a id="u04157"></a>

The gathering of primordial knowings

<a id="u04158"></a>

has pervasive and clear characteristics;

<a id="u04159"></a>

intrinsic nature is clarity-emptiness, indivisible,

<a id="u04160"></a>

and essence is not differentiated anywhere.

<a id="u04161"></a>

Gathered into four divisions,

<a id="u04162"></a>

it shows distinctions in the methods by which conceptual thought enters.

<a id="u04163"></a>

With Ground, core, and flowers,

<a id="u04164"></a>

it is beautiful and truly complete. [N-179](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179)

<a id="u04165"></a>

Through the manner of pervasion and that which pervades,

<a id="u04166"></a>

it shows self-appearance in the Ground

<a id="u04167"></a>

and exhausts differentiating conceptualization.

<a id="u04168"></a>

Distinguishing the gathered special features,

<a id="u04169"></a>

from the self-radiance of the nature of phenomena, free from conceptual thought,

<a id="u04170"></a>

there arises an appearance of primordial knowing without an object of focus.

<a id="u04171"></a>

There the yogin's ordinary mind is complete.

<a id="u04172"></a>

As a subsidiary aspect of the true gathering,

<a id="u04173"></a>

the crucial point of primordial knowing arises from the eyes.

<a id="u04174"></a>

Through knowing the eyes' gaze,

<a id="u04175"></a>

one engages together with the basic space of phenomena.

<a id="u04176"></a>

In order to exhaust the three realms,

<a id="u04177"></a>

the pulsating channel behind the eyes

<a id="u04178"></a>

is turned upward, bringing down primordial knowing. [N-179](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179)

<a id="u04179"></a>

Whatever primordial knowing arises from the crucial point,

<a id="u04180"></a>

just as the nature of phenomena endowed with awareness,

<a id="u04181"></a>

is completely liberated from all conceptual thought:

<a id="u04182"></a>

it is primordial knowing arising from basic space.

<a id="u04183"></a>

The crucial points of the eyes are above and below;

<a id="u04184"></a>

whichever avenue of effort the adept employs,

<a id="u04185"></a>

there is the appearance of all-gathering primordial knowing. [N-179](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179)

<a id="u04186"></a>

Alternatively, in the primordial knowing of the eyes,

<a id="u04187"></a>

through clearly distinguishing the five colors,

<a id="u04188"></a>

there is a likeness of the form of a complete mandala.

<a id="u04189"></a>

The experiential acquaintance arising from this

<a id="u04190"></a>

cannot be expressed by anyone—

<a id="u04191"></a>

Ema! Such great wonder!—

<a id="u04192"></a>

an appearance complete in qualities.

<a id="u04193"></a>

From pervasive primordial knowing, subtle in aspect,

<a id="u04194"></a>

it is through direct perception and on the path. [N-179](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-179)

<a id="u04195"></a>

Furthermore, this is to be explained:

<a id="u04196"></a>

from mindfulness and reflection in which differentiating conceptualization gathers,

<a id="u04197"></a>

even the dharma embodiment becomes form. [N-180](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180)

<a id="u04198"></a>

One who wishes to go beyond the three realms,

<a id="u04199"></a>

through the great crucial point of sacred primordial knowing,

<a id="u04200"></a>

examines the respective winds as breaths.

<a id="u04201"></a>

From the self-purification of stains in all phenomena,

<a id="u04202"></a>

the three embodiments are complete in the center of the body.

<a id="u04203"></a>

Why is there conceptual thought concerning this?

<a id="u04204"></a>

From the intrinsic nature of gathered mindfulness and reflection,

<a id="u04205"></a>

even the dharma embodiment becomes samsara. [N-180](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180)

<a id="u04206"></a>

When the limits of samsara are exhausted,

<a id="u04207"></a>

one is liberated in one's own place without anything being done.

<a id="u04208"></a>

In this liberation there is nothing at all.

<a id="u04209"></a>

Because there is nothing, the nature of phenomena is not a thing.

<a id="u04210"></a>

Primordial knowing being complete, the flow of delusion is cut;

<a id="u04211"></a>

being cut, going and coming are exhausted.

<a id="u04212"></a>

Because they are exhausted, it is empty primordial knowing.

<a id="u04213"></a>

Since the primordial knowing of the three embodiments has not ceased,

<a id="u04214"></a>

the existence of the three realms is empty.

<a id="u04215"></a>

By finding going and arising,

<a id="u04216"></a>

things being pure, form is exhausted. [N-180](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-180)

<a id="u04217"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the primordial knowing of appearance.

<a id="u04218"></a>

From earth, water, fire, wind, and space,

<a id="u04219"></a>

through the wonder of primordial knowing's appearance,

<a id="u04220"></a>

the blessed protector is without awareness. [N-181](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181)

<a id="u04221"></a>

Primordial knowing, not found on examination,

<a id="u04222"></a>

decisively resolves the three realms

<a id="u04223"></a>

and cuts the flow of clinging at the five gates.

<a id="u04224"></a>

The moon, a jewel, ‘mar gar,’ and light—

<a id="u04225"></a>

one who wishes to hold these

<a id="u04226"></a>

holds the appearance of embodiment arising from holding. [N-181](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181)

<a id="u04227"></a>

Then, through knowing the crucial point

<a id="u04228"></a>

of the Great Perfection, primordial knowing

<a id="u04229"></a>

is free from doing, just like space.

<a id="u04230"></a>

Through complete realization, it is free from blue. [N-181](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181)

<a id="u04231"></a>

Wondrous, marvelous primordial knowing

<a id="u04232"></a>

was absent before, is absent afterward, and absent from the beginning.

<a id="u04233"></a>

Now it is beyond the object of differentiating conceptualization:

<a id="u04234"></a>

an empty intrinsic nature free from extremes.

<a id="u04235"></a>

Beyond phrases, free from the conceptual mind, empty in essence,

<a id="u04236"></a>

its intrinsic nature not differentiated anywhere,

<a id="u04237"></a>

the deeds of compassionate responsiveness do not appear. [N-181](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181)

<a id="u04238"></a>

From the third appearance of primordial knowing,

<a id="u04239"></a>

discerning knowing arising from the mental faculty is held as an aspect of delusion. [N-181](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-181)

<a id="u04240"></a>

Here there is no limit of differentiated focus.

<a id="u04241"></a>

From the marvelous play of absence,

<a id="u04242"></a>

the result of the buddhas is found.

<a id="u04243"></a>

Outside and inside are all the basic space of phenomena;

<a id="u04244"></a>

here nothing whatsoever appears.

<a id="u04245"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the primordial knowing of objects.

<a id="u04246"></a>

Through distinctions of form and sound,

<a id="u04247"></a>

the experiences to be trained in become five.

<a id="u04248"></a>

There is the appearance of the exhaustion of knowing's experience;

<a id="u04249"></a>

increase is shown like the waxing moon.

<a id="u04250"></a>

With each appearance, an experience comes. [N-182](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-182)

<a id="u04251"></a>

Through warmth, measure, and signs,

<a id="u04252"></a>

the two truths enter in union, and the limits of conceptual thought are exhausted.

<a id="u04253"></a>

With no doing, characteristics are empty.

<a id="u04254"></a>

The basis of expression having ceased, one does not abide at an extreme.

<a id="u04255"></a>

Since outflows are exhausted, there is no material weight.

<a id="u04256"></a>

The interior of mindfulness ceases; essence is clear. [N-183](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-183)

<a id="u04257"></a>

Clear awareness does not conceptualize the limits of objects.

<a id="u04258"></a>

Free from words, truly without phrases,

<a id="u04259"></a>

all bases of dependence are exhausted.

<a id="u04260"></a>

Furthermore, one directly engages the desirable qualities.

<a id="u04261"></a>

The two trainings through form

<a id="u04262"></a>

are a support for focus and freedom from focus.

<a id="u04263"></a>

Through the special activity agreeable to the mental faculty,

<a id="u04264"></a>

first make offerings to the lama.

<a id="u04265"></a>

Become accustomed to shapes and then to colors;

<a id="u04266"></a>

through this familiarity, delusory appearance ceases.

<a id="u04267"></a>

For sound, with a pure support for focus:

<a id="u04268"></a>

piwaṃ, earthenware drum, resonant pot,

<a id="u04269"></a>

draba, the many-stringed instrument, the three-junctioned instrument,

<a id="u04270"></a>

har, flute, cymbals,

<a id="u04271"></a>

pheg, rdob, small hand drum, bell, and so forth—

<a id="u04272"></a>

join wind to each respective sound. [N-184](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184)

<a id="u04273"></a>

For smell, through a condition for focus agreeable to the mental faculty,

<a id="u04274"></a>

faith and great faith,

<a id="u04275"></a>

the supreme and the greatly distinctive,

<a id="u04276"></a>

join the yogin to great bliss. [N-184](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184)

<a id="u04277"></a>

For taste, an adept combines cane sugar, that endowed with six [tastes],

<a id="u04278"></a>

grape wine, and rice beer.

<a id="u04279"></a>

Knowing the five stages endowed with restraint,

<a id="u04280"></a>

merely tasting makes primordial knowing blaze. [N-184](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184)

<a id="u04281"></a>

For touch, with a support for focus agreeable to the mental faculty,

<a id="u04282"></a>

join each awareness to experiential acquaintance.

<a id="u04283"></a>

When a tree leaf curls rightward and contracts,

<a id="u04284"></a>

raise it: through touch, buddhahood is realized. [N-184](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-184)

<a id="u04285"></a>

Concerning phenomena, accord with the lama's authoritative transmission.

<a id="u04286"></a>

When excellent cultivation is realized,

<a id="u04287"></a>

practicing thus brings it onto the path,

<a id="u04288"></a>

and the flow of the machinery of delusion in the three realms is cut.

<a id="u04289"></a>

Furthermore, the appearance of primordial knowing:

<a id="u04290"></a>

from the white of stainless purity,

<a id="u04291"></a>

because qualities are complete, the form of appearance

<a id="u04292"></a>

becomes the indivisible, complete form embodiment.

<a id="u04293"></a>

From the yellow that generates qualities,

<a id="u04294"></a>

in an activity in which the Ground and differentiation are simultaneous,

<a id="u04295"></a>

the nature of phenomena is shown. [N-185](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-185)

<a id="u04296"></a>

From the red of complete self-expressiveness,

<a id="u04297"></a>

the center and surrounding of pure intrinsic nature,

<a id="u04298"></a>

the encircling bands and enclosure, are shown in completeness.

<a id="u04299"></a>

From the green free from doing-expressiveness,

<a id="u04300"></a>

emanations of pure enlightened activity arise.

<a id="u04301"></a>

From the blue of unchanging completeness,

<a id="u04302"></a>

not abiding at edge or center, it is pervasively empty.

<a id="u04303"></a>

Through pure intrinsic nature, the embodiments are ‘three, four.’ [N-185](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-185)

<a id="u04304"></a>

Thus, from the Great All-Penetrating Word, Root of All Phenomena:

<a id="u04305"></a>

the third chapter, the Array of Primordial Knowing, in which the root of appearance definitely emerges.[^G-U04305]

<a id="c3-transition"></a>

*[Unresolved source graphic at the Chapter 3/4 boundary; no transcription supplied.]*[^G-C3-TRANSITION]

<a id="part-4"></a>
## Chapter 4

<a id="u04306"></a>

Then the Lord of Gods, Maker of Joy,

<a id="u04307"></a>

asked the teacher, the great all-pervading lord,

<a id="u04308"></a>

about the intrinsic nature of the Array of the Nature of Phenomena.

<a id="u04309"></a>

‘Kye kye! Truly complete buddha,

<a id="u04310"></a>

through the Array of Primordial Knowing,

<a id="u04311"></a>

I have gained respite from the activities of samsara,

<a id="u04312"></a>

and been uplifted on the path of passing beyond sorrow. [N-186](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186)[^G-U04312]

<a id="u04313"></a>

I have also seen the object of intrinsic nature's appearance.

<a id="u04314"></a>

Although I have gained confidence in the buddhas' enlightened intent,

<a id="u04315"></a>

for sentient beings who will enter in the future,

<a id="u04316"></a>

so that primordial knowing free from conceptual thought may be complete,

<a id="u04317"></a>

I seek the Array of the Nature of Phenomena in evenness. [N-186](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186)

<a id="u04318"></a>

Where is the nature of phenomena of abiding to be applied?

<a id="u04319"></a>

What is the crucial point of the intervals of movement?

<a id="u04320"></a>

How is the nature of phenomena of time distinguished?

<a id="u04321"></a>

What is the nature of phenomena of buddhas like?

<a id="u04322"></a>

How does it abide at the time of sentient beings?

<a id="u04323"></a>

What are the crucial points of the body?

<a id="u04324"></a>

How does it emerge from the gates?

<a id="u04325"></a>

What are the characteristics of appearing in the object?

<a id="u04326"></a>

As what does it appear there? [N-186](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186)

<a id="u04327"></a>

Having seen, by what is it decisively established?

<a id="u04328"></a>

Having arrived, what is the measure of completeness?

<a id="a2000-c04-s01"></a>

*Scan-restored main verses:*[^G-A2000-C04-S01]

When the measure is reached, what becomes of the body?<br />
What is ordinary mind like?<br />
What is the nature of phenomena when connection is severed?

<a id="u04329"></a>

By what is the nature of phenomena of cause and result accomplished?

<a id="u04330"></a>

What is the nature of phenomena that clears in its own place?

<a id="u04331"></a>

What are the definitive phrases of the nature of phenomena?

<a id="u04332"></a>

What are its crucial points?

<a id="u04333"></a>

How is this crucial point of the time of arising held?

<a id="u04334"></a>

What is the nature of phenomena of the intermediate state like?

<a id="u04335"></a>

How do its characteristics appear?

<a id="u04336"></a>

Speak of these for the benefit of sentient beings.’

<a id="u04337"></a>

Then, [the words of] the teacher Vajradhara,

<a id="u04338"></a>

from the space of pure intrinsic nature—

<a id="u04339"></a>

these elaborations of words and phrases—

<a id="u04340"></a>

arose in the Ground free from expression. [N-186](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-186)

<a id="u04341"></a>

‘Ema! Marvelous wonder!

<a id="u04342"></a>

I shall teach an expression of wonder

<a id="u04343"></a>

unheard of in other fields.

<a id="u04344"></a>

**[Source heading: First subject of inquiry.]**

<a id="u04345"></a>

The nature of phenomena of abiding is as follows:

<a id="u04346"></a>

abiding in its fundamental disposition, it is free from virtue and wrongdoing;

<a id="u04347"></a>

abiding as cause and result, it is free from dependence;

<a id="u04348"></a>

abiding through conditions, the elements are pure—

<a id="u04349"></a>

it does not shift or change. [N-187](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187)

<a id="u04350"></a>

**[Source heading: Second subject of inquiry.]**

<a id="u04351"></a>

The nature of phenomena of the intervals of movement is like this:

<a id="u04352"></a>

I shall explain it fully as a great wonder.

<a id="u04353"></a>

Wind, according to the sequence of a day,

<a id="u04354"></a>

is reckoned as twenty thousand, one thousand,

<a id="u04355"></a>

and six hundred;

<a id="u04356"></a>

the winds of one day are thereby complete.

<a id="u04357"></a>

According to the particular distinctions,

<a id="u04358"></a>

for the young there are sixty extra;

<a id="u04359"></a>

for the old there is a shortfall.

<a id="u04360"></a>

This is through the activity of the elemental vessel. [N-188](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188)

<a id="u04361"></a>

Among sixty movements of wind,

<a id="u04362"></a>

the subtle movement of the nature of phenomena is held through reckoning.

<a id="u04363"></a>

From two such groups it is coarse;

<a id="u04364"></a>

when these are gathered as three, it is gross.

<a id="u04365"></a>

Subtract three from that: it is even. [N-188](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188)
