<a id="u04366"></a>

In brief, within twenty movements,

<a id="u04367"></a>

there is one evenness of the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u04368"></a>

**[Source heading: Third subject of inquiry.]**

<a id="u04369"></a>

The nature of phenomena of time is as follows:

<a id="u04370"></a>

outer, inner, secret,

<a id="u04371"></a>

and suchness—four.

<a id="u04372"></a>

The outer is the second [month] of autumn,

<a id="u04373"></a>

showing the abiding of the nature of phenomena of time.

<a id="u04374"></a>

The inner is the time when dawn breaks.

<a id="u04375"></a>

The secret is sleep and dreams.

<a id="u04376"></a>

Suchness is held through wind.

<a id="u04377"></a>

When the time is reached, it is complete. [N-188](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-188)

<a id="u04378"></a>

**[Source heading: Fourth subject of inquiry.]**

<a id="u04379"></a>

The nature of phenomena of buddhas:

<a id="u04380"></a>

undivided, not dividing, even—

<a id="u04381"></a>

how could words, phrases, and names exist there?

<a id="u04382"></a>

The basis of dependence is self-purified, and delusion is purified.

<a id="u04383"></a>

It is not known by the names samsara and nirvana.

<a id="u04384"></a>

Not having come about or arisen, it does not cease.

<a id="u04385"></a>

All-arising primordial knowing is not any [thing]. [N-187](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187)

<a id="u04386"></a>

There is no taking and no liberation.

<a id="u04387"></a>

In a moment free from causes and conditions,

<a id="u04388"></a>

buddhas and sentient beings appear as a foundation,

<a id="u04389"></a>

free from all multiplicity and parts. [N-187](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-187)

<a id="u04390"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifth subject of inquiry.]**

<a id="u04391"></a>

As for deluded sentient beings:

<a id="u04392"></a>

embodiments and primordial knowing are in their own manner,

<a id="u04393"></a>

genuine, fresh, and letting be—

<a id="u04394"></a>

abiding solely in ‘gyi nar’ [unresolved expression],

<a id="u04395"></a>

intrinsic nature at the faculties' objects. [N-189](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-189)

<a id="u04396"></a>

Here their own object is shown;

<a id="u04397"></a>

without entering into surrounding conditions,

<a id="u04398"></a>

in unobstructedness and penetration,

<a id="u04399"></a>

from the appearance aspects of object and knowing,

<a id="u04400"></a>

each is directly liberated from its own Ground. [N-189](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-189)

<a id="u04401"></a>

The desirable qualities are self-complete.

<a id="u04402"></a>

In the respective crucial points of diverse movement,

<a id="u04403"></a>

knowing one, one abides liberated.

<a id="u04404"></a>

Thus the nature of phenomena is realized,

<a id="u04405"></a>

free from parts.

<a id="u04406"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixth subject of inquiry.]**

<a id="u04407"></a>

The crucial points of the body are as follows:

<a id="u04408"></a>

the general [body], the heart, and the channels—

<a id="u04409"></a>

the pure, uncontrived nature of phenomena abides there. [N-190](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190)

<a id="u04410"></a>

Throughout the bodies of all sentient beings,

<a id="u04411"></a>

the nature of phenomena pervades in the manner of wind.

<a id="u04412"></a>

Subtle, light, moving, and separating:

<a id="u04413"></a>

through these four ways of functioning,

<a id="u04414"></a>

know the yoga of the four times.

<a id="u04415"></a>

Within the heart it abides as embodiment.

<a id="u04416"></a>

The embodiments of radiance have five aspects;

<a id="u04417"></a>

they hold the characteristics of their respective families.

<a id="u04418"></a>

The six embodiments of rays

<a id="u04419"></a>

gather the phenomena of intrinsic nature's appearance.

<a id="u04420"></a>

Through the three aspects of pure embodiment,

<a id="u04421"></a>

the characteristics of passing beyond sorrow are held.

<a id="u04422"></a>

Through the eight aspect-embodiments,

<a id="u04423"></a>

the doing of completing grounds and paths is done. [N-190](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190)

<a id="u04424"></a>

Within the channels are spheres.

<a id="u04425"></a>

Through three aspects of entering spheres,

<a id="u04426"></a>

the seeds connecting samsara and nirvana are planted.

<a id="u04427"></a>

Through the five arising spheres,

<a id="u04428"></a>

the mandala of yogic experience is established.

<a id="u04429"></a>

Through the six arraying spheres,

<a id="u04430"></a>

the manifold nature of phenomena is gathered into one.

<a id="u04431"></a>

Through reliance on each of these,

<a id="u04432"></a>

all phenomena have the identity of evenness. [N-190](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-190)

<a id="u04433"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventh reply.]**

<a id="u04434"></a>

As for how it emerges from the gates:

<a id="u04435"></a>

impelled by entering wind,

<a id="u04436"></a>

the four lamps become four activities.

<a id="u04437"></a>

Moved by pervading wind,

<a id="u04438"></a>

vajra chains [arise] from awareness itself.

<a id="u04439"></a>

Gathered by ripening wind,

<a id="u04440"></a>

they appear as embodiments in fivefold pairs. [N-191](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191)

<a id="u04441"></a>

From the eyes, the two winds of apprehended object and apprehending subject:

<a id="u04442"></a>

through the right, the apprehended aspect, appearance increases;

<a id="u04443"></a>

through the left, the apprehending aspect, colors are complete. [N-191](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191)

<a id="u04444"></a>

At the ears, from pervading and entering winds,

<a id="u04445"></a>

the right, through pervasion, displays sounds;

<a id="u04446"></a>

the left, through the activity of entering wind,

<a id="u04447"></a>

holds each respective [sound].

<a id="u04448"></a>

The wind of complete pervasion at the crown,

<a id="u04449"></a>

subtle and without generation,

<a id="u04450"></a>

also holds the embodiments of awareness. [N-191](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191)

<a id="u04451"></a>

**[Source heading: Eighth reply.]**

<a id="u04452"></a>

The characteristics of appearing in the object:

<a id="u04453"></a>

from space, clear blue and unstirred,

<a id="u04454"></a>

light, colors, and shapes

<a id="u04455"></a>

display the self-appearance of the five primordial knowings.

<a id="u04456"></a>

From the lamp of pure basic space,

<a id="u04457"></a>

spheres, embodiments, and purified delusory appearance—

<a id="u04458"></a>

intrinsic nature itself and essence itself—

<a id="u04459"></a>

appear nondually, without joining or separating. [N-191](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-191)

<a id="u04460"></a>

At this time the characteristic of exhaustion

<a id="u04461"></a>

is just like space, free from doing.

<a id="u04462"></a>

**[Source heading: Ninth reply.]**

<a id="u04463"></a>

As for by what it is seen:

<a id="u04464"></a>

it is seen through familiarity and the crucial points.

<a id="u04465"></a>

Familiarity starts with the preliminaries;

<a id="u04466"></a>

applying the crucial points of body and voice,

<a id="u04467"></a>

one sees the forms of pure self-appearance,

<a id="u04468"></a>

and all delusion subsides.

<a id="u04469"></a>

Through the body at the crucial point, seeing occurs.

<a id="u04470"></a>

Through the lion posture of the dharma embodiment,

<a id="u04471"></a>

free from all fear of delusion,

<a id="u04472"></a>

one sees with the vajra eye.

<a id="u04473"></a>

Through the posture of the complete enjoyment embodiment,

<a id="u04474"></a>

relying on the reclining elephant,

<a id="u04475"></a>

one fully enjoys the nature of phenomena

<a id="u04476"></a>

and sees with the lotus eye.

<a id="u04477"></a>

Through the posture of the emanation embodiment,

<a id="u04478"></a>

relying on the crouching sage,

<a id="u04479"></a>

the nature of phenomena emanates of itself,

<a id="u04480"></a>

and one sees with the dharma eye. [N-192](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192)

<a id="u04481"></a>

Furthermore, through channels and bodily exercises,

<a id="u04482"></a>

one sees the five primordial knowings of intrinsic nature's appearance

<a id="u04483"></a>

with the water-bubble eyes. [N-192](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192)

<a id="u04484"></a>

**[Source heading: Tenth reply.]**

<a id="u04485"></a>

Having seen, one applies the crucial point.

<a id="u04486"></a>

Through methods of guiding appearance,

<a id="u04487"></a>

the appearances of the four elements reach the crucial point.

<a id="u04488"></a>

By reckoning the connections of the eight winds' activities,

<a id="u04489"></a>

manifold differentiating conceptualization reaches the crucial point.

<a id="u04490"></a>

For the body: compressing, shaking out expressiveness,

<a id="u04491"></a>

and the crucial points of striking, extending, and gathering the limbs;

<a id="u04492"></a>

through subtlety, straightening, and massage—

<a id="u04493"></a>

in flesh, blood, and bone. [N-192](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-192)

<a id="u04494"></a>

**[Source heading: Eleventh reply.]**

<a id="u04495"></a>

Having thus arrived, the measures of completeness are:

<a id="u04496"></a>

cessation of what appears directly before one;

<a id="u04497"></a>

introducing appearance into earth and stone;

<a id="u04498"></a>

one's own knowing entering anything;

<a id="u04499"></a>

through that entry, moving insentient things;

<a id="u04500"></a>

taking the measure of wind's movement; [N-193](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-193)

<a id="u04501"></a>

seeing the body's minute particles arises;

<a id="u04502"></a>

whatever appears is complete as form embodiment;

<a id="u04503"></a>

there, father and mother embrace;

<a id="u04504"></a>

fivefold fathers and mothers join;

<a id="u04505"></a>

all are within a round encircling band.

<a id="u04506"></a>

The yogin's body too is clear light;

<a id="u04507"></a>

going, coming, and abiding—

<a id="u04508"></a>

all are awareness reaching its measure. [N-194](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194)

<a id="u04509"></a>

**[Source heading: Twelfth reply.]**

<a id="u04510"></a>

The manner of reaching the measure also becomes like this:

<a id="u04511"></a>

free from the particles of each body,

<a id="u04512"></a>

windows of light appear in the intervening space.

<a id="u04513"></a>

From these, rays shaped like hooks,

<a id="u04514"></a>

subtle and entwining, hold appearance.

<a id="u04515"></a>

The body's material weight self-ceases:

<a id="u04516"></a>

a stainless body of light with unimpeded penetration.

<a id="u04517"></a>

At its center is the sign of A,

<a id="u04518"></a>

and rays from the brow curl measure one armspan.

<a id="u04519"></a>

The topknot appears held back by wind. [N-194](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194)[^G-U04519]

<a id="u04520"></a>

From the fingers of the peaceful embodiments,

<a id="u04521"></a>

garlands of light gather densely.

<a id="u04522"></a>

There is activity of ordinary mind without sound.

<a id="u04523"></a>

From the palace of the skull in the head,

<a id="u04524"></a>

Herukas manifestly arise.

<a id="u04525"></a>

At this time one's body is brought to its measure:

<a id="u04526"></a>

the three embodiments, with unimpeded penetration, gathered into one. [N-194](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-194)

<a id="u04527"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirteenth reply.]**

<a id="u04528"></a>

At this time ordinary mind is clear;

<a id="u04529"></a>

through the six higher knowings,

<a id="u04530"></a>

phenomena that are far away or measurable

<a id="u04531"></a>

are known in an instant.

<a id="u04532"></a>

Immeasurable buddha fields

<a id="u04533"></a>

and likewise embodiments appear to ordinary mind.

<a id="u04534"></a>

In empty ordinary mind, stains are exhausted.

<a id="u04535"></a>

Intrinsic nature does not move; emptiness and clarity are even.

<a id="u04536"></a>

Unmixed, complete in evenness, it pervades basic space.

<a id="u04537"></a>

Appearance and existence are clear, free from multiplicity.

<a id="u04538"></a>

Without parts, there is no elaboration. [N-195](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195)

<a id="u04539"></a>

**[Source heading: Fourteenth reply.]**

<a id="u04540"></a>

At the times of such appearances,

<a id="u04541"></a>

the connection between body and ordinary mind is severed.

<a id="u04542"></a>

Even in the body whose contaminated [elements] are exhausted,

<a id="u04543"></a>

ordinary mind, characterized by clarity, emerges outward.

<a id="u04544"></a>

In the manner of a shooting star passing across,

<a id="u04545"></a>

it is clearly seen in the element of space. [N-195](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195)

<a id="u04546"></a>

At the initial times of this,

<a id="u04547"></a>

even in separation from wind,

<a id="u04548"></a>

through effort and the activity of pure wind,

<a id="u04549"></a>

it arises from being projected outside the body.

<a id="u04550"></a>

Through the two aspects of what sees,

<a id="u04551"></a>

the body is pure clear light;

<a id="u04552"></a>

ordinary mind is like proliferating sparks,

<a id="u04553"></a>

not abiding in one place, a Ground of its own clarity.

<a id="u04554"></a>

It arises in the manner of two appearance aspects.

<a id="u04555"></a>

Through this, the connection between body and ordinary mind is severed,

<a id="u04556"></a>

and one does not return to the three realms. [N-195](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-195)

<a id="u04557"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifteenth reply.]**

<a id="u04558"></a>

The nature of phenomena of cause and result:

<a id="u04559"></a>

when the source of coarse conceptual thought has ceased,

<a id="u04560"></a>

in the continuum of self-purified mindfulness,

<a id="u04561"></a>

dependent connections and wind itself are completely pure.

<a id="u04562"></a>

It is the nature of phenomena arising from wind. [N-196](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196)[^G-U04562]

<a id="u04563"></a>

In intrinsic nature, pure from the beginning,

<a id="u04564"></a>

the numbers one and many are exhausted.

<a id="u04565"></a>

There are no embodiments or primordial knowing;

<a id="u04566"></a>

having reached the limit where phenomena are exhausted,

<a id="u04567"></a>

this is held to be the nature of phenomena of the result. [N-196](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196)

<a id="u04568"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixteenth reply.]**

<a id="u04569"></a>

The nature of phenomena that clears in its own place:

<a id="u04570"></a>

when the elements clear, material weight is purified;

<a id="u04571"></a>

particles, subtle and coarse, cease at their limit,

<a id="u04572"></a>

and not even a mere part abides.

<a id="u04573"></a>

When afflictions clear, delusion clears.

<a id="u04574"></a>

Free from conceptual thought, clinging does not abide;

<a id="u04575"></a>

neither dormancy nor manifest arising—

<a id="u04576"></a>

not even a particle's portion abides.

<a id="u04577"></a>

Through the clearing of subtle and coarse in this way,

<a id="u04578"></a>

the five aggregates gradually become subtle.

<a id="u04579"></a>

When the body of the four elements is exhausted,

<a id="u04580"></a>

all is embodiment complete in primordial knowing.

<a id="u04581"></a>

Deep absorption free from conceptualization abides of itself.

<a id="u04582"></a>

Beyond contrived phenomena,

<a id="u04583"></a>

it does not abide even in the phrase ‘phenomena.’ [N-196](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-196)

<a id="u04584"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventeenth reply.]**

<a id="u04585"></a>

The definitive phrase ‘nature of phenomena’ is thus:

<a id="u04586"></a>

‘phenomena’ means gathering and assembling,

<a id="u04587"></a>

doing doings and holding characteristics,

<a id="u04588"></a>

showing self-appearance

<a id="u04589"></a>

and holding the respective classes.

<a id="u04590"></a>

‘Nature’ means no contrivance;

<a id="u04591"></a>

abiding in letting be, it genuinely pervades.

<a id="u04592"></a>

Fresh, genuine, and in its original course,

<a id="u04593"></a>

the number of words, phrases, and names is exhausted. [N-197](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197)

<a id="u04594"></a>

**[Source heading: Eighteenth reply.]**

<a id="u04595"></a>

The nature of phenomena of the crucial points is as follows:

<a id="u04596"></a>

the elements' abodes, one's body,

<a id="u04597"></a>

objects, gates, knowing, wind,

<a id="u04598"></a>

channels, spheres, and subsidiary aspects—

<a id="u04599"></a>

each respective entry abides pervasively.

<a id="u04600"></a>

Through transforming their respective preliminaries

<a id="u04601"></a>

and the abiding of the main practice,

<a id="u04602"></a>

in the nature of phenomena free from conceptual thought,

<a id="u04603"></a>

intrinsic nature abides and movement is exhausted.

<a id="u04604"></a>

From the object in which exhaustion and appearance are nondual,

<a id="u04605"></a>

through intrinsic abiding and contrived [practice],

<a id="u04606"></a>

the basic space of phenomena also appears through purity.

<a id="u04607"></a>

Through this, buddhahood comes through the crucial points. [N-197](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197)

<a id="u04608"></a>

**[Source heading: Nineteenth reply.]**

<a id="u04609"></a>

The crucial point of the time of arising is like this:

<a id="u04610"></a>

Ema! I shall explain a great wonder.

<a id="u04611"></a>

This nature of phenomena has eight times of arising,

<a id="u04612"></a>

and just as many times of abiding.

<a id="u04613"></a>

Also at four distinctive times,

<a id="u04614"></a>

when the yogin's ordinary mind abides,

<a id="u04615"></a>

there is evenness without joining or separating.

<a id="u04616"></a>

Knowing the crucial point of pure intrinsic nature,

<a id="u04617"></a>

one knows phenomena without anything being done.

<a id="u04618"></a>

Time is held through the number of transitions;

<a id="u04619"></a>

through holding it, there is purity without delusion. [N-197](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-197)

<a id="u04620"></a>

**[Source heading: Twentieth reply, in full.]**

<a id="u04621"></a>

The nature of phenomena of the intermediate state is like this:

<a id="u04622"></a>

I shall explain the most secret nature of phenomena.

<a id="u04623"></a>

Distinguished, there are four aspects;

<a id="u04624"></a>

here I shall explain the intermediate state of the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u04625"></a>

When released from the moment of death,

<a id="u04626"></a>

consciousness dissolves into space.

<a id="u04627"></a>

When space dissolves into clear light,

<a id="u04628"></a>

all subtle and coarse appearances cease.

<a id="u04629"></a>

A body of light endowed with all faculties

<a id="u04630"></a>

becomes like a shadow from the body. [N-198](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198)

<a id="u04631"></a>

At that time, when light appears in fragments,

<a id="u04632"></a>

the adept holds the spreading of appearance.

<a id="u04633"></a>

Through the spreading of moving wind,

<a id="u04634"></a>

light and rays of five colors

<a id="u04635"></a>

appear in uncertain rainbow patterns.

<a id="u04636"></a>

From entering and ripening winds,

<a id="u04637"></a>

the four directions, intermediate directions, above and below are established.

<a id="u04638"></a>

As the directions' lights gather there,

<a id="u04639"></a>

embodiments of the five families also manifest.

<a id="u04640"></a>

Blue, white, yellow, red, and green

<a id="u04641"></a>

appear with their respective objects.

<a id="u04642"></a>

Knowing how to spread and hold them,

<a id="u04643"></a>

one completes the buddhas' qualities

<a id="u04644"></a>

and does not enter the three realms. [N-198](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198)

<a id="u04645"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-first reply.]**

<a id="u04646"></a>

Their characteristics are like this:

<a id="u04647"></a>

free from dependence on coarse elements,

<a id="u04648"></a>

spheres blaze as rays in the light.

<a id="u04649"></a>

Five connected spheres are single embodiments;

<a id="u04650"></a>

from half-embodiments, forms become complete.

<a id="u04651"></a>

From embodiments with half-forms, there are fivefold pairs.

<a id="u04652"></a>

As each cluster becomes complete,

<a id="u04653"></a>

there are groups of five, ten, and a hundred;

<a id="u04654"></a>

clusters of a thousand and a hundred thousand

<a id="u04655"></a>

appear pure in the intrinsic nature of what is envisaged. [N-198](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-198)

<a id="u04656"></a>

Innumerable, beyond what expression can encompass—

<a id="u04657"></a>

when one oneself knows their intrinsic nature,

<a id="u04658"></a>

one attains the three embodiments gathered into one.

<a id="u04659"></a>

From this attainment there is no return.

<a id="u04660"></a>

It comes from making this clear. [N-199](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199)

<a id="u04661"></a>

Furthermore, the essence of the nature of phenomena

<a id="u04662"></a>

is established as essence from intrinsic nature itself.

<a id="u04663"></a>

Since its manner of appearance is not fixed as one,

<a id="u04664"></a>

the paths of entry also differ.

<a id="u04665"></a>

Through identity, knowing, and seeing liberation,

<a id="u04666"></a>

through entering, seeing, and familiarity—

<a id="u04667"></a>

from recognition, there is one's own identity;

<a id="u04668"></a>

through trust, one reaches it;

<a id="u04669"></a>

through decision, one enters confidence.

<a id="u04670"></a>

Through these three certainties,

<a id="u04671"></a>

essence being complete, the continuum ceases. [N-199](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199)

<a id="u04672"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the intrinsic nature of the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u04673"></a>

Not fixed as one thing of this kind,

<a id="u04674"></a>

it appears however it is designated.

<a id="u04675"></a>

From the basis for assigning diverse names,

<a id="u04676"></a>

it appears as elaborations of many words and phrases.

<a id="u04677"></a>

Names are uncertain and diverse;

<a id="u04678"></a>

meaning too is empty through mere designation.

<a id="u04679"></a>

Since emptiness is not established as anything,

<a id="u04680"></a>

its intrinsic nature appears pure. [N-199](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-199)

<a id="u04681"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the compassionate responsiveness of the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u04682"></a>

Since the domains of those to be trained are diverse,

<a id="u04683"></a>

the embodiments that train them are likewise.

<a id="u04684"></a>

Through the transformations of classes and faculties,

<a id="u04685"></a>

conceptual thoughts of samsara and nirvana differ.

<a id="u04686"></a>

When each is left in its own object,

<a id="u04687"></a>

this is enlightened intent of the uncontrived meaning.

<a id="u04688"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the appearance of the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u04689"></a>

In impure delusion,

<a id="u04690"></a>

without contriving the gates of the faculties,

<a id="u04691"></a>

the crucial point is leaving [them] as they are;

<a id="u04692"></a>

not altering that is the pith instruction. [N-200](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200)

<a id="u04693"></a>

In appearance without an apprehended object,

<a id="u04694"></a>

ordinary mind arises without an apprehending subject.

<a id="u04695"></a>

It is seen by eyes without activity;

<a id="u04696"></a>

drawn along a path without a nature of phenomena;

<a id="u04697"></a>

carried to a Ground without view and cultivation;

<a id="u04698"></a>

gathered into a result with nothing to do, free from effort.

<a id="u04699"></a>

Here the limits of appearance do not abide. [N-200](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200)

<a id="u04700"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the nature of phenomena of awareness.

<a id="u04701"></a>

Through naked seeing in vajra chains,

<a id="u04702"></a>

the mindfulness of moving differentiating conceptualization ceases.

<a id="u04703"></a>

Like going in space along the birds' path,[^G-U04703]

<a id="u04704"></a>

there is nowhere to go from the basic space of phenomena.

<a id="u04705"></a>

With no other, awareness-appearance being completely pure,

<a id="u04706"></a>

the causes and conditions of delusive appearance are exhausted.

<a id="u04707"></a>

Since basic space and awareness are inseparable,

<a id="u04708"></a>

the connected appearance of vajra chains is shown.

<a id="u04709"></a>

The essence of awareness is vajra chains:

<a id="u04710"></a>

in past, future, or present,

<a id="u04711"></a>

no one has made them.

<a id="u04712"></a>

Thus being unconditioned

<a id="u04713"></a>

is the intrinsic nature of vajra chains.

<a id="u04714"></a>

All differentiating conceptualization being pure,

<a id="u04715"></a>

there is buddhahood without delusion. [N-200](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-200)

<a id="u04716"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the nature of phenomena of the continuum.

<a id="u04717"></a>

The true nature of phenomena penetrates beyond words;

<a id="u04718"></a>

its own essence is without letters.

<a id="u04719"></a>

The lamp-light of self-aware primordial knowing blazes.

<a id="u04720"></a>

With the nature of phenomena pure, auspiciousness is beautiful.

<a id="u04721"></a>

The view, free from fear, completes the lion's expressiveness;

<a id="u04722"></a>

with self-awareness ripened, completeness arises naturally;

<a id="u04723"></a>

vajra chains connect, a garland of pearls;

<a id="u04724"></a>

appearances of experience form an array of jeweled inlay;

<a id="u04725"></a>

self-abiding emptiness is the mirror of the core;

<a id="u04726"></a>

deviations are cut of themselves: the mirror of awakened mind.

<a id="u04727"></a>

Not arising from another, it is self-arising itself.

<a id="u04728"></a>

Because it arises naturally, there is self-liberation.

<a id="u04729"></a>

One in basic space, there are also six expanses.

<a id="u04730"></a>

With qualities complete, precious things are heaped.

<a id="u04731"></a>

Self-awareness being taught, it is adorned with pointing out.

<a id="u04732"></a>

With appearance pure, sun and moon are joined.

<a id="u04733"></a>

Because awareness ripens, embodiment-relics blaze.

<a id="u04734"></a>

All of these arise from the Ground. [N-201](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-201)

<a id="u04735"></a>

Thus, because the nature of phenomena is inexpressible,

<a id="u04736"></a>

it arises as the foundation of conventional phrases.

<a id="u04737"></a>

The sequences of expressed phrases being completely pure,

<a id="u04738"></a>

one thus abides in the inexpressible.

<a id="u04739"></a>

Therefore the limits of phrase-sequences are exhausted.

<a id="u04740"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the nature of phenomena of liberation.

<a id="u04741"></a>

Liberated through the crucial points, striving and effort are exhausted.

<a id="u04742"></a>

Liberated from the beginning, there is no need to repeat.

<a id="u04743"></a>

Because it is self-liberation, there is no antidote.

<a id="u04744"></a>

Because it is naked liberation, it vanishes where it is seen.

<a id="u04745"></a>

Completely liberated, intrinsic nature is pure.

<a id="u04746"></a>

Liberated through time, there is no need for familiarity.

<a id="u04747"></a>

Liberated through intrinsic nature, it is uncontrived.

<a id="u04748"></a>

‘Liberated’ is merely a convention.

<a id="u04749"></a>

Whose phenomena are realization and non-realization?

<a id="u04750"></a>

For whom there is ‘liberated,’ what is the object of focus? [N-202](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-202)

<a id="u04751"></a>

For whom could entry into the three realms occur?

<a id="u04752"></a>

This is the nature of phenomena liberated from the limits of existence.

<a id="u04753"></a>

Furthermore, the nature of phenomena free from doing:

<a id="u04754"></a>

with no doing, there is liberation;

<a id="u04755"></a>

with no arising, there is no abiding.

<a id="u04756"></a>

With no going, coming and circling are exhausted.

<a id="u04757"></a>

There is neither one nor two of what is done or arisen.

<a id="u04758"></a>

There is not even mere conventional delusion.

<a id="u04759"></a>

It is empty of entry into activities of existence and nonexistence.

<a id="u04760"></a>

From the beginning, the result is free from the conceptual mind,

<a id="u04761"></a>

beyond all speaking, thinking, and expression. [N-203](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-203)

<a id="u04762"></a>

Thus, from the Great All-Penetrating Word, Root of All Phenomena:

<a id="u04763"></a>

the fourth chapter, the Array of the Nature of Phenomena, teaching the root of ordinary mind's appearance.[^G-U04763]

<a id="c4-transition"></a>

*[Unresolved source graphic at the Chapter 4/5 boundary; no transcription supplied.]*[^G-C4-TRANSITION]

<a id="part-5"></a>
## Chapter 5

<a id="u04764"></a>

Then, through completely pure intrinsic nature,

<a id="u04765"></a>

from the inexpressible dharma embodiment,

<a id="u04766"></a>

from the space of the nature of phenomena, free from conceptual elaborations,

<a id="u04767"></a>

from the inexpressible foundation,

<a id="u04768"></a>

phrases of meaning in a naturally arisen array

<a id="u04769"></a>

arose like light proliferating from the teacher's tongue,

<a id="u04770"></a>

without being made by anyone. [N-204](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-204)

<a id="u04771"></a>

The Lord of Gods supplicated:

<a id="u04772"></a>

‘Kyema! Blessed one, lord without delusion,

<a id="u04773"></a>

by whom will the Array of View and Cultivation

<a id="u04774"></a>

be held?

<a id="u04775"></a>

What is the natural state established in its own disposition?

<a id="u04776"></a>

How should one view it?

<a id="u04777"></a>

Distinguish the boundary between viewing phrases and meaning.

<a id="u04778"></a>

What are the essence and intrinsic nature of view?

<a id="u04779"></a>

What are the divisions of expressed phrases and names?

<a id="u04780"></a>

What is that called cultivation like?

<a id="u04781"></a>

What are its activity and understanding?

<a id="u04782"></a>

What cultivation abides without deviation?

<a id="u04783"></a>

How do cultivation's characteristics appear?

<a id="u04784"></a>

What are the faults of not cultivating?

<a id="u04785"></a>

I wish to hear these.

<a id="u04786"></a>

Vajra-holder, speak to me.’

<a id="u04787"></a>

Then, from uncontrived space,

<a id="u04788"></a>

the one bearing thirty-two marks

<a id="u04789"></a>

spoke the Array of View and Cultivation thus:

<a id="u04790"></a>

‘Lord of Gods, listen well.

<a id="u04791"></a>

Whoever doubts this

<a id="u04792"></a>

obscures the embodiments of the buddhas.

<a id="u04793"></a>

**[Source heading: First reply.]**

<a id="u04794"></a>

Those who hold view and cultivation

<a id="u04795"></a>

have excellent faith and diligence,

<a id="u04796"></a>

are able to give and respect the lama,

<a id="u04797"></a>

possess disciplined observance and avoid wrongdoing;

<a id="u04798"></a>

unchanging and utterly stable, they hold it.

<a id="u04799"></a>

Alternatively, one whose own impetus is quiet,

<a id="u04800"></a>

letting be, with a simple person's relaxation,

<a id="u04801"></a>

whose realization is small and whose intrinsic nature is quiet,

<a id="u04802"></a>

who is free from doings of body and voice,

<a id="u04803"></a>

and possesses discerning knowing, will hold it. [N-205](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-205)

<a id="u04804"></a>

**[Source heading: Second reply.]**

<a id="u04805"></a>

The natural state established in its own disposition

<a id="u04806"></a>

also has seven manners of abiding:

<a id="u04807"></a>

spontaneous presence from the side of diversity;

<a id="u04808"></a>

indeterminacy from the aspect of the extent of change;

<a id="u04809"></a>

certainty through not changing;

<a id="u04810"></a>

appearance's expressiveness transforming into anything;

<a id="u04811"></a>

all arising, together with affirmation;

<a id="u04812"></a>

primordial purity because delusion is purified;

<a id="u04813"></a>

and the variegation held to be diverse manners of appearance. [N-206](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-206)

<a id="u04814"></a>

Through such distinctions in what is asserted,

<a id="u04815"></a>

because the Great Perfection appears as anything,

<a id="u04816"></a>

although differing assertions are numerous,

<a id="u04817"></a>

in the view of abiding in its own disposition

<a id="u04818"></a>

there is no one, no two, and freedom from anything.

<a id="u04819"></a>

Even taking life and taking what is not given

<a id="u04820"></a>

arise from me and are my phenomena. [N-207](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207)

<a id="u04821"></a>

View, cultivation, and activity too

<a id="u04822"></a>

are beyond the extremes of existence, nonexistence, and emptiness.

<a id="u04823"></a>

In brief, they are distinguished through characteristics.

<a id="u04824"></a>

**[Source heading: Third reply.]**

<a id="u04825"></a>

There are two ways of viewing this:

<a id="u04826"></a>

viewing phrases and viewing meaning.

<a id="u04827"></a>

Viewing phrases has seven stages;

<a id="u04828"></a>

their respective assertions are constructed by the conceptual mind:

<a id="u04829"></a>

freedom from doing and absence of striving;

<a id="u04830"></a>

gradual entry and drawing out distinctions of self and other;

<a id="u04831"></a>

taking life, and freedom from virtue and wrongdoing;

<a id="u04832"></a>

nothing done and nothing arisen;

<a id="u04833"></a>

unaltered appearance, self-purified;

<a id="u04834"></a>

and afflictions held to be liberated in intrinsic nature. [N-207](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207)

<a id="u04835"></a>

Meaning is viewed in direct perception of the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u04836"></a>

not through discerning knowing,

<a id="u04837"></a>

but as the very domain of the faculties.

<a id="u04838"></a>

It is not decisively reached through phrases.

<a id="u04839"></a>

In the identity of seeing the three crucial points,

<a id="u04840"></a>

because the three phrases are explained as crucial points,

<a id="u04841"></a>

there are no sharp or dull faculties.

<a id="u04842"></a>

Because seeing the nature of phenomena purifies,

<a id="u04843"></a>

there is no good or bad in activities.

<a id="u04844"></a>

The nature of phenomena is empty and without divisions;

<a id="u04845"></a>

virtue and wrongdoing, both, are removed. [N-207](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-207)

<a id="u04846"></a>

Without seeing self-awareness directly,

<a id="u04847"></a>

the assertions of the nine stages do not liberate.

<a id="u04848"></a>

When the nature of phenomena is exhausted and its limit reached,

<a id="u04849"></a>

the three embodiments are the appearance of the path.

<a id="u04850"></a>

**[Source heading: Fourth reply.]**

<a id="u04851"></a>

The boundary between the views of phrases and meaning:

<a id="u04852"></a>

phrases are causes of expression;

<a id="u04853"></a>

whatever expresses or produces an emanation

<a id="u04854"></a>

is mental examination that thinks about it.

<a id="u04855"></a>

Meaning is oneself viewing oneself.

<a id="u04856"></a>

Through the three measures of direct perception,

<a id="u04857"></a>

one engages directly with vajra chains.

<a id="u04858"></a>

Through naked seeing and naked liberation,

<a id="u04859"></a>

whatever one sees is meaning. [N-208](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-208)

<a id="u04860"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifth reply.]**

<a id="u04861"></a>

The essence of view is seeing,

<a id="u04862"></a>

unchanged by phrases of mental examination.

<a id="u04863"></a>

Neither authoritative transmission nor the lama's pith instructions

<a id="u04864"></a>

shows it by any [means]. [N-208](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-208)

<a id="u04865"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixth reply.]**

<a id="u04866"></a>

The intrinsic nature of view pervades environment and inhabitants;

<a id="u04867"></a>

nothing is unpervaded, so anything is liberated.

<a id="u04868"></a>

In liberated phenomena, differentiated grasping is absent.

<a id="u04869"></a>

Whatever appears is one's own nature of phenomena;

<a id="u04870"></a>

there is no separate contrived nature of phenomena.

<a id="u04871"></a>

However one acts, non-conceptuality arises.

<a id="u04872"></a>

There is self-appearance without rejecting and accepting.

<a id="u04873"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventh reply.]**

<a id="u04874"></a>

The divisions of expressed phrases and names

<a id="u04875"></a>

are outer, inner, secret,

<a id="u04876"></a>

and unsurpassed—the four views.

<a id="u04877"></a>

The outer is viewing bearers of properties:

<a id="u04878"></a>

in the respective assertions of the eight stages,

<a id="u04879"></a>

mental examination and grasping are maintained. [N-209](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-209)

<a id="u04880"></a>

The inner: viewing the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u04881"></a>

one sees essence and intrinsic nature as illusion.

<a id="u04882"></a>

The secret: viewing self-awareness,

<a id="u04883"></a>

one sees its uninterrupted connection with vajra chains.

<a id="u04884"></a>

Suchness is basic space and awareness. [N-209](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-209)

<a id="u04885"></a>

**[Source heading: Eighth reply.]**

<a id="u04886"></a>

What is called cultivation is ordinary mind abiding,

<a id="u04887"></a>

cutting outer and inner movement,

<a id="u04888"></a>

and ceasing apprehended object and apprehending subject.

<a id="u04889"></a>

What arises from intrinsic nature's own disposition

<a id="u04890"></a>

is to be called meditative stabilization.

<a id="u04891"></a>

Wind, channels, spheres,

<a id="u04892"></a>

the body's crucial points, the eyes, and emptiness—

<a id="u04893"></a>

these are held to be cultivation.

<a id="u04894"></a>

The light and colors of letters,

<a id="u04895"></a>

and the emanations' proliferating and gathering,

<a id="u04896"></a>

are explained as deep absorption.

<a id="u04897"></a>

Uncontrived, resting of itself, is the natural state.

<a id="u04898"></a>

Because there is no change from that, however [it occurs],

<a id="u04899"></a>

it is the buddhas' enlightened intent. [N-210](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-210)

<a id="u04900"></a>

**[Source heading: Ninth reply.]**

<a id="u04901"></a>

Its activity and understanding:

<a id="u04902"></a>

through activity, clinging to objects is cut,

<a id="u04903"></a>

afflictions and the like are rejected,

<a id="u04904"></a>

and the root of samsara is drawn out.

<a id="u04905"></a>

Through understanding, the nature of ordinary mind is held,

<a id="u04906"></a>

made to abide, and wind is exhausted;

<a id="u04907"></a>

the path of the empty nature of phenomena is found,

<a id="u04908"></a>

and there is ground-appearance of pure intrinsic nature.

<a id="u04909"></a>

**[Source heading: Tenth reply.]**

<a id="u04910"></a>

Cultivation that abides without deviation:

<a id="u04911"></a>

the faculties' gates are clear,

<a id="u04912"></a>

and the mental faculty's conceptual thought does not move.

<a id="u04913"></a>

The mental faculty that examines knowing itself

<a id="u04914"></a>

does not escape outward.

<a id="u04915"></a>

It is not a clarity marked by divisions;

<a id="u04916"></a>

it has not gone into an emptiness cut off separately;

<a id="u04917"></a>

it is not a continuous succession of bliss;

<a id="u04918"></a>

it has not become a stagnant pool of equanimity. [N-211](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-211)

<a id="u04919"></a>

Resting in clear mindfulness and awareness;

<a id="u04920"></a>

training's contrary aspects having become the path;

<a id="u04921"></a>

an apprehending aspect with a continuous inward ease;

<a id="u04922"></a>

an apprehending aspect clear in its manner of parts;

<a id="u04923"></a>

and casting out colors and syllables—[^G-U04923]

<a id="u04924"></a>

when one has not fallen to the side of any of these,

<a id="u04925"></a>

this is cultivation abiding without deviation. [N-211](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-211)

<a id="u04926"></a>

**[Source heading: Eleventh reply.]**

<a id="u04927"></a>

The characteristics and manner of appearance of cultivation:

<a id="u04928"></a>

its characteristic is empty, clear awareness;

<a id="u04929"></a>

its intrinsic nature is mixed with nothing;

<a id="u04930"></a>

the limits of apprehended object and apprehending subject are exhausted;

<a id="u04931"></a>

and the nature of phenomena is pure in its own identity.

<a id="u04932"></a>

Its manner of appearance is not delusion.

<a id="u04933"></a>

In the intrinsic nature of pure causes and conditions,

<a id="u04934"></a>

things and absence of things are self-purified;

<a id="u04935"></a>

clinging to the objects of the five gates is purified;

<a id="u04936"></a>

the order of the elements is purified;

<a id="u04937"></a>

and the increasing appearance of pure primordial knowing is shown.

<a id="u04938"></a>

There is the nature of phenomena of exhaustion. [N-212](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-212)

<a id="u04939"></a>

**[Source heading: Twelfth reply.]**

<a id="u04940"></a>

The faults of not cultivating are:

<a id="u04941"></a>

the characteristics of samsara manifest;

<a id="u04942"></a>

self and other, objects and knowing,

<a id="u04943"></a>

a view bound to phrases,

<a id="u04944"></a>

and objects bound to focus—

<a id="u04945"></a>

afflictions bind [one] as a self.

<a id="u04946"></a>

The path of buddhahood is also lost;

<a id="u04947"></a>

the intrinsic nature of the result is not known;

<a id="u04948"></a>

there is no Ground of the evenness of all phenomena;

<a id="u04949"></a>

self-awareness is bound in the three realms;

<a id="u04950"></a>

and one falls into what is accompanied by conceptual thought. [N-212](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-212)

<a id="u04951"></a>

**[Source heading: Below this, the condensed meaning is explained.]**

<a id="u04952"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the wheel of the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u04953"></a>

Through the view of abiding, all phenomena are realized.

<a id="u04954"></a>

With intrinsic nature pure, samsara and nirvana are distinguished.

<a id="u04955"></a>

With nothing done, there is arising.

<a id="u04956"></a>

Brought onto the true path, it pervades.

<a id="u04957"></a>

Completely arisen, the nature of phenomena is empty.

<a id="u04958"></a>

With no doing, non-arising is complete.

<a id="u04959"></a>

Samsara being purified, there is passing beyond sorrow.

<a id="u04960"></a>

Furthermore, the view of pure seeing:

<a id="u04961"></a>

the great completeness of self-purified absence of things.

<a id="u04962"></a>

There is no need to make offerings to deities;

<a id="u04963"></a>

there is no mantra recitation or austerity;

<a id="u04964"></a>

no incense, lamps, or food offerings to deities;

<a id="u04965"></a>

no gathering offerings or offerings to the lama;

<a id="u04966"></a>

no praise or burnt offerings;

<a id="u04967"></a>

no scope of activity of the six perfections.

<a id="u04968"></a>

Thus, with no Ground or root whatsoever:

<a id="u04969"></a>

taking life, taking what is not given, and unchaste activity— [N-213](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-213)

<a id="u04970"></a>

there is no view, no cultivation, and no activity.

<a id="u04971"></a>

With nothing to view or enact, what assertion of cultivation is there?

<a id="u04972"></a>

Ordinary mind's assertions of emptiness and clarity cease.

<a id="u04973"></a>

Likewise, phenomena and the phenomena of buddhahood are absent. [N-213](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-213)

<a id="u04974"></a>

Furthermore, the view of intrinsic nature:

<a id="u04975"></a>

the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature, without things.

<a id="u04976"></a>

Earth, water, fire, and wind—the elements—are manifestly buddha.

<a id="u04977"></a>

Without contrivance, there is great self-liberation itself.

<a id="u04978"></a>

Desire, hatred, dull confusion, and awakening—four. [N-214](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214)

<a id="u04979"></a>

The activity of differentiating conceptualization and karma is confidence in realization.

<a id="u04980"></a>

Without rejecting samsara, there is the buddhas' enlightened intent.

<a id="u04981"></a>

Body, voice, and mental faculty being refined, the three embodiments are complete.

<a id="u04982"></a>

Ordinary mind, ignorance, and delusion are the Ground of the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u04983"></a>

Karma, habitual tendencies, and clinging are crucial points of view. [N-214](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-214)

<a id="u04984"></a>

Furthermore, the contrived view:

<a id="u04985"></a>

for a buddha that has not arisen from phenomena,

<a id="u04986"></a>

a result complete without being generated by a cause,

<a id="u04987"></a>

one takes a body not destroyed by conditions.

<a id="u04988"></a>

With tenets not arising from authoritative transmission,

<a id="u04989"></a>

and pith instructions not relying on the continuum,

<a id="u04990"></a>

one cuts the root of passing beyond sorrow,

<a id="u04991"></a>

and makes the result of samsara ripen.

<a id="u04992"></a>

An untrue, impure Ground—

<a id="u04993"></a>

in the foundation of the six afflictions,

<a id="u04994"></a>

the seed of awakening is emptied out.

<a id="u04995"></a>

Forms of the three lower destinies ripen,

<a id="u04996"></a>

and the three embodiments too are ‘lag bcangs’ [unresolved]. [N-215](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-215)

<a id="u04997"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the view of what is expressed.

<a id="u04998"></a>

If life is not cut, one does not become buddha. [N-216](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-216)

<a id="u04999"></a>

If the windows are not closed,

<a id="u05000"></a>

the darkness itself is not cleared. [N-216](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-216)

<a id="u05001"></a>

The teacher of the buddhas,

<a id="u05002"></a>

at the king of Dharma in hell,

<a id="u05003"></a>

leads through Dharma to the three lower destinies.

<a id="u05004"></a>

Through practicing wrongdoing, virtue is attained. [N-217](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217)

<a id="u05005"></a>

Through viewing, [one enters] samsara in the three realms;

<a id="u05006"></a>

through cultivating, one gathers all the hosts of afflictions;

<a id="u05007"></a>

through enacting activity, [one enters] deviation and obscuration;

<a id="u05008"></a>

the result is the very cause of samsara. [N-217](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217)

<a id="u05009"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the connection of symbolic meaning and phrases.

<a id="u05010"></a>

In the midst of an expanse of the conflagration of an age,

<a id="u05011"></a>

a person, without being burned,

<a id="u05012"></a>

expounds the Dharma of the scriptural collections from his mouth,

<a id="u05013"></a>

while in action cutting another's life—

<a id="u05014"></a>

and his virtuous practice increases. How strange! [N-217](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217)

<a id="u05015"></a>

Inside a spacious iron house without a door,

<a id="u05016"></a>

in the midst of an expanse of lightless darkness,

<a id="u05017"></a>

although sun, moon, and clear light are there,

<a id="u05018"></a>

no one sees them.

<a id="u05019"></a>

The objects of each one's faculties are clear,

<a id="u05020"></a>

yet there is no seeing. How strange! [N-217](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-217)

<a id="u05021"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the view that apprehends objects.

<a id="u05022"></a>

Emptying the Ground of samsara in the three realms,

<a id="u05023"></a>

one sweeps away passing beyond sorrow.

<a id="u05024"></a>

Cutting the root of ignorance and samsara,

<a id="u05025"></a>

one uproots the result of the three embodiments.

<a id="u05026"></a>

Putting on the armor of darkness, one extinguishes the sun;

<a id="u05027"></a>

the clear means of seeing is covered by darkness.

<a id="u05028"></a>

Giving an edge its sharp temper, not even one [thing] is cut.

<a id="u05029"></a>

With great breadth and an empty center, not even one [thing] fits.

<a id="u05030"></a>

Gathering diversity, one is not found.

<a id="u05031"></a>

Conceptual thought at the extremes of existence and nonexistence is cleared. [N-218](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218)

<a id="u05032"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the view of desiring ordinary mind.

<a id="u05033"></a>

Striking all, not even one is cut;

<a id="u05034"></a>

striking one, all are to be killed.

<a id="u05035"></a>

A hundred people in iron fetters—not one is freed;

<a id="u05036"></a>

holding one, all are freed.

<a id="u05037"></a>

Through freedom, the path of liberation above is exhausted.

<a id="u05038"></a>

The cause of entering activity ceases. [N-218](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-218)

<a id="u05039"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the wheel of activity.

<a id="u05040"></a>

Since there is no arising, there is no self;

<a id="u05041"></a>

neither taking nor casting away is the seal of embodiment.

<a id="u05042"></a>

Do not search for elements; do not reject what exists.

<a id="u05043"></a>

Arouse desire, but do not become attached.

<a id="u05044"></a>

Having become attached, let go.

<a id="u05045"></a>

Do not disparage another's body or set [your own] above it.

<a id="u05046"></a>

Do not interrupt the flow of mantra and gestures.

<a id="u05047"></a>

Do not reject tenets; do not reject Dharma.

<a id="u05048"></a>

Do not disparage companions or the lama. [N-219](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219)

<a id="u05049"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain pledges and vows.

<a id="u05050"></a>

Lamas and buddhas are supports for offerings;

<a id="u05051"></a>

to Dharma, the community, chosen deities,

<a id="u05052"></a>

and vajra ḍākinīs,

<a id="u05053"></a>

do not cut the flow of gathering offerings and other offerings.

<a id="u05054"></a>

On seeing a lama endowed with the characteristics,

<a id="u05055"></a>

make praise and offerings complete.

<a id="u05056"></a>

Do not disparage women; do not become angry.

<a id="u05057"></a>

Do not disparage the aggregates; rely on the chosen deity.

<a id="u05058"></a>

Relying on mandalas, mantras, deities,

<a id="u05059"></a>

and gestures is authentic.

<a id="u05060"></a>

Do not break up gatherings of the faithful;

<a id="u05061"></a>

do not disturb the ordinary minds of those possessing virtue.

<a id="u05062"></a>

Possessing vows, abide in the pledges. [N-219](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-219)

<a id="u05063"></a>

Furthermore, the stages of training:

<a id="u05064"></a>

complete the body's activity,

<a id="u05065"></a>

and thoroughly cut speech.

<a id="u05066"></a>

Let ordinary mind abide, and examine and investigate it.

<a id="u05067"></a>

In the stages of progressively entering activities,

<a id="u05068"></a>

know the measures and characteristics.

<a id="u05069"></a>

Constantly, through ointments and pills,

<a id="u05070"></a>

introduce the channels into a net of spheres. [N-220](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220)

<a id="u05071"></a>

Garlands, necklaces, and half-[necklaces] too,

<a id="u05072"></a>

beautiful and encircling, are to be connected.

<a id="u05073"></a>

The doing of activity is clear and without things.

<a id="u05074"></a>

Take the foremost of supreme accomplishments;

<a id="u05075"></a>

make the wheel of adornments at the six places. [N-220](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220)

<a id="u05076"></a>

Furthermore, reliance and entry:

<a id="u05077"></a>

relying on the lama ceases the flow of samsara;

<a id="u05078"></a>

relying on empowerment ripens body and voice;

<a id="u05079"></a>

relying on pledges brings accomplishments;[^G-U05079]

<a id="a2000-c05-s01"></a>

*Scan-restored main verses:*[^G-A2000-C05-S01]

through reliance on the view, the three realms are cut off;<br />
through reliance on cultivation, the flow of delusion stops;

<a id="u05080"></a>

relying on activity trains the body.

<a id="u05081"></a>

Whatever arises from these objects of reliance

<a id="u05082"></a>

is itself pledges and vows.

<a id="u05083"></a>

Activities of entry have eight aspects;

<a id="u05084"></a>

bodily entry is fivefold;

<a id="u05085"></a>

voice has eight aspects;

<a id="u05086"></a>

ordinary mind's entry is thirty-twofold.

<a id="u05087"></a>

The special functioning of body and ordinary mind

<a id="u05088"></a>

is the distinction between karma and primordial knowing. [N-220](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220)

<a id="u05089"></a>

Furthermore, the abiding-wheel of cultivation:

<a id="u05090"></a>

through training the body with diverse bodily exercises,

<a id="u05091"></a>

ordinary mind comes to abide.

<a id="u05092"></a>

Through three applications, soothe the channels;

<a id="u05093"></a>

draw ordinary mind in gently.

<a id="u05094"></a>

Through applications of signs and skillful craft—[^G-U05094]

<a id="u05095"></a>

through the distinct methods for body and ordinary mind,

<a id="u05096"></a>

make the yogin's ordinary mind abide. [N-220](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-220)

<a id="u05097"></a>

Furthermore, cut the flow of deluded clinging.

<a id="u05098"></a>

Gather the light of sun and moon; draw in the ocean.

<a id="u05099"></a>

Weigh the Mount Merus of a three-thousandfold world on a balance.

<a id="u05100"></a>

Reverse the Ground of ignorance: self-awareness. [N-221](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221)

<a id="u05101"></a>

Draw samsara out entirely; block the path of passing beyond sorrow.

<a id="u05102"></a>

Sweep away phenomena and persons.

<a id="u05103"></a>

The result is self-complete, the Ground of the nature of phenomena;

<a id="u05104"></a>

doing and acting self-cease.

<a id="u05105"></a>

Draw out from the root craving that desires another.

<a id="u05106"></a>

Delusion concerning phenomena makes an upper basis (yas gzhi). [N-221](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221)[^G-U05106]

<a id="u05107"></a>

That itself is the object liberated in its own place.

<a id="u05108"></a>

The three realms made the path, there is no buddha.

<a id="u05109"></a>

For whom could the teacher's teaching exist? It does not.

<a id="u05110"></a>

All phenomena are without a Ground; for whom could a root exist?

<a id="u05111"></a>

Ordinary mind free from existence and without desire,

<a id="u05112"></a>

uncontrived in intrinsic nature, is beyond expression.

<a id="u05113"></a>

Deluded conceptual thought of doing and acting is exhausted.

<a id="u05114"></a>

There are no phenomena, no buddhas, and no sentient beings.

<a id="u05115"></a>

There is no I, no self, no apprehended object and no apprehending subject. [N-221](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-221)

<a id="u05116"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the one taste of the dharma embodiment.

<a id="u05117"></a>

It is not expressed in phrases;

<a id="u05118"></a>

there is not even mere abiding called ordinary mind.

<a id="u05119"></a>

Because it is absent, in the appearance of emptiness

<a id="u05120"></a>

the source of mindfulness and knowing is exhausted.

<a id="u05121"></a>

In the nature of phenomena in which nothing is done,

<a id="u05122"></a>

there is no arising. Such great wonder!

<a id="u05123"></a>

In the dharma embodiment without arising,

<a id="u05124"></a>

there is no going. Such great wonder!

<a id="u05125"></a>

In awareness with nowhere to circle,

<a id="u05126"></a>

there is the nature of phenomena without arising.

<a id="u05127"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain gathering and evenness.

<a id="u05128"></a>

Rejecting multiplicity, one is free from convention.

<a id="u05129"></a>

One is not established; intrinsic nature is pure.

<a id="u05130"></a>

Intrinsic nature being clear, meaning is accomplished.

<a id="u05131"></a>

Self-purity and the foundation,

<a id="u05132"></a>

and the foundation mixed with samsara—[^G-U05132]

<a id="u05133"></a>

hollow and without a core:

<a id="u05134"></a>

called ‘ya ma rla,’ beyond indication. [N-222](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-222)

<a id="u05135"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the delusion of intrinsic nature.

<a id="u05136"></a>

Time and faculties,

<a id="u05137"></a>

distinctions, entry, and respective activities;

<a id="u05138"></a>

aggregates, elements, and sense bases—

<a id="u05139"></a>

in an instant when causes and conditions coincide,

<a id="u05140"></a>

like jewels and masses of cloud,

<a id="u05141"></a>

they circle like a body and its shadow.

<a id="u05142"></a>

Through different dependencies upon the basis of delusion,

<a id="u05143"></a>

the activities of sentient beings are diverse. [N-222](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-222)

<a id="u05144"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain pure appearance.

<a id="u05145"></a>

The vision of the direct perception of the nature of phenomena

<a id="u05146"></a>

definitely emerges through the faculties' gates

<a id="u05147"></a>

and is clear in cloudless space.

<a id="u05148"></a>

The vision of increasing experience:

<a id="u05149"></a>

the colors of primordial knowing come forth,

<a id="u05150"></a>

standing upright and arising as spokes,

<a id="u05151"></a>

as diverse spheres and embodiments,

<a id="u05152"></a>

clear in the domain of coarse appearance.

<a id="u05153"></a>

The vision of awareness reaching its limit:

<a id="u05154"></a>

the complete enjoyment embodiment's marks and signs are clear;

<a id="u05155"></a>

from rainbows of indeterminate colors,

<a id="u05156"></a>

the five families appear as fathers and mothers.

<a id="u05157"></a>

The vision of the exhaustion of nature of phenomena:

<a id="u05158"></a>

appearances of experience are emptied out,

<a id="u05159"></a>

the body is exhausted, and the objects of the faculties too are exhausted;

<a id="u05160"></a>

the assemblage of realizations and delusion is self-liberated, [N-223](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-223)

<a id="u05161"></a>

and there is freedom from expressed phrases.

<a id="u05162"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the sounds and signs of the elements.

<a id="u05163"></a>

When fire's sound crackles,

<a id="u05164"></a>

and sparks wave and proliferate,

<a id="u05165"></a>

the yogin does not accomplish the aim.

<a id="u05166"></a>

When it rises openly, blazing without interruption,

<a id="u05167"></a>

slow and long, with a sharp tip,

<a id="u05168"></a>

the yogin accomplishes a great aim. [N-224](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224)

<a id="u05169"></a>

I shall also explain the colors.

<a id="u05170"></a>

When it is red with a black tip,

<a id="u05171"></a>

and pieces break off toward oneself,

<a id="u05172"></a>

obstacles occur.

<a id="u05173"></a>

White and clear, like a lotus;

<a id="u05174"></a>

sharp and of diverse colors;

<a id="u05175"></a>

wheels, parasols, weapon-points;

<a id="u05176"></a>

red, clear, limpid rays proliferating;

<a id="u05177"></a>

vajras, victory banners, bells,

<a id="u05178"></a>

checkerboard patterns, tents and houses,

<a id="u05179"></a>

tiered stupas, and so forth:

<a id="u05180"></a>

for the yogin, there is great completeness. [N-224](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224)

<a id="u05181"></a>

Having directly perceived the meaning,

<a id="u05182"></a>

one passes beyond the three realms.

<a id="u05183"></a>

Furthermore, the subsidiary aspects of the elements:

<a id="u05184"></a>

water resounds with diverse sounds

<a id="u05185"></a>

and shows many white designs;

<a id="u05186"></a>

the yogin excellently accomplishes the aim.

<a id="u05187"></a>

Earth, through bouncing and ringing,

<a id="u05188"></a>

leaping at the edges and rolling, brings accomplishment.

<a id="u05189"></a>

Wind, through whistling and pressing,

<a id="u05190"></a>

and through being blue-green and billowing, does likewise. [N-224](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224)

<a id="u05191"></a>

If one has not met with such signs,

<a id="u05192"></a>

the adept repeats the rite and undertakes it again.

<a id="u05193"></a>

Through three, five, or seven times,

<a id="u05194"></a>

it will be accomplished; there is no doubt about this.

<a id="u05195"></a>

Accord definitely with the respective signs. [N-224](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-224)

<a id="u05196"></a>

Thus, from the Great All-Penetrating Word, Root of All Phenomena:

<a id="u05197"></a>

the fifth chapter, the Array of View and Cultivation, teaching the root of abiding.[^G-U05197]

<a id="c5-transition"></a>

*[Unresolved source graphic at the Chapter 5/6 boundary; no transcription supplied.]*[^G-C5-TRANSITION]

<a id="part-6"></a>
## Chapter 6

<a id="u05198"></a>

Then, although all phenomena are inexpressible,

<a id="u05199"></a>

from the many deluded conceptual thoughts of sentient beings,

<a id="u05200"></a>

also as a foundation for words, phrases, and conventions,

<a id="u05201"></a>

from uncontrived space,

<a id="u05202"></a>

phrases arise: the teacher abides in distinction. [N-225](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-225)

<a id="u05203"></a>

To the buddha arisen from the lotus,

<a id="u05204"></a>

Maker of Joy supplicated thus:

<a id="u05205"></a>

‘Kye kye! You who abide in the lotus's center,

<a id="u05206"></a>

here in the mandala of space,

<a id="u05207"></a>

sixty-four groups of a hundred thousand

<a id="u05208"></a>

drops of light appear.

<a id="u05209"></a>

What is the cause of the seventeen utmost refinements?

<a id="u05210"></a>

What is the dharma embodiment like?

<a id="u05211"></a>

To what is complete enjoyment embodiment applied?

<a id="u05212"></a>

How many emanation embodiments are there?

<a id="u05213"></a>

What are the characteristics of embodiment?

<a id="u05214"></a>

How does it abide?

<a id="u05215"></a>

What are the embodiments of intrinsic nature and the nature of phenomena?

<a id="u05216"></a>

Blessed one, speak to me.’

<a id="u05217"></a>

Then the blessed Vajradhara

<a id="u05218"></a>

rose from the lotus's center

<a id="u05219"></a>

and made the gesture of the sky-soarer.

<a id="u05220"></a>

Merely by doing this,

<a id="u05221"></a>

from the space of the nature of phenomena's self-appearance,

<a id="u05222"></a>

from inexpressible intrinsic nature,

<a id="u05223"></a>

as expressed phrases, these words

<a id="u05224"></a>

arose naturally, without being made. [N-225](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-225)

<a id="u05225"></a>

**[Source heading: First reply.]**

<a id="u05226"></a>

‘Kye kye! Lord of Gods, listen.

<a id="u05227"></a>

I shall truly explain the sixty-four

<a id="u05228"></a>

groups of a hundred thousand white lights.

<a id="u05229"></a>

From the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature,

<a id="u05230"></a>

distinctions of phrases proliferate;

<a id="u05231"></a>

from three divisions, there are nine expanses.

<a id="u05232"></a>

When phrases are gathered, there are ślokas:

<a id="u05233"></a>

sixty-four groups of a hundred thousand,

<a id="u05234"></a>

with thirty-five thousand chapters

<a id="u05235"></a>

and twenty-one thousand textual sections.

<a id="u05236"></a>

What is expressed equals the bounds of space

<a id="u05237"></a>

and cuts the conceptual thoughts of beings to be trained. [N-226](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-226)

<a id="u05238"></a>

Here, when the subject matter is closely condensed,

<a id="u05239"></a>

there are a hundred and eighty fixings,

<a id="u05240"></a>

a thousand and a half crucial junctures,

<a id="u05241"></a>

three thousand gatherings of great crucial points,

<a id="u05242"></a>

four hundred thousand deviations and obscurations,

<a id="u05243"></a>

and twenty thousand named continua.

<a id="u05244"></a>

The sequences of expressed phrases are clearly arrayed. [N-226](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-226)

<a id="u05245"></a>

**[Source heading: Second reply.]**

<a id="u05246"></a>

I shall explain the seventeen utmost refinements of light:

<a id="u05247"></a>

Self-Arising and Self-Liberation,

<a id="u05248"></a>

Naturally Arisen Itself and Complete at the Root,[^G-U05248]

<a id="u05249"></a>

Endowed with Beauty and Jeweled Array,

<a id="u05250"></a>

Garland of Pearls and Without Letters— [N-227](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-227)

<a id="u05251"></a>

Mirror of the Core and Mirror of Awakened Mind,

<a id="u05252"></a>

Union of Sun and Moon, Adorned with Pointing Out,

<a id="u05253"></a>

Blazing Embodiment-Relics, Heap of Jewels,

<a id="u05254"></a>

Blazing Lamp, and Six Great Expanses.

<a id="u05255"></a>

Thus these sixteen sequences

<a id="u05256"></a>

definitely proliferate from Thal Gyur

<a id="u05257"></a>

and appear to sentient beings to be trained.

<a id="u05258"></a>

By holding the teaching in which vital content is gathered,

<a id="u05259"></a>

one liberates from samsara for as long as ages exist,

<a id="u05260"></a>

and complete buddhahood manifests. [N-228](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-228)

<a id="u05261"></a>

The dharma embodiment: speech and awakened mind,

<a id="u05262"></a>

qualities, and enlightened activity.

<a id="u05263"></a>

Embodiment is emptiness and clarity,

<a id="u05264"></a>

an intrinsic nature without signs.

<a id="u05265"></a>

Speech is free from words, phrases, and names,

<a id="u05266"></a>

free from expression and discussion.

<a id="u05267"></a>

Awakened mind, without thinking or movement,

<a id="u05268"></a>

is beyond proliferation and activity.

<a id="u05269"></a>

Its quality is neither coming back nor returning;

<a id="u05270"></a>

basic space and primordial knowing pervade,

<a id="u05271"></a>

and intrinsic nature is uncontrived.

<a id="u05272"></a>

Enlightened activity has not come about, arisen, or been done;

<a id="u05273"></a>

it comes about of its own force and does not cease.

<a id="u05274"></a>

Not done, there is no doing. [N-229](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229)

<a id="u05275"></a>

**[Source heading: Fourth reply.]**

<a id="u05276"></a>

The complete enjoyment embodiment too has embodiment, speech, awakened mind,

<a id="u05277"></a>

qualities, and enlightened activity—five.

<a id="u05278"></a>

Embodiment appears but has no intrinsic nature.

<a id="u05279"></a>

Clarity and that which makes clear are pervaded by awareness.

<a id="u05280"></a>

Speech is naturally arisen and self-appearance,

<a id="u05281"></a>

a wheel of the aspects of proliferating and gathering.

<a id="u05282"></a>

Awakened mind is seeing without a flow,

<a id="u05283"></a>

the expressiveness of knowing, and enlightened intent. [N-229](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229)

<a id="u05284"></a>

Qualities: the marks and signs are complete;

<a id="u05285"></a>

clear enlightened intent and higher knowing are complete.

<a id="u05286"></a>

Enlightened activity: from the wheel of Dharma,

<a id="u05287"></a>

outer, inner, and secret [teachings] unfold,

<a id="u05288"></a>

and for the buddhas of the respective families

<a id="u05289"></a>

and for bodhisattvas too,

<a id="u05290"></a>

they unfold from the naturally arisen tongue,

<a id="u05291"></a>

showing the retinue through their own essence. [N-229](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-229)

<a id="u05292"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifth reply.]**

<a id="u05293"></a>

The emanation embodiment too has embodiment, speech, awakened mind,

<a id="u05294"></a>

qualities, and enlightened activity—five in each case.

<a id="u05295"></a>

Embodiment, with marks and signs,

<a id="u05296"></a>

emanates bodies for whatever beings to be trained appear.

<a id="u05297"></a>

Speech has excellent phrase-composition:

<a id="u05298"></a>

the six aspects of Brahmā's voice.

<a id="u05299"></a>

Awakened mind, through primordial knowing of objects of knowledge,

<a id="u05300"></a>

abides in enlightened intent for the two benefits, one's own and others'.

<a id="u05301"></a>

Qualities are completeness of knowing.

<a id="u05302"></a>

Enlightened activity completes without remainder

<a id="u05303"></a>

the outer, inner, and secret deeds,

<a id="u05304"></a>

abiding in five perfections. [N-230](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230)

<a id="u05305"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixth reply.]**

<a id="u05306"></a>

Embodiment's characteristics, essence,

<a id="u05307"></a>

definitive wording, and divisions;

<a id="u05308"></a>

the respective fields and beings to be trained;

<a id="u05309"></a>

the activities of differentiation and the measure of arising—[^G-U05309]

<a id="u05310"></a>

through distinctions in characteristics and understanding:

<a id="u05311"></a>

characteristics are established from phenomena,

<a id="u05312"></a>

established from desirable qualities,

<a id="u05313"></a>

and established from diverse methods.

<a id="u05314"></a>

Understanding: intrinsic nature is pure;

<a id="u05315"></a>

the respective domains of appearance are shown;

<a id="u05316"></a>

and, endowed with form, the faculties are complete. [N-230](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230)

<a id="u05317"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventh reply.]**

<a id="u05318"></a>

Embodiment's manner of abiding has three aspects:

<a id="u05319"></a>

because it is liberated from phenomena of signs,

<a id="u05320"></a>

it is not a form marked by divisions;

<a id="u05321"></a>

because the intrinsic nature of clarity is complete,

<a id="u05322"></a>

it appears but has no intrinsic nature;

<a id="u05323"></a>

to train the continua of those to be trained,

<a id="u05324"></a>

it abides unmoving in great equipoise.

<a id="u05325"></a>

Unmoving and clear,

<a id="u05326"></a>

it appears as the aspect of awareness and knowing:

<a id="u05327"></a>

even, without partiality, and undivided. [N-230](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-230)

<a id="u05328"></a>

**[Source heading: Eighth reply.]**

<a id="u05329"></a>

The intrinsic nature and nature of phenomena of embodiment:

<a id="u05330"></a>

intrinsic nature is emptiness and clarity,

<a id="u05331"></a>

the unceasing manifestation of knowing and awareness.

<a id="u05332"></a>

The nature of phenomena of embodiment is complete;

<a id="u05333"></a>

there is no return, going, or coming,

<a id="u05334"></a>

and no speaking, thinking, or expression.

<a id="u05335"></a>

Furthermore, accomplishing embodiments:

<a id="u05336"></a>

through the distinctions taught above,

<a id="u05337"></a>

in one's own ordinary mind, the seed of the dharma embodiment,

<a id="u05338"></a>

apply the crucial point of direct perception,

<a id="u05339"></a>

and become constantly familiar with it.

<a id="u05340"></a>

This is accomplishing the dharma embodiment.

<a id="u05341"></a>

Train in desirable qualities, the seed of the complete enjoyment embodiment,

<a id="u05342"></a>

and become familiar with them:

<a id="u05343"></a>

this is accomplishing complete enjoyment.

<a id="u05344"></a>

As for the seed of the emanation embodiment, the four elements:

<a id="u05345"></a>

train in their word and meaning;

<a id="u05346"></a>

through familiarity, the emanation embodiment is accomplished. [N-231](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-231)

<a id="u05347"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the scope of fire's activities.

<a id="u05348"></a>

Distinguished through colors:

<a id="u05349"></a>

white, clear, and gentle accomplishes pacifying;

<a id="u05350"></a>

yellow, subtle, and long accomplishes increasing;

<a id="u05351"></a>

red, limpid, and proliferating is the activity of power;

<a id="u05352"></a>

blue-green, black, and short accomplishes the fierce.

<a id="u05353"></a>

When it is variegated, broken, and whirling,

<a id="u05354"></a>

distinctions of various activities are accomplished.

<a id="u05355"></a>

When it is extremely subtle, there is falling.

<a id="u05356"></a>

When broken and fierce, obstacles arise.

<a id="u05357"></a>

When spreading tongues of fire have smoke,

<a id="u05358"></a>

pledges deteriorate. [N-232](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232)

<a id="u05359"></a>

Distinctions through sound:

<a id="u05360"></a>

three successive lhab lhab sounds and many spar spar sounds

<a id="u05361"></a>

accomplish diverse [activities].

<a id="u05362"></a>

Fierceness and crackling [accomplish] the fierce;

<a id="u05363"></a>

roaring and dense [sound accomplish] pacifying and increasing, the two;

<a id="u05364"></a>

resounding and ringing accomplish the supreme. [N-232](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232)

<a id="u05365"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the scope of water's activities.

<a id="u05366"></a>

The definitive signs of colors:

<a id="u05367"></a>

through the four beginning with white,

<a id="u05368"></a>

pacifying and the rest are definitely accomplished.

<a id="u05369"></a>

When brown-black, rough, and whirling,

<a id="u05370"></a>

the yogin does not accomplish the aim.

<a id="u05371"></a>

When white and clear, rising and billowing,

<a id="u05372"></a>

the supreme accomplishment is definitely attained.

<a id="u05373"></a>

Through sounds that resound and ring,

<a id="u05374"></a>

whoosh, roar, and are dense,

<a id="u05375"></a>

food, clothing, and the result are accomplished.

<a id="u05376"></a>

Through sir, sil, and lhab sounds,

<a id="u05377"></a>

the bliss of each of the three embodiments is attained. [N-232](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232)

<a id="u05378"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the scope of earth's activities.

<a id="u05379"></a>

Know white, red, and the other colors from the preceding account.

<a id="u05380"></a>

Indeterminate change does not accomplish the aim.

<a id="u05381"></a>

Clarity and shining accomplish the supreme.

<a id="u05382"></a>

Its sounds are shag and sdig— [N-232](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232)

<a id="a2000-c06-s01"></a>

*Scan-restored main verses:*[^G-A2000-C06-S01]

through tog and sing, various activities [are accomplished];<br />
through roaring and sing, the supreme is attained.

<a id="u05383"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain the scope of wind's activities.

<a id="u05384"></a>

White, red, and the other colors are as above.

<a id="u05385"></a>

Blackness and turbulence do not accomplish the aim.

<a id="u05386"></a>

Variegated blazing accomplishes the supreme.

<a id="u05387"></a>

Its sounds are roaring, muffled, and diverse;

<a id="u05388"></a>

ringing and diverse [sounds] bring supreme attainment. [N-232](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-232)

<a id="u05389"></a>

Furthermore, I shall explain signs and intrinsic nature.

<a id="u05390"></a>

After the preceding signs arise,

<a id="u05391"></a>

after three months and six months,

<a id="u05392"></a>

through the two, one knows the sounds of the insentient and the aware.

<a id="u05393"></a>

After ten months and eighteen months,

<a id="u05394"></a>

each respective sound and the voices of the six classes

<a id="u05395"></a>

are known just as they are uttered. [N-233](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-233)

<a id="u05396"></a>

Furthermore, the stages of the result:

<a id="u05397"></a>

the dharma embodiment is through the activity of ordinary mind;

<a id="u05398"></a>

through complete enjoyment embodiment, [it] is attained;[^G-U05398]

<a id="u05399"></a>

the emanation embodiment is through the body.

<a id="u05400"></a>

Essence itself is through equal portions. [N-233](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-233)

<a id="u05401"></a>

The body: the elements are pure.

<a id="u05402"></a>

Voice is joined to āli and kāli.

<a id="u05403"></a>

Ordinary mind, without things, is unimpeded penetration.

<a id="u05404"></a>

The gathering of the three is great bliss.

<a id="u05405"></a>

Furthermore, this is to be fully explained:

<a id="u05406"></a>

through thoroughly knowing embodiment's characteristics,

<a id="u05407"></a>

all delusion ceases at one time.

<a id="u05408"></a>

Through fully knowing embodiment's intrinsic nature,

<a id="u05409"></a>

the boundary between clarity and emptiness is held.

<a id="u05410"></a>

Through knowing essence, there is purification into emptiness.

<a id="u05411"></a>

Through knowing ‘dang’ [reading unresolved], non-conceptuality arises. [N-234](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-234)[^G-U05411]

<a id="u05412"></a>

Knowing the great identity,

<a id="u05413"></a>

one cuts the root of samsara in the three realms.

<a id="u05414"></a>

Through these, confidence in self-liberation

<a id="u05415"></a>

is certainly attained without anything being done.

<a id="u05416"></a>

Ema! Such wondrous marvel!

<a id="u05417"></a>

In space in which nothing is done,

<a id="u05418"></a>

uncontrived phrases arise;

<a id="u05419"></a>

unthought, naturally arisen, they are understood by the conceptual mind.

<a id="u05420"></a>

Having understood thus, through knowing and realization,

<a id="u05421"></a>

[one trusts] that, as the root of all the scriptural collections,

<a id="u05422"></a>

this king of continua, Thal Gyur itself,

<a id="u05423"></a>

this most secret, unsurpassed teaching,

<a id="u05424"></a>

is indeed this. [N-235](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-235)

<a id="u05425"></a>

All the retinues appearing through intrinsic nature,

<a id="u05426"></a>

having aroused an utterly delighted ordinary mind,

<a id="u05427"></a>

read and preserve this continuum,

<a id="u05428"></a>

write it and hold it;

<a id="u05429"></a>

holding and preserving it at the throat and crown,

<a id="u05430"></a>

they engage in honoring all buddhas.

<a id="u05431"></a>

Having accepted and pledged this,

<a id="u05432"></a>

they praise the teacher, the blessed buddha—

<a id="u05433"></a>

his embodiment complete in qualities. [N-235](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-235)

<a id="u05434"></a>

Then all those fully assembled,

<a id="u05435"></a>

with ordinary minds of joy, delight, and gladness arisen,

<a id="u05436"></a>

[behold] space, self-purified and clear,

<a id="u05437"></a>

and inexpressible phrases self-arising.

<a id="u05438"></a>

Rejoicing and finding bliss,

<a id="u05439"></a>

they become even with freedom from conceptual elaborations

<a id="u05440"></a>

in the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature. [N-235](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-235)

<a id="u05441"></a>

Of all phenomena as the single unique sphere,

<a id="u05442"></a>

having attained the enlightened intent of equal taste,

<a id="u05443"></a>

[to] the lotus-arisen blessed one,

<a id="u05444"></a>

they say, ‘Excellent! Excellent! Excellent!

<a id="u05445"></a>

You have ripened us as the three embodiments;

<a id="u05446"></a>

we supplicate and rejoice.’ [N-235](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-235)

<a id="u05447"></a>

This is called the Array of Embodiments,

<a id="u05448"></a>

the sixth chapter.[^G-U05448]

<a id="part-7"></a>
## Full-work colophon and closing material

<a id="u05449"></a>

Causing Precious Things to Arise:

<a id="u05450"></a>

the continuum called the Great All-Penetrating Word, Taught as the Root of All Phenomena,

<a id="u05451"></a>

specially arrayed from the sixty-four hundred-thousand ślokas of the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature, is complete. [N-236](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-236)

<a id="u05452"></a>

Seal! Seal! Seal!

<a id="u05453"></a>

Seal! Seal! Seal!

<a id="u05454"></a>

Seal! Seal! Seal!

<a id="u05455"></a>

This king of continua

<a id="u05456"></a>

is not available to just anyone, except exceptional beings.

<a id="u05457"></a>

Glorious queen guarding mantra, dark-brown Wrathful Queen, Copper-Faced One: protect it! [N-237](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-237)

<a id="u05458"></a>

Samaya.

<a id="u05459"></a>

So phag.[^G-U05459]

<a id="u05460"></a>

Ag tham. [N-237](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-237)[^G-U05460]

<a id="u05461"></a>

Glorious Black Protector, I entrust it to you.

<a id="u05462"></a>

If it is given to an unfit vessel, impose the penalty of the pledge. [N-237](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-237)

<a id="u05463"></a>

Samaya.

<a id="u05464"></a>

Seal! Seal! Seal!

<a id="u05465"></a>

The Thal Gyur continuum has seven mother-and-child [texts]. [N-238](LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-238)

<a id="u05466"></a>

Virtue! Virtue! Virtue!
